(function (EB) {
  'use strict';
  const U = (EB.U = {});

  U.makeRng = function (seed) {
    let s = (seed >>> 0) || 0x9e3779b9;
    const f = function () {
      s ^= s << 13; s >>>= 0;
      s ^= s >>> 17;
      s ^= s << 5; s >>>= 0;
      return s / 4294967296;
    };
    f.int = (n) => Math.floor(f() * n);
    f.pick = (arr) => (arr.length ? arr[Math.floor(f() * arr.length)] : undefined);
    f.chance = (p) => f() < p;
    f.shuffle = (arr) => { const a = arr.slice(); for (let i = a.length - 1; i > 0; i--) { const j = Math.floor(f() * (i + 1)); [a[i], a[j]] = [a[j], a[i]]; } return a; };
    f.state = () => s;
    return f;
  };

  U.snake = (k) => k.replace(/[A-Z]/g, (m) => '_' + m.toLowerCase());
  U.clamp = (v, lo, hi) => Math.max(lo, Math.min(hi, v));
  U.round = (v) => Math.round(v);
  U.uid = (() => { let n = 0; return (p) => (p || 'u') + (++n); })();
  U.deepCopy = (o) => JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(o));
  U.title = (s) => String(s || '').replace(/_/g, ' ').replace(/\b\w/g, (m) => m.toUpperCase());
  U.sum = (arr) => arr.reduce((a, b) => a + b, 0);

  // Map loose damage-type names onto the canonical set used for resistances.
  U.DMG_ALIAS = { radiant: 'holy', light: 'holy', decay: 'necrotic', nature: 'poison', spirit: 'psychic', magic: 'arcane', sound: 'sound', void: 'void' };
  U.canonType = (t) => U.DMG_ALIAS[t] || t || 'physical';
})(globalThis.EB = globalThis.EB || {});

;
(function (EB) {
  'use strict';
  const U = EB.U;
  const K = (EB.K = {}); // core API

  K.TEMPO = { quick: 60, normal: 100, standard: 100, slow: 150 };
  K.RANK_NAMES = ['Iron', 'Bronze', 'Silver', 'Gold'];
  K.RANK_POWER = [1, 1.3, 1.6, 2.0];
  K.DOT_SCALE = 2;
  K.ATTRS = ['might', 'swiftness', 'spirit', 'resilience'];
  K.PHYS = { physical: 1, blood: 1, force: 1 };

  // ---------- content access ----------
  K.C = () => EB.content;
  // Things applied to a unit during its own turn don't tick down at the end of that same turn.
  K.stamp = () => EB.turnStamp || '';
  K.fresh = (x, u) => x.stamp === u.id + ':' + EB.turnNo;
  K.statusDef = (id) => EB.content.statuses[id] || { id, name: U.title(id), kind: 'affliction', category: 'magic', maxStacks: 5 };
  K.isBoon = (id) => K.statusDef(id).kind === 'boon';
  K.tpl = (id) => EB.content.templates[id];

  // ---------- combatants ----------
  K.makeUnit = function (b, spec) {
    const u = {
      id: spec.id || U.uid('c'),
      name: spec.name || 'Unknown',
      side: spec.side || 'enemy',
      row: spec.row || 'vanguard',
      kind: spec.kind || 'enemy', // hero | familiar | conjuration | enemy | decoy
      owner: spec.owner || null,
      isPlayer: !!spec.isPlayer,
      tags: new Set(spec.tags || []),
      base: Object.assign({ might: 10, swiftness: 10, spirit: 10, resilience: 10 }, spec.attrs || {}),
      rank: spec.rank || 0,
      powerScale: spec.powerScale || 1,
      hpScale: spec.hpScale || 1,
      speedScale: spec.speedScale || 1,
      statuses: {},
      mods: [],
      hooks: [],
      shields: [],
      abilities: [],
      actions: spec.actions || [], // template actions (familiars / conjurations)
      next: [], // next-ability mods
      echoes: [],
      flags: {},
      alive: true,
      t: 0,
      turns: 0,
      icon: spec.icon || null,
      essences: spec.essences || [],
      stance: spec.stance || 'aggressive',
      ai: spec.ai || null,
      intent: null,
      history: [],
      lastDamage: [],
      expireAt: spec.duration ? { turns: spec.duration } : null,
      template: spec.template || null,
      boss: !!spec.boss,
      phases: spec.phases || null,
      level: spec.level || 1,
      lootTable: spec.loot || null,
    };
    if (spec.boss) u.tags.add('boss');
    if (spec.flying) u.flags.flying = true;
    u.baseHp = spec.maxHp || Math.round((40 + 5 * u.base.resilience) * u.hpScale);
    u.baseMana = spec.maxMana || Math.round(30 + 4 * u.base.spirit);
    u.baseSta = spec.maxSta || Math.round(30 + 4 * u.base.resilience);
    u.hp = u.baseHp; u.mana = u.baseMana; u.sta = u.baseSta;
    for (const a of spec.abilities || []) K.learn(u, a.id || a, a.rank != null ? a.rank : u.rank);
    return u;
  };

  K.learn = function (u, abId, rank) {
    const def = typeof abId === 'string' ? EB.content.abilities[abId] : abId;
    if (!def) return null;
    const slot = { def: K.effective(def, rank || 0), base: def, id: def.id, rank: rank || 0, cd: 0, uses: 0, on: false };
    u.abilities.push(slot);
    return slot;
  };

  // ---------- riders / rank effective ability ----------
  function mapEffects(effs, fn) {
    return (effs || []).map((e) => {
      if (!e || typeof e !== 'object') return e;
      const n = fn(Object.assign({}, e));
      for (const k of ['effects', 'then', 'else', 'perStack', 'onDeath', 'accept', 'refuse', 'pool', 'options', 'reward', 'bonus', 'breakPenalty', 'penalty']) {
        if (Array.isArray(n[k])) n[k] = mapEffects(n[k], fn);
      }
      return n;
    });
  }
  K.mapEffects = mapEffects;

  K.effective = function (def, rank) {
    const a = U.deepCopy(def);
    a.rank = rank;
    a.powerMult = K.RANK_POWER[rank] || 1;
    a.critBonus = 0;
    a.condMult = 1;
    a.extraTargets = 0;
    a.rowSpill = 0;
    a.summonStats = 1;
    a.familiarUnlock = 0;
    a.formUnlock = 0;
    a.costMult = 1;
    const tiers = ['bronze', 'silver', 'gold'].slice(0, rank);
    for (const tier of tiers) {
      const rid = def.riders && def.riders[tier];
      const r = rid && EB.content.riders[rid];
      if (!r) continue;
      const p = r.patch;
      if (p.scalePower) a.powerMult *= p.scalePower;
      if (p.cooldown) a.cooldown = Math.max(0, (a.cooldown || 0) + p.cooldown);
      if (p.costMult) a.costMult *= p.costMult;
      if (p.duration === 'combat') a.effects = mapEffects(a.effects, (e) => { if (e.duration != null && e.op !== 'transform') e.duration = 'combat'; return e; });
      else if (p.duration) a.effects = mapEffects(a.effects, (e) => { if (typeof e.duration === 'number') e.duration += p.duration; return e; });
      if (p.stacks) a.effects = mapEffects(a.effects, (e) => { if (e.op === 'apply_status' && typeof e.stacks === 'number') e.stacks += p.stacks; return e; });
      if (p.addSignature) {
        const ess = EB.content.essences[def.essence];
        if (ess && ess.signatureStatus) {
          const boon = K.isBoon(ess.signatureStatus);
          a.effects = a.effects.concat([{ op: 'apply_status', status: ess.signatureStatus, stacks: p.addSignature, to: boon ? 'self' : undefined }]);
        }
      }
      if (p.extraTargets) a.extraTargets += p.extraTargets;
      if (p.rowSpill) a.rowSpill = p.rowSpill;
      if (p.tempo) a.tempo = p.tempo;
      if (p.onKill) a.onKill = p.onKill;
      if (p.addEffect) a.effects = a.effects.concat([U.deepCopy(p.addEffect)]);
      if (p.summonCount) a.effects = mapEffects(a.effects, (e) => { if (e.op === 'summon') e.count = (e.count || 1) + p.summonCount; return e; });
      if (p.summonStats) a.summonStats *= p.summonStats;
      if (p.familiarUnlock) a.familiarUnlock += p.familiarUnlock;
      if (p.formUnlock) a.formUnlock += p.formUnlock;
      if (p.zoneRows) a.effects = mapEffects(a.effects, (e) => { if (e.op === 'zone') e.row = p.zoneRows; return e; });
      if (p.auraRows) a.auraRows = p.auraRows;
      if (p.crit) a.critBonus += p.crit;
      if (p.conditionalMult) a.condMult *= p.conditionalMult;
      if (p.limitPerTurn) a.effects = mapEffects(a.effects, (e) => { if (e.op === 'reaction') e.limitPerTurn = Math.max(e.limitPerTurn || 1, p.limitPerTurn); return e; });
      if (p.allyShare) a.allyShare = p.allyShare;
      if (p.healthCost === 0 && a.cost) delete a.cost.health;
    }
    return a;
  };

  // ---------- stats ----------
  const FORM_KEY = (k) => U.snake(k);
  function modSources(u) { return u.mods; }

  // Sum a stat across mods, statuses, form, item. vs: optional other unit (for targeted mods).
  K.stat = function (u, key, vs) {
    let s = 0;
    for (const m of modSources(u)) {
      if (m.stat !== key) continue;
      if (m.vs && (!vs || m.vs !== vs.id)) continue;
      s += m.amt;
    }
    const FX = EB.FX;
    for (const id in u.statuses) {
      const fx = FX[id];
      if (!fx || !fx.mods) continue;
      if ((u.flags.suppressBoons || (u.statuses.locked && u.statuses.locked.stacks >= 3)) && K.isBoon(id)) continue;
      const v = fx.mods[key];
      if (v) s += v * u.statuses[id].stacks * (1 + (u.statuses[id].potency || 0));
    }
    if (u.form && u.form.mods) { const v = u.form.mods[key]; if (typeof v === 'number') s += v; }
    if (u.item && u.item.mods) { const v = u.item.mods[key]; if (typeof v === 'number') s += v; }
    return s;
  };
  K.flag = function (u, key) {
    if (u.flags[key]) return true;
    if (u.form && u.form.mods && u.form.mods[key]) return true;
    if (u.item && u.item.mods && u.item.mods[key]) return true;
    return false;
  };

  K.attr = function (u, k) {
    let v = u.base[k] * (1 + K.stat(u, k) + K.stat(u, 'all_attributes'));
    return Math.max(1, v);
  };
  K.maxHp = function (u) {
    const m = 1 + K.stat(u, 'max_health');
    return Math.max(1, Math.round((u.baseHp + (u.base.resilience - 10) * 0 ) * Math.max(0.2, m)));
  };
  K.maxMana = (u) => u.baseMana;
  K.maxSta = (u) => u.baseSta;
  K.hpPct = (u) => u.hp / K.maxHp(u);

  K.DEFAULT_SCALE = (type) => (K.PHYS[type] ? { might: 1 } : { spirit: 1 });
  K.hit = function (u, scale, type) {
    const sc = scale && Object.keys(scale).length ? scale : K.DEFAULT_SCALE(type);
    let sum = 0, w = 0;
    for (const k in sc) {
      const kk = K.ATTRS.includes(k) ? k : 'spirit';
      sum += K.attr(u, kk) * sc[k]; w += sc[k];
    }
    const a = w ? sum / Math.max(1, w) : 10;
    return (3 + 1.2 * a) * u.powerScale;
  };

  // ---------- battle helpers ----------
  K.alive = (b) => b.units.filter((u) => u.alive);
  K.allies = (b, u, incSelf) => b.units.filter((x) => x.alive && x.side === u.side && (incSelf || x !== u) && !x.flags.banished);
  K.enemies = (b, u) => b.units.filter((x) => x.alive && x.side !== u.side && !x.flags.banished);
  K.isEnemy = (a, c) => a.side !== c.side;
  K.byId = (b, id) => b.units.find((x) => x.id === id);
  K.opp = (side) => (side === 'ally' ? 'enemy' : 'ally');
  K.rowOf = (b, side, row) => b.units.filter((x) => x.alive && x.side === side && x.row === row && !x.flags.banished);
  K.hasStatus = (u, id) => !!(u.statuses[id] && u.statuses[id].stacks > 0);
  K.stacks = (u, id) => (u.statuses[id] ? u.statuses[id].stacks : 0);

  K.log = function (b, ev) { ev.time = b.time; b.log.push(ev); if (b.onEvent) b.onEvent(ev); };
  K.say = function (b, msg, cls) { K.log(b, { k: 'text', msg, cls: cls || '' }); };

  // ---------- modifiers ----------
  K.addMod = function (b, u, stat, amt, dur, src, opts) {
    opts = opts || {};
    if (!stat || !amt) return;
    const m = { stamp: K.stamp(), stat, amt, dur: dur == null ? 2 : dur, src: src ? src.id : null, vs: opts.vs || null, aura: opts.aura || null, tag: opts.tag || null };
    u.mods.push(m);
    if (stat === 'max_health' && amt > 0) u.hp = Math.min(K.maxHp(u), u.hp + Math.round(u.baseHp * amt));
    return m;
  };
  K.removeAura = function (b, auraKey) {
    for (const u of b.units) {
      u.mods = u.mods.filter((m) => m.aura !== auraKey);
      u.hooks = u.hooks.filter((h) => h.aura !== auraKey);
    }
    b.zones = b.zones.filter((z) => z.aura !== auraKey);
  };

  // ---------- shields ----------
  K.addShield = function (b, u, amt, dur, src) {
    amt = Math.round(amt * (1 + K.stat(src || u, 'shield_strength')));
    if (amt <= 0) return 0;
    u.shields.push({ stamp: K.stamp(), amt, max: amt, dur: dur == null ? 2 : dur, src: src ? src.id : null });
    u.lastShield = amt;
    K.log(b, { k: 'shield', tgt: u.id, amt });
    return amt;
  };
  K.shieldTotal = (u) => U.sum(u.shields.map((s) => s.amt));

  // ---------- healing ----------
  K.heal = function (b, src, tgt, amt, opts) {
    opts = opts || {};
    if (!tgt.alive || amt <= 0) return 0;
    if (tgt.flags.noHealing || (tgt.flags.oath && tgt.flags.oath.restriction === 'no_healing_from_others' && src && src !== tgt)) {
      return 0;
    }
    if (K.hasStatus(tgt, 'salted') && tgt.flags.saltBound) return 0;
    let mult = 1 + K.stat(src || tgt, 'healing_done') + K.stat(tgt, 'healing_received');
    if (src && src.flags.healingMult) mult *= src.flags.healingMult;
    amt = Math.round(amt * Math.max(0, mult));
    if (tgt.flags.storeHealing) { tgt.flags.stored = (tgt.flags.stored || 0) + amt; return 0; }
    const before = tgt.hp;
    tgt.hp = Math.min(K.maxHp(tgt), tgt.hp + amt);
    const done = tgt.hp - before;
    const over = amt - done;
    if (over > 0 && opts.overhealToShield) K.addShield(b, tgt, over, 2, src);
    if (over > 0 && src && src.flags.overflowGain) K.applyStatus(b, src, src, 'overflowing', 1);
    if (done > 0 || amt > 0) K.log(b, { k: 'heal', src: src && src.id, tgt: tgt.id, amt: done });
    // bleeding loses 2 stacks to healing (unless blocked)
    if (K.hasStatus(tgt, 'bleeding') && !tgt.flags.blockBleedHealRemoval) K.reduceStatus(b, tgt, 'bleeding', 2);
    if (src) {
      K.fire(b, src, 'when_you_heal', { other: tgt, amount: done });
      for (const a of K.allies(b, tgt)) K.fire(b, a, 'when_ally_healed', { other: tgt, amount: done });
    }
    return done;
  };

  K.restore = function (b, u, res, amt) {
    if (res === 'health') return amt >= 0 ? K.heal(b, u, u, amt) : K.loseHp(b, u, -amt, u);
    const key = res === 'mana' ? 'mana' : 'sta';
    const max = res === 'mana' ? K.maxMana(u) : K.maxSta(u);
    const before = u[key];
    u[key] = U.clamp(u[key] + amt, 0, max);
    return u[key] - before;
  };

  // direct health loss (costs, self-damage) - bypasses shields
  K.loseHp = function (b, u, amt, src) {
    amt = Math.round(amt);
    if (amt <= 0) return 0;
    u.hp -= amt;
    K.log(b, { k: 'dmg', src: src && src.id, tgt: u.id, amt, type: 'blood', pay: true });
    if (u.hp <= 0) K.checkDeath(b, u, src);
    return amt;
  };

  // ---------- damage ----------
  // Computes the number before shields. src may be null (environment).
  const setTimeout0 = (fn) => fn();
  K.computeDamage = function (b, src, tgt, base, type, o) {
    o = o || {};
    const ct = U.canonType(type);
    let mult = 1;
    if (src) {
      mult += K.stat(src, 'damage') + K.stat(src, type + '_damage') + (ct !== type ? K.stat(src, ct + '_damage') : 0);
      if (K.PHYS[type]) mult += K.stat(src, 'physical_damage');
      if (o.spell) mult += K.stat(src, 'spell_power');
      if (o.weapon) mult += K.stat(src, 'weapon_power');
      if (o.dot) mult += K.stat(src, 'dot_damage');
      if (o.aoe) mult += K.stat(src, 'aoe_damage');
      if (o.ranged) mult += K.stat(src, 'ranged_damage');
      if (src.kind === 'familiar') mult += K.stat(src, 'familiar_damage') + (src.owner ? K.stat(K.byId(b, src.owner) || src, 'familiar_damage') : 0);
      if (src.kind === 'conjuration' || src.kind === 'familiar') mult += src.owner ? K.stat(K.byId(b, src.owner) || src, 'summon_damage') : 0;
      if (src.flags.stealthStrike) mult += K.stat(src, 'sneak_attack_damage');
      // damage_vs_* family
      for (const m of src.mods) {
        if (!m.stat.startsWith('damage_vs_')) continue;
        const what = m.stat.slice(10);
        if (K.vsMatches(b, src, tgt, what, m)) mult += m.amt;
      }
      if (src.form && src.form.mods) for (const k in src.form.mods) if (k.startsWith('damage_vs_') && K.vsMatches(b, src, tgt, k.slice(10), {})) mult += src.form.mods[k];
      if (src.item && src.item.mods) {
        const im = src.item.mods;
        if (im.vs_asleep && K.hasStatus(tgt, 'asleep')) mult += im.vs_asleep;
        if (im.vs_afraid && K.hasStatus(tgt, 'feared')) mult += im.vs_afraid;
        if (im.vs_corrupted && tgt.tags.has('corrupted')) mult += im.vs_corrupted;
      }
      // feared/terror bonus from the one who terrifies
      if (K.hasStatus(tgt, 'feared') && K.hasStatus(tgt, 'terror')) mult += 0.15;
    }
    // side-relative statuses on the target
    for (const id in tgt.statuses) {
      const fx = EB.FX[id]; const st = tgt.statuses[id];
      if (!fx) continue;
      if (fx.sideTaken && src && st.srcSide === src.side) mult += fx.sideTaken * st.stacks;
      if (fx.srcTaken && src && (st.src === src.id || st.src === src.owner)) mult += Math.min(fx.srcTakenMax || 9, fx.srcTaken * st.stacks);
      if (fx.typeTaken && fx.typeTaken[ct]) mult += fx.typeTaken[ct] * st.stacks;
      if (fx.typeTaken && fx.typeTaken[type] && type !== ct) mult += fx.typeTaken[type] * st.stacks;
      if (fx.corruptTaken && tgt.tags.has('corrupted') && (ct === 'holy')) mult += fx.corruptTaken * st.stacks;
    }
    if (src && K.hasStatus(src, 'soaked') && ct === 'fire') mult -= 0.06 * K.stacks(src, 'soaked');
    if (src && K.hasStatus(src, 'duped') && src.statuses.duped.src === tgt.id) mult -= 0.05 * K.stacks(src, 'duped');
    let taken = 1 + K.stat(tgt, 'damage_taken');
    if (o.ranged) taken += K.stat(tgt, 'damage_taken_ranged') - K.stat(tgt, 'ranged_resist');
    if (o.aoe && tgt.form && tgt.form.mods && tgt.form.mods.aoe_vulnerability) taken += tgt.form.mods.aoe_vulnerability;
    if (tgt.form && tgt.form.mods) {
      if (ct === 'fire' && tgt.form.mods.fire_vulnerability) taken += tgt.form.mods.fire_vulnerability;
      if (ct === 'holy' && tgt.form.mods.light_vulnerability) taken += tgt.form.mods.light_vulnerability;
    }
    if (tgt.tags.has('construct') && src) taken += K.stat(src, 'construct_damage_taken');
    if (b.zones.length && EB.B) {
      if (tgt.row === 'vanguard' && EB.B.zoneHeld(b, 'zon_iron_barricade', tgt.side)) taken -= 0.25;
      if (K.PHYS[type] && b.zones.some((z) => z.template === 'zon_arena' && K.byId(b, z.src) && K.byId(b, z.src).side !== tgt.side)) taken += 0.15;
      if (o.melee && tgt.row === 'rear' && src && EB.B.zoneHeld(b, 'zon_firewall', tgt.side)) { const zs = b.zones.find((z) => z.template === 'zon_firewall'); const c = K.byId(b, zs.src); if (c) setTimeout0(() => K.damage(b, c, src, K.hit(c) * 0.3, 'fire', { canCrit: false, dot: true })); }
    }
    let red = U.clamp(K.stat(tgt, 'damage_reduction'), -1, 0.8);
    let res;
    const ign = (o.ignoreResist || 0) + (K.stacks(tgt, 'laid_bare') >= 3 ? 0.1 : 0);
    if (K.PHYS[type] && type !== 'blood') {
      const armor = o.ignoreArmor ? Math.min(0, K.stat(tgt, 'armor')) : K.stat(tgt, 'armor') * (1 - (o.armorPierce || 0));
      res = armor + K.stat(tgt, 'physical_resist');
    } else {
      res = K.stat(tgt, 'magic_resist') * 0.6 + K.stat(tgt, type + '_resist') + (ct !== type ? K.stat(tgt, ct + '_resist') : 0);
    }
    res = U.clamp(res - ign, -1, 0.85);
    let dmg = base * Math.max(0.1, mult) * Math.max(0.1, taken) * (1 - red) * (1 - res);
    if (tgt.statuses.frozen && !o.dot) dmg *= 1.5;
    if (tgt.flags.encased && !o.dot) dmg *= 1.25;
    if (tgt.flags.pressureHit && !o.dot) dmg *= 1.25;
    return Math.max(0, dmg);
  };

  K.vsMatches = function (b, src, tgt, what, m) {
    switch (what) {
      case 'target': return !m.vs || m.vs === tgt.id;
      case 'self': return tgt.id === m.src;
      case 'rear': return tgt.row === 'rear';
      case 'wounded': return K.hpPct(tgt) < 0.5;
      case 'vanguard': return tgt.row === 'vanguard';
      case 'corrupted': case 'undead': case 'beast': case 'construct': case 'boss': return tgt.tags.has(what);
      case 'asleep': return K.hasStatus(tgt, 'asleep');
      case 'feared': return K.hasStatus(tgt, 'feared') || K.hasStatus(tgt, 'terror');
      default:
        return K.hasStatus(tgt, what);
    }
  };

  K.critChance = function (b, src, tgt, o) {
    let c = 0.05 + K.stat(src, 'crit') + (o.critBonus || 0);
    for (const id in tgt.statuses) {
      const fx = EB.FX[id]; const st = tgt.statuses[id];
      if (!fx) continue;
      if (fx.critTaken) c += fx.critTaken * st.stacks;
      if (fx.srcCrit && st.src === src.id) c += fx.srcCrit * st.stacks;
    }
    if (K.hasStatus(src, 'fortune')) c += 0;
    return U.clamp(c, 0, 1);
  };

  // Main entry: deal damage from src to tgt. Returns final health damage.
  K.damage = function (b, src, tgt, base, type, o) {
    o = o || {};
    if (!tgt.alive || base <= 0) return 0;
    if (tgt.flags.invulnerable || tgt.flags.banished) { K.log(b, { k: 'text', msg: `${tgt.name} is untouchable.`, cls: 'dim' }); return 0; }
    if (tgt.flags.immuneDamageOnce) { tgt.flags.immuneDamageOnce = false; K.log(b, { k: 'miss', src: src && src.id, tgt: tgt.id, why: 'negated' }); return 0; }
    let dmg = o.raw ? base : K.computeDamage(b, src, tgt, base, type, o);
    let crit = false;
    if (src && o.canCrit !== false && !o.dot) {
      const cc = o.forceCrit ? 1 : K.critChance(b, src, tgt, o);
      if (b.rng() < cc) {
        crit = true;
        dmg *= 1.5 + K.stat(src, 'crit_damage');
      }
    }
    if (tgt.flags.negateDamagePct) dmg *= 1 - tgt.flags.negateDamagePct;
    dmg = Math.round(dmg);
    if (dmg <= 0) dmg = 1;
    // guard stacks: block 15% each, then spent
    if (!o.dot && K.hasStatus(tgt, 'guard')) {
      const g = K.stacks(tgt, 'guard');
      dmg = Math.round(dmg * (1 - Math.min(0.9, 0.15 * g)));
      K.removeStatus(b, tgt, 'guard');
    }
    if (tgt.statuses.frozen && !o.dot) K.removeStatus(b, tgt, 'frozen');
    tgt.flags.encased = false; tgt.flags.pressureHit = false;
    // shields
    let absorbed = 0;
    if (!o.bypassShield) {
      for (const s of tgt.shields) {
        if (dmg <= 0) break;
        const take = Math.min(s.amt, dmg);
        s.amt -= take; dmg -= take; absorbed += take;
      }
      const broke = tgt.shields.some((s) => s.amt <= 0 && s.max > 0);
      tgt.shields = tgt.shields.filter((s) => s.amt > 0);
      if (absorbed > 0) {
        K.fire(b, tgt, 'shield_absorbs', { other: src, amount: absorbed });
        if (broke) K.fire(b, tgt, 'shield_breaks', { other: src });
      }
    }
    // share damage links
    if (dmg > 0 && tgt.flags.shareDamage && !o.shared) {
      for (const sh of tgt.flags.shareDamage) {
        const w = K.byId(b, sh.with);
        if (w && w.alive && w !== tgt) {
          const part = Math.round(dmg * sh.pct);
          dmg -= part;
          K.damage(b, src, w, part, type, { shared: true, canCrit: false, bypassCompute: true, raw: true });
        }
      }
    }
    // health
    const before = tgt.hp;
    let floor = -Infinity;
    if (tgt.flags.cannotDropBelow) floor = Math.max(floor, Math.round(K.maxHp(tgt) * tgt.flags.cannotDropBelow));
    if (dmg > 0) tgt.hp = Math.max(tgt.hp - dmg, floor === -Infinity ? tgt.hp - dmg : Math.min(tgt.hp, floor));
    const dealt = before - tgt.hp;
    tgt.lastDamage.unshift({ amt: dealt, type, src: src && src.id, turn: tgt.turns });
    if (tgt.lastDamage.length > 6) tgt.lastDamage.pop();
    tgt.lastDamageTaken = { amt: dealt + absorbed, type };
    K.log(b, { k: 'dmg', src: src && src.id, tgt: tgt.id, amt: dealt, abs: absorbed, type, crit, dot: !!o.dot });
    if (src) {
      src.dealtThisTurn = (src.dealtThisTurn || 0) + dealt;
      if (!o.dot) src.directThisAction = (src.directThisAction || 0) + dealt;
      if (dealt > 0 && !o.dot && !o.reflected) {
        // stealth breaks on dealing damage
        if (K.hasStatus(src, 'stealthed') && !src.flags.keepStealth) K.removeStatus(b, src, 'stealthed');
        // lifesteal
        const ls = K.stat(src, 'lifesteal');
        if (ls > 0) K.heal(b, src, src, dealt * ls);
      }
      if (o.dot && dealt > 0 && src.form && src.form.mods && src.form.mods.lifesteal_from_dots) K.heal(b, src, src, dealt * src.form.mods.lifesteal_from_dots);
      if (crit) {
        K.fire(b, src, 'when_you_crit', { other: tgt, amount: dealt });
        for (const a of K.allies(b, src)) K.fire(b, a, 'ally_crits', { other: tgt, ally: src });
        K.fire(b, src, 'linked_crits', { other: tgt });
      }
    }
    // events on the victim side
    if (dealt + absorbed > 0) {
      const ev = { other: src, amount: dealt, type, dot: !!o.dot, melee: !!o.melee, ranged: !!o.ranged, spell: !!o.spell };
      K.fire(b, tgt, 'when_take_damage', ev);
      K.fire(b, tgt, 'when_you_take_damage', ev);
      if (src && K.isEnemy(src, tgt)) K.fire(b, tgt, 'when_take_damage_from_enemy', ev);
      K.fire(b, tgt, 'target_takes_damage', ev);
      K.fire(b, tgt, 'linked_takes_damage', ev);
      if (!o.dot && src && K.isEnemy(src, tgt)) {
        // gain-on-hit boons
        if (tgt.flags.gainOnHit) for (const g of tgt.flags.gainOnHit) K.applyStatus(b, tgt, tgt, g, 1);
        // atonement & ire when hurt
        if (tgt.flags.atonementOnHurt) K.applyStatus(b, tgt, tgt, 'atonement', 1);
        for (const a of K.allies(b, tgt, true)) if (a.flags.ireOnHurt) K.applyStatus(b, a, a, 'ire', 1);
        // Steady aim lost when hit hard
        if (K.hasStatus(tgt, 'aim') && dealt > 0.2 * K.maxHp(tgt) && !tgt.flags.aimCannotBreak) K.removeStatus(b, tgt, 'aim');
      }
      // shards: extra damage per stack on each hit
      if (!o.dot && !o.shardsProc && K.hasStatus(tgt, 'shards') && src) {
        const s = K.stacks(tgt, 'shards');
        K.damage(b, src, tgt, 0.06 * s * K.hit(src, null, 'physical'), 'physical', { dot: true, shardsProc: true, canCrit: false });
      }
      // chained: share to other chained
      if (!o.chainShare && K.hasStatus(tgt, 'chained') && dealt > 0) {
        const pct = (b.flags.chainedFull ? 1 : 0.1 * K.stacks(tgt, 'chained'));
        for (const x of b.units) if (x !== tgt && x.alive && x.side === tgt.side && K.hasStatus(x, 'chained')) {
          K.damage(b, src, x, Math.round(dealt * pct), type, { chainShare: true, dot: true, canCrit: false, raw: true });
        }
      }
      // asleep breaks on damage
      if (K.hasStatus(tgt, 'asleep') && !(src && src.flags.sleepSafe) && !(tgt.statuses.asleep.minTurns > 0)) K.removeStatus(b, tgt, 'asleep');
    }
    if (EB.X && EB.X.onHpChange) EB.X.onHpChange(b, tgt, before, src);
    if (tgt.hp <= 0) K.checkDeath(b, tgt, src, o);
    return dealt;
  };

  // ---------- death ----------
  K.checkDeath = function (b, u, killer, o) {
    if (!u.alive || u.hp > 0) return;
    // linked_would_die
    K.fire(b, u, 'linked_would_die', { other: killer });
    if (u.hp > 0) return;
    if (K.stacks(u, 'grit') >= 5) { u.hp = 1; K.removeStatus(b, u, 'grit'); K.say(b, `${u.name} refuses to fall. (Grit)`, 'good'); return; }
    if (K.stacks(u, 'grace') >= 9) { u.hp = 1; K.removeStatus(b, u, 'grace'); K.say(b, `${u.name} lands on their feet. (Grace)`, 'good'); return; }
    if (u.flags.selfRevive && u.flags.selfRevive.uses > 0) {
      u.flags.selfRevive.uses--;
      u.hp = Math.max(1, Math.round(K.maxHp(u) * (u.flags.selfRevive.health || 0.3)));
      K.say(b, `${u.name} rises again!`, 'good');
      K.log(b, { k: 'revive', tgt: u.id });
      return;
    }
    if (u.flags.oath && u.flags.oath.restriction === 'accept_death_once' && !u.flags.oath.used) {
      u.flags.oath.used = true; u.hp = Math.round(K.maxHp(u) * 0.3); K.say(b, `${u.name}'s oath holds death back once.`, 'good'); return;
    }
    u.hp = 0;
    u.alive = false;
    K.log(b, { k: 'death', tgt: u.id, src: killer && killer.id });
    K.say(b, deathLine(u), u.side === 'ally' ? 'bad' : 'good');
    // drop auras sourced by this unit
    for (const x of b.units) x.mods = x.mods.filter((m) => !(m.aura && m.src === u.id));
    b.zones = b.zones.filter((z) => z.src !== u.id || z.persist);
    // death statuses
    if (K.hasStatus(u, 'ashen')) {
      const st = u.statuses.ashen; const s = K.byId(b, st.src);
      if (s && s.alive) K.heal(b, s, s, K.maxHp(s) * 0.03 * st.stacks);
    }
    if (K.hasStatus(u, 'infected')) {
      const st = u.statuses.infected; const near = K.allies(b, u).filter((x) => x.alive);
      if (near.length) K.applyStatus(b, K.byId(b, st.src) || null, b.rng.pick(near), 'infected', Math.ceil(st.stacks / 2));
    }
    if (K.hasStatus(u, 'spores')) {
      const s = K.byId(b, u.statuses.spores.src);
      if (s && s.alive && EB.templateIds && EB.content.templates.cnj_puffball) EB.OPS.summon({ b, actor: s, ab: { summonStats: 1, powerMult: 1 } }, { template: 'cnj_puffball', count: 1, duration: 3 }, []);
    }
    if (u.onDeath && killer) EB.X.run({ b, actor: K.byId(b, u.owner) || u, ab: null, power: 1 }, u.onDeath, K.enemies(b, u));
    // events
    if (killer) {
      K.fire(b, killer, 'on_kill', { other: u });
      K.fire(b, killer, 'on_kill_by_you_or_familiar', { other: u });
      if (killer.owner) { const ow = K.byId(b, killer.owner); if (ow) K.fire(b, ow, 'on_kill_by_you_or_familiar', { other: u }); }
      killer.killsThisAction = (killer.killsThisAction || 0) + 1;
    }
    for (const x of b.units) if (x.alive && x.side !== u.side) K.fire(b, x, 'enemy_dies', { other: u });
    b.corpses.push({ id: u.id, side: u.side, name: u.name });
    // dying hero's summons fade
    for (const x of b.units) if (x.alive && x.owner === u.id && x.kind !== 'familiar') { x.alive = false; K.log(b, { k: 'fade', tgt: x.id }); }
    // a vow that fails/succeeds
    if (b.onDeathCheck) b.onDeathCheck(u);
    K.checkEnd(b);
  };
  function deathLine(u) {
    if (u.kind === 'decoy') return `${u.name} pops like a soap bubble.`;
    if (u.kind === 'conjuration') return `${u.name} unravels.`;
    if (u.side === 'ally' && u.isPlayer) return `${u.name} goes down.`;
    return `${u.name} is defeated.`;
  }

  K.checkEnd = function (b) {
    if (b.over) return;
    const foes = b.units.filter((u) => u.side === 'enemy' && u.alive && u.kind !== 'decoy' && u.kind !== 'conjuration' && !u.flags.summoned);
    const hero = b.units.find((u) => u.isPlayer);
    if (!foes.length && !b.units.some((u) => u.side === 'enemy' && u.alive && u.flags.banished)) { b.over = true; b.winner = 'ally'; K.log(b, { k: 'end', winner: 'ally' }); }
    else if (hero && !hero.alive) { b.over = true; b.winner = 'enemy'; K.log(b, { k: 'end', winner: 'enemy' }); }
  };

  // ---------- statuses ----------
  K.resistChance = function (b, tgt, id, src) {
    const d = K.statusDef(id);
    if (d.kind === 'boon') return 0;
    let r = K.stat(tgt, 'affliction_resist');
    const cat = d.category;
    if (cat === 'mind') r += K.stat(tgt, 'mind_resist');
    if (cat === 'curse') r += K.stat(tgt, 'curse_resist');
    if (cat === 'poison') r += K.stat(tgt, 'poison_resist');
    if (id === 'feared' || id === 'terror' || id === 'dread') r += K.stat(tgt, 'fear_resist') + K.stat(tgt, 'mind_resist') * 0.5;
    if (id === 'stunned') r += K.stat(tgt, 'stun_resist');
    if (id === 'staggered') r += K.stat(tgt, 'staggered_resist');
    if (src) r -= K.stat(src, 'affliction_potency') + (cat === 'curse' ? K.stat(src, 'curse_potency') : 0);
    if (tgt.boss && ['stunned', 'frozen', 'asleep', 'feared', 'confused', 'paralyzed'].includes(id)) r += 0.35;
    return U.clamp(r, 0, 0.9);
  };

  K.isImmune = function (b, u, id) {
    if (u.flags.immune && u.flags.immune[id]) return true;
    if (u.form && u.form.mods && Array.isArray(u.form.mods.immune) && (u.form.mods.immune.includes(id) || u.form.mods.immune.includes(K.statusDef(id).category))) return true;
    const fx = EB.FX;
    for (const sid in u.statuses) { const f = fx[sid]; if (f && f.immune && f.immune(u, id)) return true; }
    if (u.tags.has('construct') && ['bleeding', 'poisoned', 'feared', 'asleep', 'confused', 'beguiled', 'temptation', 'terror'].includes(id)) return true;
    if (u.form && u.form.mods && u.form.mods.fear_immune && (id === 'feared' || id === 'terror')) return true;
    if (u.form && u.form.mods && u.form.mods.curse_immune && K.statusDef(id).category === 'curse') return true;
    return false;
  };

  // opts: {dur, force, noEvents}
  K.applyStatus = function (b, src, tgt, id, stacks, opts) {
    opts = opts || {};
    if (!tgt || !tgt.alive || !id) return 0;
    const d = K.statusDef(id);
    stacks = stacks == null ? 1 : stacks;
    if (stacks <= 0) return 0;
    const affl = d.kind !== 'boon';
    if (affl && K.isImmune(b, tgt, id)) { K.log(b, { k: 'resist', tgt: tgt.id, id, why: 'immune' }); return 0; }
    if (!affl && tgt.flags.blockBoons) return 0;
    if (!affl && tgt.flags.blockBoonsChance && b.rng() < tgt.flags.blockBoonsChance) return 0;
    if (affl && src && src.side !== tgt.side && !opts.force) {
      if (K.hasStatus(tgt, 'warded')) { K.reduceStatus(b, tgt, 'warded', 1); K.log(b, { k: 'resist', tgt: tgt.id, id, why: 'warded' }); return 0; }
      if (tgt.flags.reflectNext && (tgt.flags.reflectNext.cat === 'any' || tgt.flags.reflectNext.cat === d.category || (tgt.flags.reflectNext.cat === 'control' && EB.FX[id] && EB.FX[id].control) || tgt.flags.reflectNext.cat === 'spell' || tgt.flags.reflectNext.cat === 'magic' && d.category === 'magic')) {
        tgt.flags.reflectNext = null;
        K.say(b, `${tgt.name} turns ${d.name} back on ${src.name}.`, 'good');
        return K.applyStatus(b, tgt, src, id, stacks, Object.assign({}, opts, { force: true }));
      }
      if (b.rng() < K.resistChance(b, tgt, id, src)) { K.log(b, { k: 'resist', tgt: tgt.id, id }); K.fire(b, tgt, 'target_fails_chance', { other: src, status: id }); return 0; }
    }
    if (src && src.flags.modApps && src.flags.modApps[id]) stacks += src.flags.modApps[id];
    if (src && src.flags.statusGain && src.flags.statusGain[id] && !affl) stacks = Math.round(stacks * (1 + src.flags.statusGain[id]) * 100) / 100;
    // kindled immune to chilled; some statuses prevent hiding
    if (id === 'chilled' && K.hasStatus(tgt, 'kindled')) return 0;
    if (id === 'stealthed' && (tgt.flags.noStealth || ['starlit', 'quarry', 'laid_bare', 'spotted'].some((x) => K.hasStatus(tgt, x)))) { K.log(b, { k: 'resist', tgt: tgt.id, id, why: 'can\'t hide' }); return 0; }
    const fx = EB.FX[id] || {};
    let max = d.maxStacks || 1;
    if (src && src.flags.maxStacks && src.flags.maxStacks[id]) max += src.flags.maxStacks[id];
    if (tgt.flags.maxStacks && tgt.flags.maxStacks[id]) max += tgt.flags.maxStacks[id];
    const cur = tgt.statuses[id];
    let dur = opts.dur;
    if (dur == null) dur = fx.defaultDur != null ? fx.defaultDur : d.decay != null ? 'combat' : affl ? (max > 1 ? 4 : 2) : 'combat';
    if (typeof dur === 'number' && src && affl) {
      dur += Math.round(K.stat(src, 'affliction_duration'));
      if (src.flags.statusDur && src.flags.statusDur[id]) dur += src.flags.statusDur[id];
    }
    const pow = src ? K.hit(src, null, (d.tick && d.tick.type) || 'magic') : 10;
    if (cur) {
      cur.stacks = Math.min(max, cur.stacks + stacks);
      if (dur === 'combat' || cur.dur === 'combat') cur.dur = 'combat';
      else { cur.dur = Math.max(cur.dur, dur); cur.stamp = K.stamp(); }
      if (src) { cur.src = src.id; cur.srcSide = src.side; cur.pow = Math.max(cur.pow || 0, pow); }
    } else {
      tgt.statuses[id] = { stamp: K.stamp(), stacks: Math.min(max, stacks), dur, src: src ? src.id : null, srcSide: src ? src.side : null, pow, fresh: true };
    }
    const now = tgt.statuses[id];
    if (src && src.flags.potency && src.flags.potency[id]) { now.potency = src.flags.potency[id]; now.mult = 1 + now.potency; }
    if (now.stacks % 1 !== 0 && now.stacks >= 1 && id !== 'empowered' && id !== 'bloodprice') now.stacks = Math.round(now.stacks * 100) / 100;
    K.log(b, { k: 'status', tgt: tgt.id, id, stacks: now.stacks, add: stacks, boon: !affl });
    if (fx.onApply) fx.onApply(b, tgt, now, src);
    // thresholds
    if (fx.at && now.stacks >= fx.at.n && !opts.noThreshold) {
      if (fx.at.reset !== false) delete tgt.statuses[id];
      K.log(b, { k: 'burst', tgt: tgt.id, id });
      fx.at.fn(b, tgt, src, now);
    }
    if (!opts.noEvents) {
      if (affl) {
        K.fire(b, tgt, 'when_affliction_applied_to_you', { other: src, status: id });
        K.fire(b, tgt, 'when_affliction_applied_to_target', { other: src, status: id });
        if (d.category === 'mind' && src && K.isEnemy(src, tgt)) K.fire(b, tgt, 'enemy_mind_affliction_on_you', { other: src, status: id });
      }
      if (id === 'blessed') K.fire(b, tgt, 'when_you_gain_blessed', { other: src });
      if (id === 'verse') K.fire(b, tgt, 'when_you_gain_verse', { other: src });
      if (id === 'stealthed') K.fire(b, tgt, 'when_self_stealthed', { other: src });
      if (now.stacks >= 5 && cur && cur.stacks - stacks < 5) K.fire(b, tgt, 'status_reaches_5', { other: src, status: id });
      if (src) {
        const ess = src.signatureStatuses;
        if (ess && ess.includes(id)) K.fire(b, src, 'when_you_apply_signature', { other: tgt, status: id });
      }
    }
    return stacks;
  };

  K.reduceStatus = function (b, u, id, n) {
    const st = u.statuses[id];
    if (!st) return 0;
    const take = Math.min(st.stacks, n);
    st.stacks -= take;
    if (st.stacks <= 0.001) K.removeStatus(b, u, id);
    return take;
  };
  K.removeStatus = function (b, u, id) {
    const st = u.statuses[id];
    if (!st) return 0;
    delete u.statuses[id];
    u.lastRemoved = u.lastRemoved || [];
    u.lastRemoved.unshift({ id, stacks: st.stacks, dur: st.dur, src: st.src });
    const fx = EB.FX[id];
    if (fx && fx.onRemove) fx.onRemove(b, u, st);
    K.log(b, { k: 'unstatus', tgt: u.id, id });
    return st.stacks;
  };
  K.afflictions = (u) => Object.keys(u.statuses).filter((id) => !K.isBoon(id));
  K.boons = (u) => Object.keys(u.statuses).filter((id) => K.isBoon(id));

  // ---------- events / hooks ----------
  // A hook lives on the unit whose events it watches.
  K.addHook = function (b, u, h) {
    h.id = h.id || U.uid('h');
    h.stamp = K.stamp();
    h.used = 0;
    u.hooks.push(h);
    return h;
  };
  K.fire = function (b, u, trig, ev) {
    if (!u || !u.hooks.length || b.depth > 6) return;
    for (const h of u.hooks.slice()) {
      if (h.trigger !== trig) continue;
      if (h.limitPerTurn && h.used >= h.limitPerTurn) continue;
      if (h.filter && !h.filter(b, u, ev)) continue;
      const caster = K.byId(b, h.caster) || u;
      if (!caster.alive && !h.posthumous) continue;
      h.used++;
      if (b.stats) b.stats[trig] = (b.stats[trig] || 0) + 1;
      b.depth++;
      try {
        if (h.fn) h.fn(b, u, ev, h);
        else EB.X.runHook(b, u, h, ev);
      } finally { b.depth--; }
      if (h.once) u.hooks = u.hooks.filter((x) => x !== h);
    }
  };
  // fire for every member of a side (optionally excluding one)
  K.fireSide = function (b, side, trig, ev, except) {
    for (const x of b.units) if (x.alive && x.side === side && x !== except) K.fire(b, x, trig, ev);
  };
})(globalThis.EB = globalThis.EB || {});

;
(function (EB) {
  'use strict';
  const K = EB.K;
  // Mechanical definitions for every status. Text lives in content; numbers live here.
  // mods are per stack. "at" = threshold burst. skip() returns a reason when the turn is lost.
  const stun = (n) => (b, u, src) => K.applyStatus(b, src, u, 'stunned', 1, { dur: n || 1, force: true });
  const apply = (id, stacks, dur) => (b, u, src) => K.applyStatus(b, src, u, id, stacks, { dur, force: true });
  const both = (...fns) => (b, u, src, st) => fns.forEach((f) => f(b, u, src, st));
  const dot = (type, per) => ({ type, per });

  const FX = (EB.FX = {
    // ----- shared afflictions -----
    burning: { tickDmg: dot('fire', 0.05) },
    bleeding: { tickDmg: dot('blood', 0.05) },

    asleep: { control: true, skip: () => 'is asleep', defaultDur: 2, endTurn: (b, u, st) => { if (st.minTurns > 0) st.minTurns--; } },

    arcana: { mods: { spell_power: 0.04 }, at: { n: 5, fn: (b, u) => { u.flags.freeSpell = true; K.say(b, `${u.name}'s Arcana overflows: next spell is free.`, 'good'); } } },
    // ----- essence statuses -----
    ashen: { mods: { accuracy: -0.04, healing_received: -0.05 } },

    bone_plating: { mods: { damage_reduction: 0.05 } },
    aim: { mods: { crit: 0.05, accuracy: 0.03 } },

    runebound: { mods: { magic_resist: -0.05 }, at: { n: 5, fn: apply('silenced', 1, 2) } },

    eroded: { mods: { armor: -0.05, swiftness: -0.03 }, at: { n: 5, fn: (b, u, src) => { K.applyStatus(b, src, u, 'slowed', 1, { dur: 2, force: true }); K.applyStatus(b, src, u, 'vulnerable', 2, { dur: 2, force: true }); } } },
    constricted: { mods: { speed: -0.05, evasion: -0.04 }, tickDmg: dot('physical', 0.03) },
    shrouded: { mods: { accuracy: -0.05 }, srcCrit: 0.05 },
    guard: {},
    hushed: { mods: { spell_cost: 0.06 }, at: { n: 5, fn: apply('silenced', 1, 2) } },
    temptation: { mods: { mind_resist: -0.03 }, at: { n: 7, fn: (b, u, src) => EB.X.indulge(b, u, src) } },
    smothered: { mods: { accuracy: -0.05, stamina_regen: -0.05 }, at: { n: 5, fn: both(apply('blinded', 1, 1), apply('silenced', 1, 1)) } },
    verse: {},
    anima: { mods: { spirit_damage: 0.02, psychic_damage: 0.02, mind_resist: 0.03 } },
    static: { mods: { speed: 0.02 } },
    webbed: { mods: { swiftness: -0.06 }, at: { n: 5, fn: both(apply('rooted', 1, 1), stun(1)) } },
    starlit: { noHide: true },
    sunstruck: { mods: { accuracy: -0.05 }, typeTaken: { holy: 0.05, radiant: 0.05 }, at: { n: 5, fn: apply('blinded', 1, 2) } },
    momentum: { mods: { speed: 0.03, damage: 0.02 } },
    thorns: { thorns: true },
    swell: {},
    time_lag: { delayPct: 0.05, at: { n: 5, fn: (b, u) => { u.flags.loseNextTurn = true; } } },
    quarry: { mods: { evasion: -0.03 }, sideTaken: 0.04, noHide: true },
    laid_bare: { mods: { evasion: -0.04 }, noHide: true },
    unmade: { mods: { max_health: -0.03, magic_resist: -0.05 }, at: { n: 6, fn: (b, u) => EB.X.banish(b, u, 2) } },
    sworn: { mods: { damage: 0.02, damage_reduction: 0.02, affliction_resist: 0.02 } },
    untamed: { mods: { damage: 0.02, speed: 0.02 } },
    buffeted: { mods: { accuracy: -0.04 }, at: { n: 5, fn: (b, u) => { if (!K.flag(u, 'noMove')) u.row = 'rear'; u.t += 30; K.say(b, `${u.name} is blown back.`, 'good'); } } },
    withered: { mods: { max_health: -0.03, healing_received: -0.04 }, turnStart: (b, u) => { u.flags.witherSta = K.stacks(u, 'withered') >= 5; } },
    harried: { srcTaken: 0.04, at: { n: 5, fn: (b, u) => { u.t += 40; } } },
    ire: { mods: { damage: 0.03 } },
    // mobility & utility essences
    stride: { mods: { speed: 0.03, evasion: 0.03 } },
    // ----- divine & astral essences -----
    radiance: { mods: { healing_done: 0.03, holy_damage: 0.03, radiant_damage: 0.03 }, at: { n: 6, fn: (b, u, src) => { K.heal(b, u, u, K.maxHp(u) * 0.15); EB.X.cleanse(b, u, 'any', 1); K.say(b, `${u.name}'s Radiance breaks like a sunrise.`, 'good'); } } },
    ferried: { mods: { damage_taken: 0.03 }, at: { n: 5, fn: (b, u, src) => {
      if (!u.alive) return;
      if (u.hp <= K.maxHp(u) * 0.35 && !u.boss) { K.say(b, `The last door opens for ${u.name}.`, 'good'); K.loseHp(b, u, u.hp, src); K.checkDeath(b, u, src); }
      else if (src) K.damage(b, src, u, K.hit(src) * 1.2, 'spirit', { canCrit: false });
    } } },
    stakes: { mods: { crit: 0.03, crit_damage: 0.05, evasion: -0.02 } },
    hunted: { srcTaken: 0.04, srcTakenMax: 0.24, mods: { evasion: -0.02 }, onApply: (b, u) => { if (K.hasStatus(u, 'stealthed')) K.removeStatus(b, u, 'stealthed'); } },
    woven: { mods: { speed: -0.03 }, at: { n: 5, fn: (b, u, src) => { K.applyStatus(b, src, u, 'rooted', 1, { dur: 2, force: true }); K.applyStatus(b, src, u, 'vulnerable', 2, { force: true }); K.say(b, `The weave closes on ${u.name}.`, 'good'); } } },
    witnessed: { critTaken: 0.04, mods: { evasion: -0.03 }, onApply: (b, u) => { if (K.hasStatus(u, 'stealthed')) K.removeStatus(b, u, 'stealthed'); } },
    altitude: { mods: { evasion: 0.03, ranged_damage: 0.04 }, onApply: (b, u, st) => { if (st.stacks >= 3 && !u.flags.noFly) { u.flags.flying = true; u.flags.altitudeFlight = true; } }, endTurn: (b, u, st) => { if (st.stacks < 4 && u.flags.altitudeFlight && !u.flags.flyingTimed) { u.flags.flying = false; u.flags.altitudeFlight = false; } }, onRemove: (b, u) => { if (u.flags.altitudeFlight && !u.flags.flyingTimed) { u.flags.flying = false; u.flags.altitudeFlight = false; } } },
    bearing: { mods: { accuracy: 0.04, crit: 0.03 } },
    hooked: { mods: { evasion: -0.04 } },
  });

  // Fill in anything missing (future content) so nothing crashes.
  EB.ensureFX = function () {
    for (const id in EB.content.statuses) {
      if (!FX[id]) FX[id] = {};
      const d = EB.content.statuses[id];
      if (d.tick && !FX[id].tickDmg && !FX[id].tickHeal && d.tick.op === 'damage') FX[id].tickDmg = dot(d.tick.type, d.tick.powerPerStack);
      if (d.tick && d.tick.op === 'heal' && !FX[id].tickHeal) FX[id].tickHeal = d.tick.powerPerStack;
      if (d.kind === 'boon' && FX[id].defaultDur == null && d.maxStacks > 1) FX[id].defaultDur = 'combat';
    }
  };
})(globalThis.EB = globalThis.EB || {});

;
(function (EB) {
  'use strict';
  const U = EB.U, K = EB.K;
  const X = (EB.X = {});

  // ---------- hostility classification ----------
  const HOSTILE_OPS = new Set(('damage dispel steal_boon armor_break spread_status detonate_status execute execute_if_below interrupt control mind_control push pull_row push_row banish polymorph taunt silence_strongest increase_cooldowns skip_turn cancel_next_action convert_boons shield_strip remove_shields suppress_boons block_boons isolate trap contract offer_deal dispel_illusions steal_stat steal_status chain per_stack force_attack_ally lose_action_chance block_teleport block_stealth block_flight block_movement lock_teleport block_cleanse block_cleanse_status falsify_intent duel challenge random_damage pull_to_vanguard swap_positions shuffle_rows convert_boon_to copy_afflictions transfer_signature status_tick_now trigger_ticks burst_status_now extend_afflictions block_transform block_flee block_parry block_retreat suppress_enemy_auras increase_spell_costs backfire_chance shorten_boons forbid_ability_type calm pay_signature lose_signature taunt_reverse taunt_to_self untargetable_by isolate random_retarget_chance penalty_for_tricks block_boons_chance prevent_stealth reveal_when_stealthed block_stealth_enemies prevent_rise devour_summon cannot_target spread_on_turn asleep_duration_min copy_last_ability repeat_target_last_action chain_from_corpse bonus_per_chained_enemy spill_full_damage_to_chained trap_row trap_chance pierce_to_row_behind steal_boon hide_status').split(' '));
  X.isHostile = function (eff) {
    if (!eff || !eff.op) return false;
    if (eff.op === 'apply_status') return !K.isBoon(eff.status);
    if (eff.op === 'consume_status') return !K.isBoon(eff.status);
    if (eff.op === 'modify_stat') return eff.amount < 0 && !/taken|cost|fail/.test(eff.stat) || (eff.amount > 0 && /taken|cost|fail_chance/.test(eff.stat));
    if (eff.op === 'resource') return eff.percent < 0;
    if (eff.op === 'timeline') return eff.kind === 'delay';
    if (eff.op === 'reveal' || eff.op === 'conditional') return false;
    return HOSTILE_OPS.has(eff.op);
  };

  // ---------- target resolution ----------
  function firstOr(list, fallback) { return list && list.length ? list : fallback; }
  X.lowest = (arr) => arr.slice().sort((a, b) => K.hpPct(a) - K.hpPct(b))[0];
  X.strongest = (arr) => arr.slice().sort((a, b) => b.hp - a.hp)[0];
  X.targetable = (u) => u.alive && !u.flags.banished;

  X.resolve = function (ctx, spec, defaults, eff) {
    const b = ctx.b, a = ctx.actor;
    const ev = ctx.event || {};
    const def = (defaults || []).filter((u) => u && u.alive);
    const prim = def[0] || ctx.targets && ctx.targets[0];
    const rnd = (arr) => (arr.length ? [b.rng.pick(arr)] : []);
    switch (spec) {
      case undefined: case null: case '': return def;
      case 'self': return [a];
      case 'actor': return ctx.hook && ev.other && ev.other.alive ? [ev.other] : [a];
      case 'target': return def.length ? def : (ctx.targets || []).filter((u) => u.alive);
      case 'attacker': case 'source': case 'next_attacker':
        if (ev.other && ev.other.alive) return [ev.other];
        if (ctx.source && ctx.source.alive) return [ctx.source];
        return def.filter((u) => K.isEnemy(a, u));
      case 'owner': case 'summoner': {
        const o = a.owner ? K.byId(b, a.owner) : null;
        if (ctx.hook && ctx.hookListener && !K.isEnemy(a, ctx.hookListener)) return [ctx.hookListener];
        return [o && o.alive ? o : a];
      }
      case 'linked': case 'that_familiar': {
        if (ctx.linked && ctx.linked.alive) return [ctx.linked];
        if (ctx.hookListener && ctx.hookListener !== a) return [ctx.hookListener];
        const l = (a.flags.links || []).map((id) => K.byId(b, id)).filter((u) => u && u.alive);
        return l.length ? l : def;
      }
      case 'enemy_all': case 'all_enemies': return K.enemies(b, a);
      case 'ally_all': return K.allies(b, a, true);
      case 'everyone': return K.alive(b).filter((u) => !u.flags.banished);
      case 'all_familiars': return K.allies(b, a, true).filter((u) => u.kind === 'familiar' && (u.owner === a.id || a.owner === u.owner));
      case 'random_familiar': return rnd(K.allies(b, a, true).filter((u) => u.kind === 'familiar'));
      case 'ally_random': return rnd(firstOr(K.allies(b, a), [a]));
      case 'enemy_random': case 'random_enemy': return rnd(K.enemies(b, a).filter((u) => !K.hasStatus(u, 'stealthed')));
      case 'random_target': return rnd(K.alive(b).filter((u) => u !== a));
      case 'enemy_row': case 'target_row': {
        const t = prim && K.isEnemy(a, prim) ? prim : (K.enemies(b, a)[0]);
        if (!t) return [];
        return K.rowOf(b, t.side, t.row);
      }
      case 'enemy_adjacent': case 'adjacent': {
        const t = prim && K.isEnemy(a, prim) ? prim : ev.other;
        if (!t) return rnd(K.enemies(b, a));
        return K.rowOf(b, t.side, t.row).filter((u) => u !== t);
      }
      case 'enemy_vanguard': { const s = K.opp(a.side); const v = K.rowOf(b, s, 'vanguard'); return v.length ? v : K.rowOf(b, s, 'rear'); }
      case 'ally_vanguard': case 'vanguard': return K.rowOf(b, a.side, 'vanguard');
      case 'ally_rear': case 'rear': return K.rowOf(b, a.side, 'rear');
      case 'ally_row': return K.rowOf(b, a.side, a.row);
      case 'lowest_ally': { const l = X.lowest(K.allies(b, a, true)); return l ? [l] : []; }
      case 'lowest_enemy': { const l = X.lowest(K.enemies(b, a)); return l ? [l] : []; }
      case 'enemy_strongest': case 'strongest_enemy': { const l = X.strongest(K.enemies(b, a)); return l ? [l] : []; }
      case 'enemy_single': { const t = def.find((u) => K.isEnemy(a, u)) || (ev.other && K.isEnemy(a, ev.other) && ev.other.alive ? ev.other : null); return t ? [t] : rnd(K.enemies(b, a)); }
      case 'ally_single': case 'ally_or_self': { const t = def.find((u) => !K.isEnemy(a, u)); return t ? [t] : [a]; }
      case 'decoy': return K.allies(b, a).filter((u) => u.kind === 'decoy');
      case 'void': return [];
      case 'most_afflicted_enemy': { const e = K.enemies(b, a).sort((x, y) => K.afflictions(y).length - K.afflictions(x).length)[0]; return e ? [e] : []; }
      default: return def;
    }
  };

  // ---------- effect runner ----------
  X.run = function (ctx, effects, defaults) {
    if (!effects) return;
    for (const eff of effects) {
      if (!ctx.actor.alive && !ctx.posthumous) return;
      if (ctx.b.over && ctx.b.stopOnEnd) return;
      X.runOne(ctx, eff, defaults);
    }
  };

  X.runOne = function (ctx, eff, defaults) {
    if (!eff || typeof eff !== 'object') return;
    const b = ctx.b;
    const fn = EB.OPS[eff.op];
    if (!fn) { b.unknownOps = b.unknownOps || {}; b.unknownOps[eff.op] = (b.unknownOps[eff.op] || 0) + 1; return; }
    let tg = X.resolve(ctx, eff.to, defaults, eff);
    // missed targets drop hostile effects
    if (ctx.missed && ctx.missed.size && X.isHostile(eff)) tg = tg.filter((u) => !ctx.missed.has(u.id));
    if (eff.chance != null && eff.op !== 'parry' && eff.op !== 'reflect' && eff.op !== 'interrupt' && eff.op !== 'redirect' && eff.op !== 'push_row' && eff.op !== 'pull_row' && eff.op !== 'reduce_cooldowns' && eff.op !== 'polymorph' && eff.op !== 'calm' && eff.op !== 'force_attack_ally' && eff.op !== 'pull_to_vanguard' && eff.op !== 'chance_summon' && eff.op !== 'echo_next' && eff.op !== 'redirect_attacks_away' && eff.op !== 'redirect_to_decoy') {
      let p = eff.chance;
      p -= 0.05 * K.stacks(ctx.actor, 'jinxed');
      if (b.rng() >= p) {
        // fortune reroll
        if (K.stacks(ctx.actor, 'fortune') > 0 && ctx.actor.flags.rerollWithFortune !== false && b.rng() < p) { K.reduceStatus(b, ctx.actor, 'fortune', 1); }
        else { for (const t of tg) if (K.isEnemy(ctx.actor, t)) K.fire(b, t, 'target_fails_chance', { other: ctx.actor }); return; }
      }
    }
    fn(ctx, eff, tg, defaults);
  };

  // derive a child context
  X.sub = function (ctx, patch) { return Object.assign({}, ctx, patch); };

  X.power = function (ctx, eff) {
    let p = (eff.power || 0) * (ctx.power || 1);
    return p;
  };

  // ---------- hooks (reactions) ----------
  const START_END = new Set(['start_of_turn', 'end_of_turn', 'after_2_turns', 'fuse_expires']);
  const WARD = new Set(['target_attacked', 'target_takes_damage', 'when_affliction_applied_to_target', 'enemy_casts_spell_at_target', 'linked_attacked', 'linked_crits', 'linked_hits', 'linked_hits_shrouded', 'linked_takes_damage', 'linked_would_die', 'target_fails_chance', 'marked_target_hit_by_you']);
  const SIDE = new Set(['ally_acts', 'ally_crits', 'ally_health_below_30', 'ally_or_familiar_hits', 'ally_start_of_turn', 'enemy_acts', 'enemy_acts_in_zone', 'enemy_attacks_ally', 'enemy_attacks_familiar', 'enemy_attacks_into_vanguard', 'enemy_casts_spell', 'enemy_dies', 'enemy_health_below_20', 'enemy_start_of_turn', 'enemy_uses_ability', 'first_enemy_action', 'familiar_hits_target', 'when_ally_healed', 'when_ally_hit', 'when_familiar_hit', 'marked_target_bleeds']);
  X.TRIGGER_KIND = (t) => (START_END.has(t) ? 'owner' : WARD.has(t) ? 'ward' : SIDE.has(t) ? 'side' : 'you');

  X.installReaction = function (ctx, eff, targets) {
    const b = ctx.b, a = ctx.actor, ab = ctx.ab || {};
    const kind = X.TRIGGER_KIND(eff.trigger);
    let listeners;
    if (kind === 'owner') {
      listeners = targets.length ? targets : [a];
      if (eff.trigger === 'start_of_turn' && ab.target === 'self') listeners = [a];
    } else if (kind === 'ward') {
      listeners = ctx.linked ? [ctx.linked] : targets.length ? targets : [a];
      if (eff.trigger === 'marked_target_hit_by_you') listeners = [a];
    } else if (kind === 'side') listeners = [a];
    else {
      const allyTargets = targets.filter((u) => !K.isEnemy(a, u));
      listeners = ctx.inZone ? targets : allyTargets.length && !ctx.linked && ['ally_single', 'ally_all', 'ally_or_self', 'familiar_single', 'ally_row', 'ally_vanguard', 'ally_rear'].includes(ab.target) ? allyTargets : [a];
    }
    let dur = eff.duration;
    if (dur == null) dur = ctx.passive || ctx.formHook || ctx.itemHook ? 'combat' : ab.toggle ? 'aura' : 3;
    for (const l of listeners) {
      if (!l || !l.alive) continue;
      K.addHook(b, l, {
        trigger: eff.trigger, effects: eff.effects, caster: a.id, ab, power: ctx.power || 1,
        limitPerTurn: eff.limitPerTurn || (kind === 'owner' ? 0 : 0) || (ctx.passive && kind === 'you' ? 0 : 0),
        dur: dur === 'aura' ? 'combat' : dur, aura: ctx.auraKey || null, wait: eff.trigger === 'after_2_turns' ? 2 : null,
        tag: ctx.hookTag || null, crit: ctx.critBonus || 0, linked: ctx.linked ? ctx.linked.id : null,
      });
    }
  };

  X.runHook = function (b, listener, h, ev) {
    const caster = K.byId(b, h.caster) || listener;
    const ctx = { b, actor: caster, ab: h.ab, power: h.power || 1, event: ev || {}, hook: h, hookListener: listener, critBonus: h.crit || 0, targets: [], source: ev && ev.other, linked: h.linked ? K.byId(b, h.linked) : listener !== caster ? listener : null, posthumous: !!h.posthumous };
    const fixed = h.fixedTargets ? h.fixedTargets.map((id) => K.byId(b, id)).filter((u) => u && u.alive) : null;
    for (const eff of h.effects || []) {
      let defaults;
      if (fixed && fixed.length) { ctx.targets = fixed; X.runOne(ctx, eff, fixed.filter((t) => X.isHostile(eff) ? K.isEnemy(caster, t) : true).length ? fixed : [caster]); continue; }
      if (X.isHostile(eff)) {
        if (ev && ev.other && ev.other.alive && K.isEnemy(caster, ev.other)) defaults = [ev.other];
        else if (K.isEnemy(caster, listener)) defaults = [listener];
        else if (ev && ev.target && K.isEnemy(caster, ev.target)) defaults = [ev.target];
        else { const e = K.enemies(b, caster); defaults = e.length ? [b.rng.pick(e)] : []; }
      } else {
        defaults = K.isEnemy(caster, listener) ? [caster] : [listener];
      }
      ctx.targets = defaults;
      X.runOne(ctx, eff, defaults);
    }
  };

  // ---------- hp threshold events ----------
  X.onHpChange = function (b, u, before, src) {
    const max = K.maxHp(u);
    const was = before / max, now = u.hp / max;
    if (was >= 0.3 && now < 0.3 && u.hp > 0) {
      K.fire(b, u, 'health_below_30', { other: src });
      for (const a of K.allies(b, u)) K.fire(b, a, 'ally_health_below_30', { other: u, target: u });
    }
    if (was >= 0.2 && now < 0.2 && u.hp > 0) for (const e of K.enemies(b, u)) K.fire(b, e, 'enemy_health_below_20', { other: u });
  };

  // ---------- attack resolution ----------
  // Returns {hit:bool, target:unit}
  X.attackRoll = function (ctx, tgt, o) {
    const b = ctx.b, a = ctx.actor;
    o = o || {};
    const ev = { other: a, melee: !!o.melee, ranged: !!o.ranged, spell: !!o.spell, ability: ctx.ab, negated: false, dodged: false };
    K.fire(b, tgt, 'when_attacked', ev);
    K.fire(b, tgt, 'target_attacked', ev);
    K.fire(b, tgt, 'linked_attacked', ev);
    for (const x of K.allies(b, tgt)) K.fire(b, x, 'enemy_attacks_ally', Object.assign({}, ev, { target: tgt }));
    if (tgt.kind === 'familiar') for (const x of K.allies(b, tgt)) K.fire(b, x, 'enemy_attacks_familiar', Object.assign({}, ev, { target: tgt }));
    if (tgt.row === 'vanguard') K.fireSide(b, tgt.side, 'enemy_attacks_into_vanguard', Object.assign({}, ev, { target: tgt }));
    if (o.ranged) K.fire(b, tgt, 'ranged_attack_on_you', ev);
    if (o.spell) {
      K.fire(b, tgt, 'enemy_casts_spell_at_you', ev);
      K.fire(b, tgt, 'enemy_casts_spell_at_target', ev);
    }
    if (ev.redirect && ev.redirect.alive && ev.redirect !== tgt) { tgt = ev.redirect; K.say(b, `The attack is redirected to ${tgt.name}.`); }
    if (!a.alive || !tgt.alive) return { hit: false, target: tgt };
    if (ev.negated) { K.log(b, { k: 'miss', src: a.id, tgt: tgt.id, why: 'negated' }); return { hit: false, target: tgt }; }
    if (ev.dodged) { K.log(b, { k: 'miss', src: a.id, tgt: tgt.id, why: 'dodged' }); K.fire(b, tgt, 'attack_misses_you', ev); return { hit: false, target: tgt }; }
    if (a.flags.attacksMiss) return miss('swings at nothing');
    if (tgt.flags.phased && !o.spell) return miss('passes through');
    if (o.cantMiss || ctx.cantMiss) return { hit: true, target: tgt };
    // one-shot defensive flags
    const f = tgt.flags;
    if (f.dodgeNext) { f.dodgeNext = false; return miss('dodged'); }
    if (o.melee && f.parryNext && !f.noParry && !(a.flags.blockParry > 0) && !(a.item && a.item.mods && a.item.mods.unparryable)) {
      const pc = f.parryNext.chance == null ? 1 : f.parryNext.chance;
      if (b.rng() < pc) { f.parryNext = null; K.log(b, { k: 'miss', src: a.id, tgt: tgt.id, why: 'parried' }); if (f.counterPower) X.counter(ctx, tgt, a, f.counterPower); return { hit: false, target: tgt }; }
    }
    if (K.stacks(tgt, 'evasive') > 0) { K.reduceStatus(b, tgt, 'evasive', 1); return miss('evaded'); }
    if (K.stacks(tgt, 'foresight') >= 2 && b.rng() < 0.5) { K.reduceStatus(b, tgt, 'foresight', 2); return miss('foresaw'); }
    if (f.phaseDodge && b.rng() < f.phaseDodge) return miss('phased');
    if (f.dodgeSeen && a.id in (tgt.seenAttackers || {})) { if (b.rng() < 0.5) return miss('saw it coming'); }
    const parry = f.noParry || a.flags.blockParry ? 0 : K.stat(tgt, 'parry') + (tgt.item && tgt.item.mods && tgt.item.mods.block_chance || 0) + (f.parrySeen && tgt.seenAttackers && tgt.seenAttackers[a.id] ? f.parrySeen : 0);
    if (o.melee && parry > 0 && b.rng() < parry) { K.log(b, { k: 'miss', src: a.id, tgt: tgt.id, why: 'parried' }); return { hit: false, target: tgt }; }
    tgt.seenAttackers = tgt.seenAttackers || {}; tgt.seenAttackers[a.id] = 1;
    let p = 0.95 + K.stat(a, 'accuracy') - K.stat(tgt, 'evasion') - K.stat(tgt, 'evasion_vs_actor', a);
    if (tgt.form && tgt.form.mods && tgt.form.mods.untargetable_chance) p -= tgt.form.mods.untargetable_chance;
    p -= 0.05 * K.stacks(a, 'jinxed');
    if (o.melee && tgt.flags.flying && !a.flags.flying) p -= 0.15;
    if (o.spell) p += 0.1;
    p = U.clamp(p, 0.15, 1);
    if (b.rng() < p) return { hit: true, target: tgt };
    return miss('missed');
    function miss(why) { K.log(b, { k: 'miss', src: a.id, tgt: tgt.id, why }); K.fire(b, tgt, 'attack_misses_you', ev); return { hit: false, target: tgt }; }
  };

  X.counter = function (ctx, by, vs, power) {
    const b = ctx.b;
    if (!by.alive || !vs.alive) return;
    K.say(b, `${by.name} counters!`);
    K.damage(b, by, vs, K.hit(by, null, 'physical') * power, 'physical', { melee: true });
  };

  // after an attack lands on a target
  X.onHitEvents = function (ctx, tgt, o) {
    const b = ctx.b, a = ctx.actor;
    const ev = { other: tgt, target: tgt, melee: !!o.melee, ranged: !!o.ranged, spell: !!o.spell };
    a.attackedThisTurn = true;
    K.fire(b, a, 'when_you_hit', ev);
    K.fire(b, a, 'linked_hits', ev);
    if (K.hasStatus(tgt, 'shrouded')) K.fire(b, a, 'linked_hits_shrouded', ev);
    if (ctx.fromStealth && !ctx.stealthFired) { ctx.stealthFired = true; K.fire(b, a, 'first_hit_from_stealth', ev); }
    if (K.hasStatus(tgt, 'marked')) {
      const m = K.byId(b, tgt.statuses.marked.src);
      if (m === a || (m && a.owner === m.id)) K.fire(b, m, 'marked_target_hit_by_you', ev);
    }
    for (const x of K.allies(b, a)) K.fire(b, x, 'ally_or_familiar_hits', Object.assign({}, ev, { ally: a }));
    if (a.kind === 'familiar') { const ow = K.byId(b, a.owner); if (ow) K.fire(b, ow, 'familiar_hits_target', ev); }
    const vev = { other: a, melee: !!o.melee, ranged: !!o.ranged, spell: !!o.spell };
    K.fire(b, tgt, 'when_hit', vev);
    if (o.melee) K.fire(b, tgt, 'when_hit_melee', vev); else K.fire(b, tgt, 'when_hit_ranged', vev);
    for (const x of K.allies(b, tgt)) K.fire(b, x, 'when_ally_hit', Object.assign({}, vev, { target: tgt }));
    if (tgt.kind === 'familiar') for (const x of K.allies(b, tgt)) K.fire(b, x, 'when_familiar_hit', Object.assign({}, vev, { target: tgt }));
    // thorns, retaliation
    if (o.melee && K.stacks(tgt, 'thorns') > 0 && a.alive) {
      const s = K.stacks(tgt, 'thorns');
      K.damage(b, tgt, a, K.hit(tgt) * 0.04 * s * (1 + K.stat(tgt, 'thorns_damage')), 'physical', { reflected: true, canCrit: false, dot: true });
      if (b.rng() < Math.min(0.5, 0.1 * s)) K.applyStatus(b, tgt, a, 'bleeding', 1);
    }
    const ret = K.stat(tgt, 'thorns_damage') + K.stat(tgt, 'retaliation_damage');
    if (o.melee && ret > 0 && !K.stacks(tgt, 'thorns') && a.alive) K.damage(b, tgt, a, K.hit(tgt) * ret, 'physical', { reflected: true, canCrit: false, dot: true });
    // on-hit applications installed by abilities
    if (a.flags.onHitApply) for (const oh of a.flags.onHitApply) if (b.rng() < (oh.chance == null ? 1 : oh.chance)) K.applyStatus(b, a, tgt, oh.status, oh.stacks || 1);
    if (o.weapon && a.item && a.item.onHit) X.run(X.sub(ctx, { itemHook: true }), a.item.onHit, [tgt]);
    // rumble when hit
    if (tgt.flags.rumbleOnHit) K.applyStatus(b, tgt, tgt, 'rumble', 1);
    if (a.flags.rampageOnHit) K.applyStatus(b, a, a, 'rampage', 1);
    if (K.stacks(a, 'untamed') >= 5 && b.rng() < 0.2) {
      const r = b.rng.int(4);
      if (r === 0) K.applyStatus(b, a, tgt, 'bleeding', 2); else if (r === 1) K.applyStatus(b, a, tgt, 'staggered', 1); else if (r === 2) K.heal(b, a, a, K.maxHp(a) * 0.05); else K.applyStatus(b, a, a, 'hasted', 1, { dur: 1 });
    }
    if (K.stacks(a, 'majesty') >= 0) {}
    // stealth breaks / momentum
  };

  // ---------- generic helpers used by statuses & ops ----------
  X.cleanse = function (b, u, category, count) {
    const list = K.afflictions(u).filter((id) => {
      if (u.flags.blockCleanse) return false;
      if (u.flags.blockCleanseStatus && u.flags.blockCleanseStatus[id]) return false;
      if (id === 'doom' && u.statuses.doom && u.statuses.doom.dur === 1) return false;
      if (!category || category === 'any') return true;
      const d = K.statusDef(id);
      if (category === 'control') return !!(EB.FX[id] && EB.FX[id].control);
      return d.category === category;
    });
    let n = 0;
    for (const id of list) { if (n >= (count || 99)) break; K.removeStatus(b, u, id); n++; }
    if (n) K.log(b, { k: 'cleanse', tgt: u.id, n });
    return n;
  };
  X.dispel = function (b, u, count) {
    let n = 0;
    for (const id of K.boons(u)) { if (n >= (count || 1)) break; if (id === 'moonphase' || id === 'swell') continue; K.removeStatus(b, u, id); n++; }
    // also strip timed positive mods
    if (n < count) { const pm = u.mods.filter((m) => m.amt > 0 && typeof m.dur === 'number'); for (const m of pm.slice(0, count - n)) { u.mods.splice(u.mods.indexOf(m), 1); n++; } }
    if (n) K.log(b, { k: 'dispel', tgt: u.id, n });
    return n;
  };
  X.banish = function (b, u, turns) {
    if (u.boss) { u.t += 60; K.say(b, `${u.name} flickers but holds its place.`); return; }
    u.flags.banished = turns + 1;
    K.say(b, `${u.name} is banished from the fight for ${turns} turn${turns > 1 ? 's' : ''}.`, 'good');
    K.log(b, { k: 'banish', tgt: u.id });
  };
  X.lockAbilities = function (u, n) {
    const list = u.abilities.filter((a) => !a.def.passive).sort((x, y) => (y.def.cooldown || 0) - (x.def.cooldown || 0));
    list.forEach((a, i) => { a.locked = i < n; });
  };
  X.forgetStrongest = function (b, u, turns) {
    const s = u.abilities.filter((a) => !a.def.passive).sort((x, y) => (y.def.cooldown || 0) - (x.def.cooldown || 0))[0];
    if (s) { s.cd = Math.max(s.cd, turns); K.say(b, `${u.name} forgets how to use ${s.def.name}.`, 'good'); }
  };
  X.indulge = function (b, u, src) {
    const sins = ['sloth', 'gluttony', 'greed', 'wrath', 'pride', 'envy', 'lust'];
    const sin = b.rng.pick(sins);
    u.flags.loseNextTurn = true;
    switch (sin) {
      case 'gluttony': K.heal(b, u, u, K.maxHp(u) * 0.05); break;
      case 'wrath': K.damage(b, u, u, K.hit(u) * 0.5, 'physical', { canCrit: false }); break;
      case 'greed': if (src) { const bn = K.boons(u)[0]; if (bn) { K.removeStatus(b, u, bn); } } break;
      case 'pride': K.applyStatus(b, src, u, 'exposed', 1, { dur: 2, force: true }); break;
      case 'envy': K.applyStatus(b, src, u, 'weakened', 2, { dur: 2, force: true }); break;
      case 'lust': K.applyStatus(b, src, u, 'beguiled', 2, { force: true }); break;
      default: K.applyStatus(b, src, u, 'drowsy', 2, { force: true });
    }
    K.say(b, `${u.name} gives in to ${sin}.`, 'good');
  };
})(globalThis.EB = globalThis.EB || {});

;
(function (EB) {
  'use strict';
  const U = EB.U, K = EB.K, X = EB.X;
  const B = (EB.B = {});

  const RANGED_TAGS = new Set(['Bow', 'Ranged', 'Thrown', 'Sling']);
  const FLOW_OPS = new Set(['conditional', 'reaction', 'link', 'random_effect', 'random_of', 'choose_one', 'choose_vow', 'option', 'delayed_effect', 'per_enemy_with', 'per_consumed', 'per_dispelled', 'per_stack', 'next_ability_mod', 'echo_next', 'reveal', 'trap', 'trap_row', 'consume_status', 'oath', 'vow', 'contract', 'offer_deal', 'zone', 'summon', 'transform', 'equip_conjured', 'link_all_allies', 'chance_summon', 'raise_spirit', 'create_decoy', 'create_decoy_of']);
  const SELFISH = new Set(['heal', 'shield', 'cleanse', 'resource', 'regen', 'modify_stat', 'apply_status', 'extend_boons', 'cannot_drop_below', 'reduce_cooldowns', 'status_immunity', 'immune_status', 'dodge', 'parry', 'reflect', 'reflect_next', 'shield_grows', 'regen_shield', 'guaranteed_crit', 'buff_next_attack', 'invulnerable', 'immune_damage_once', 'self_revive', 'self_revive_once', 'add_stack_fraction', 'heal_per_status', 'heal_per_affliction', 'pay_health', 'pay_resource', 'stealth', 'store_healing', 'drop_aggro', 'untargetable_by_ai', 'fly', 'phase', 'phase_dodge', 'phase_dodge_chance', 'dodge_chance', 'intercept', 'counter', 'restore_last_shield', 'share_signature', 'give_status', 'status_gain_bonus', 'max_stacks', 'modify_applications', 'status_duration_bonus', 'status_potency', 'crit_chance_bonus', 'crit_heal_chance', 'lifesteal_from_all', 'share_lifesteal', 'convert_damage_to_heal', 'negate_damage']);

  B.isSpell = (def) => def.type === 'spell';
  B.firstDamageType = function (def) {
    let t = null;
    K.mapEffects(def.effects, (e) => { if (!t && e.op === 'damage') t = e.type; return e; });
    return t;
  };
  B.isMelee = function (def, u) {
    if (def.type !== 'special_attack' && !def.weapon) return false;
    if ((def.tags || []).some((t) => RANGED_TAGS.has(t))) return false;
    const t = B.firstDamageType(def);
    if (def.weapon) return !(u && u.item && u.item.mods && (u.item.mods.range || u.item.mods.reach === 'any'));
    return !t || K.PHYS[t];
  };
  B.isRanged = (def, u) => !B.isSpell(def) && !B.isMelee(def, u) && (def.type === 'special_attack' || def.weapon);
  B.hostileTarget = (t) => ['enemy_single', 'enemy_all', 'enemy_row', 'enemy_vanguard'].includes(t);
  B.friendlyTarget = (t) => ['self', 'ally_single', 'ally_or_self', 'ally_all', 'ally_row', 'ally_vanguard', 'ally_rear', 'familiar_single'].includes(t);

  // ---------- creation ----------
  B.create = function (opts) {
    const b = {
      rng: U.makeRng(opts.seed || Date.now()),
      time: 0, units: [], log: [], zones: [], corpses: [], flags: {}, depth: 0,
      over: false, winner: null, round: 0, current: null, env: opts.env || {}, turnNo: 0,
      onEvent: opts.onEvent || null, history: [], intentsVisible: true,
    };
    for (const spec of opts.units || []) B.addUnit(b, spec);
    return b;
  };
  B.addUnit = function (b, spec) {
    const u = spec.base ? spec : K.makeUnit(b, spec);
    b.units.push(u);
    u.signatureStatuses = (u.essences || []).map((e) => EB.content.essences[e] && EB.content.essences[e].signatureStatus).filter(Boolean);
    return u;
  };

  B.start = function (b) {
    // passives & companions
    for (const u of b.units.slice()) B.initUnit(b, u);
    for (const u of b.units) u.t = B.delay(b, u, 'standard') * (0.35 + 0.4 * b.rng());
    for (const u of b.units) if (u.ai || !u.isPlayer) EB.AI && EB.AI.plan(b, u);
    K.say(b, b.env.intro || 'Battle begins.', 'sys');
  };

  B.initUnit = function (b, u) {
    if (u.initDone) return;
    u.initDone = true;
    for (const slot of u.abilities) {
      const d = slot.def;
      if (d.passive) {
        B.execute(b, u, slot, B.defaultTargets(b, u, d), { passive: true, free: true });
      } else if (d.type === 'familiar' && u.isPlayer && !u.flags.noAutoFamiliar) {
        // familiars are permanent companions: present at combat start
        B.execute(b, u, slot, [], { free: true, quiet: true });
      }
    }
  };

  // ---------- timeline ----------
  B.delay = function (b, u, tempo) {
    const base = K.TEMPO[tempo] || 100;
    const sw = K.attr(u, 'swiftness');
    let f = (100 / (100 + 3 * sw)) * 1.3;
    const spd = K.stat(u, 'speed') + (u.speedScale - 1);
    let d = (base * f) / Math.max(0.3, 1 + spd);
    if (K.hasStatus(u, 'slowed')) d += 30;
    if (K.hasStatus(u, 'hasted')) d -= 25;
    for (const id in u.statuses) { const fx = EB.FX[id]; if (fx && fx.delay) d += fx.delay * u.statuses[id].stacks; if (fx && fx.delayPct) d *= 1 + fx.delayPct * u.statuses[id].stacks; }
    return Math.max(20, Math.round(d));
  };

  // Predict the next n turns (for the timeline bar)
  B.forecast = function (b, n) {
    const sim = b.units.filter((u) => u.alive && !u.flags.banished).map((u) => ({ u, t: u.t }));
    const out = [];
    if (!sim.length) return out;
    for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) {
      sim.sort((a, c) => a.t - c.t || K.attr(c.u, 'swiftness') - K.attr(a.u, 'swiftness'));
      const s = sim[0];
      out.push(s.u);
      s.t += B.delay(b, s.u, s.u.intent && s.u.intent.tempo || 'standard');
    }
    return out;
  };

  B.nextUnit = function (b) {
    const live = b.units.filter((u) => u.alive);
    if (!live.length) return null;
    live.sort((a, c) => a.t - c.t || K.attr(c, 'swiftness') - K.attr(a, 'swiftness') || (a.isPlayer ? -1 : 1));
    return live[0];
  };

  // Advance to the next unit whose turn it is. Returns {unit, skipped}
  B.advance = function (b) {
    if (b.over) return null;
    const u = B.nextUnit(b);
    if (!u) return null;
    b.time = Math.max(b.time, u.t);
    b.current = u;
    b.turnNo++;
    EB.turnNo = b.turnNo; EB.turnStamp = u.id + ':' + b.turnNo;
    const skip = B.startTurn(b, u);
    if (b.over) return { unit: u, skipped: true };
    if (!u.alive) { return { unit: u, skipped: true }; }
    if (skip) {
      K.log(b, { k: 'skip', tgt: u.id, why: skip });
      K.say(b, `${u.name} ${skip}.`, u.side === 'ally' ? 'bad' : 'good');
      B.endTurn(b, u, B.delay(b, u, 'standard'));
      return { unit: u, skipped: true };
    }
    return { unit: u, skipped: false };
  };

  B.startTurn = function (b, u) {
    u.turns++;
    u.attackedThisTurn = false;
    u.dealtThisTurn = 0;
    u.actedThisTurn = false;
    u.hpAtTurnStart = u.hpAtTurnStart || [];
    u.hpAtTurnStart.unshift(u.hp); if (u.hpAtTurnStart.length > 5) u.hpAtTurnStart.pop();
    u.rowHistory = u.rowHistory || []; u.rowHistory.unshift(u.row); if (u.rowHistory.length > 5) u.rowHistory.pop();
    for (const h of u.hooks) h.used = 0;
    for (const h of u.hooks) if (h.trigger === 'first_enemy_action') h.firstDone = false;
    K.log(b, { k: 'turn', tgt: u.id });
    // banishment
    if (u.flags.banished) {
      u.flags.banished--;
      if (u.flags.banished > 0) return 'is elsewhere';
      K.say(b, `${u.name} returns to the fight.`); if (K.hasStatus(u, 'unmade')) K.removeStatus(b, u, 'unmade');
    }
    // summon expiry
    if (u.expireAt) { u.expireAt.turns--; if (u.expireAt.turns < 0) { u.alive = false; K.log(b, { k: 'fade', tgt: u.id }); K.say(b, `${u.name} fades.`, 'dim'); K.checkEnd(b); return 'fades'; } }
    // cooldowns
    for (const s of u.abilities) if (s.cd > 0) s.cd -= 1 + (b.rng() < K.stat(u, 'cooldown_recovery') ? 1 : 0);
    for (const k in u.actionCds || {}) if (u.actionCds[k] > 0) u.actionCds[k]--;
    // resources
    const manaRegen = (3 + 0.45 * K.attr(u, 'spirit')) * (1 + K.stat(u, 'mana_regen'));
    let staRegen = (3 + 0.5 * K.attr(u, 'resilience')) * (1 + K.stat(u, 'stamina_regen'));
    if (u.flags.witherSta) staRegen *= 0.9;
    K.restore(b, u, 'mana', Math.round(manaRegen));
    K.restore(b, u, 'stamina', Math.round(staRegen));
    const regen = K.stat(u, 'regeneration') + (u.form && u.form.mods && u.form.mods.regen || 0);
    if (regen > 0) K.heal(b, u, u, K.maxHp(u) * regen);
    if (u.flags.regenPct) K.heal(b, u, u, K.maxHp(u) * u.flags.regenPct);
    if (u.form && u.form.mods) {
      if (u.form.mods.health_loss_per_turn) K.loseHp(b, u, K.maxHp(u) * u.form.mods.health_loss_per_turn, u);
      if (u.form.mods.coffers_per_turn) K.applyStatus(b, u, u, 'coffers', u.form.mods.coffers_per_turn);
    }
    if (u.flags.terrorCd > 0) u.flags.terrorCd--;
    // long fights wear everyone down: healing weakens after turn 15
    if (u.turns > 15 && u.turns % 3 === 0) { K.addMod(b, u, 'healing_received', -0.1, 'combat', null, { tag: 'exhaustion' }); if (u.isPlayer && u.turns === 18) K.say(b, 'The fight drags on. Healing is getting less effective for everyone.', 'sys'); }
    // status ticks
    for (const id of Object.keys(u.statuses)) {
      const st = u.statuses[id]; if (!st) continue;
      const fx = EB.FX[id] || {};
      const src = K.byId(b, st.src);
      if (fx.tickDmg && (!fx.tickDmgIf || fx.tickDmgIf(u))) {
        B.tick(b, u, id, 1);
        if (!u.alive) return 'falls';
      }
      if (fx.tickHeal) K.heal(b, src || u, u, fx.tickHeal * st.stacks * (st.pow || 10) * K.DOT_SCALE);
      if (fx.tickHealPct) K.heal(b, src || u, u, K.maxHp(u) * fx.tickHealPct * st.stacks * (1 + (src && src.flags.statusTickBonus && src.flags.statusTickBonus[id] || 0)));
      if (fx.turnStart) fx.turnStart(b, u, st);
      if (fx.fuse) {
        st.fuseT = (st.fuseT == null ? 2 : st.fuseT) - 1;
        if (st.fuseT <= 0) {
          delete u.statuses[id];
          K.say(b, `The fuse on ${u.name} goes off!`, 'good');
          K.damage(b, src, u, (st.pow || 10) * fx.fuse.power * (st.mult || 1), fx.fuse.type, { canCrit: false });
          K.fire(b, u, 'fuse_expires', { other: src }); if (src) K.fire(b, src, 'fuse_expires', { other: u });
          if (st.onBurst && src) X.run({ b, actor: src, power: 1, targets: [u] }, st.onBurst, [u]);
        }
      }
      if (!u.alive) return 'falls';
    }
    if (u.flags.saltBound) u.flags.saltBound = false;
    // moon & tide cycles
    if (K.hasStatus(u, 'moonphase')) {
      const st = u.statuses.moonphase; st.phase = ((st.phase || 0) + (u.flags.phaseLocked ? 0 : 1)) % 4;
      if (st.phase === 1) K.fire(b, u, 'phase_becomes_full', {});
    }
    if (u.flags.tide) u.flags.tideHigh = !u.flags.tideHigh;
    // hooks
    K.fire(b, u, 'start_of_turn', {});
    for (const a of K.allies(b, u, true)) K.fire(b, a, 'ally_start_of_turn', { other: u, target: u });
    for (const e of K.enemies(b, u)) K.fire(b, e, 'enemy_start_of_turn', { other: u, target: u });
    // zones
    for (const z of b.zones.slice()) B.zoneTick(b, z, u);
    if (!u.alive || b.over) return 'falls';
    // skip checks
    if (u.flags.loseNextTurn) { u.flags.loseNextTurn = false; return 'loses the turn'; }
    if (u.flags.skipNext) { u.flags.skipNext = false; return 'loses the turn'; }
    for (const id of Object.keys(u.statuses)) {
      const fx = EB.FX[id];
      if (fx && fx.skip) {
        const why = fx.skip(b, u, u.statuses[id]);
        if (why) { if (fx.consumeOnSkip) K.removeStatus(b, u, id); if (id === 'frozen') K.removeStatus(b, u, id); return why; }
      }
    }
    if (u.flags.loseActionChance && b.rng() < u.flags.loseActionChance.p) return 'hesitates';
    const forget = 0.05 * K.stacks(u, 'forgetting');
    if (forget && b.rng() < forget) return 'loses track of what it was doing';
    return null;
  };

  B.tick = function (b, u, id, times) {
    const st = u.statuses[id]; if (!st) return;
    const fx = EB.FX[id] || {};
    const td = fx.tickDmg; if (!td) return;
    const src = K.byId(b, st.src);
    for (let i = 0; i < (times || 1); i++) {
      if (!u.alive || !u.statuses[id]) return;
      let amt = td.per * st.stacks * (st.pow || 10) * K.DOT_SCALE;
      amt *= 1 + 0.06 * K.stacks(u, 'torment');
      if (src && src.flags.statusTickBonus && src.flags.statusTickBonus[id]) amt *= 1 + src.flags.statusTickBonus[id];
      K.damage(b, src, u, amt, td.type, { dot: true, canCrit: false });
      if (id === 'bleeding' && K.hasStatus(u, 'marked')) { const m = K.byId(b, u.statuses.marked.src); if (m) K.fire(b, m, 'marked_target_bleeds', { other: u }); }
    }
  };

  B.zoneTick = function (b, z, u) {
    const tpl = z.inline || K.tpl(z.template); if (!tpl) return;
    const caster = K.byId(b, z.src);
    if (!caster) return;
    const ally = !K.isEnemy(caster, u);
    const trig = tpl.trigger || 'start_of_turn';
    if (trig === 'ally_start_of_turn' && !ally) return;
    if (trig === 'enemy_start_of_turn' && ally) return;
    if (!B.inZone(b, z, u)) return;
    const ctx = { b, actor: caster, ab: { target: ally ? 'ally_single' : 'enemy_single' }, power: z.power || 1, targets: [u], inZone: true, zone: z, posthumous: true };
    // zone effects: hostile ones hit enemies, friendly ones help allies
    for (const eff of tpl.effects || []) {
      const hostile = X.isHostile(eff);
      if (hostile && ally && trig === 'start_of_turn') continue;
