      if (!hostile && !ally && trig === 'start_of_turn') continue;
      X.runOne(ctx, Object.assign({}, eff, { to: eff.to === 'self' ? undefined : eff.to }), [u]);
    }
  };
  // Zones that react when an enemy acts inside them. Returns true if the action is lost.
  B.actInZones = function (b, u) {
    let lost = false;
    for (const z of b.zones.slice()) {
      const tpl = z.inline || K.tpl(z.template); if (!tpl || tpl.trigger !== 'enemy_acts_in_zone') continue;
      const caster = K.byId(b, z.src); if (!caster || !K.isEnemy(caster, u) || !B.inZone(b, z, u)) continue;
      if (b.stats) b.stats.enemy_acts_in_zone = (b.stats.enemy_acts_in_zone || 0) + 1;
      if (z.template === 'zon_gilded_ground' && b.rng() < 0.25) { K.say(b, `${u.name} stops to grab coins.`, 'good'); lost = true; }
      if (z.template === 'zon_ice_field' && b.rng() < 0.2) { K.say(b, `${u.name} slips on the ice.`, 'good'); lost = true; }
      X.run({ b, actor: caster, power: z.power || 1, targets: [u], posthumous: true, inZone: true }, tpl.effects || [], [u]);
      if (!u.alive) return true;
    }
    return lost;
  };
  B.zoneHeld = function (b, tplId, side) { return b.zones.some((z) => z.template === tplId && (K.byId(b, z.src) || {}).side === side); };
  B.inZone = function (b, z, u) {
    const caster = K.byId(b, z.src);
    const row = z.row || 'everyone';
    const enemySide = caster ? K.opp(caster.side) : 'enemy';
    const allySide = caster ? caster.side : 'ally';
    switch (row) {
      case 'everyone': return true;
      case 'enemy_all': return u.side === enemySide;
      case 'enemy_vanguard': return u.side === enemySide && u.row === 'vanguard';
      case 'enemy_rear': return u.side === enemySide && u.row === 'rear';
      case 'ally_all': return u.side === allySide;
      case 'ally_vanguard': return u.side === allySide && u.row === 'vanguard';
      case 'ally_rear': return u.side === allySide && u.row === 'rear';
      default: return true;
    }
  };

  B.endTurn = function (b, u, delay) {
    // statuses
    for (const id of Object.keys(u.statuses)) {
      const st = u.statuses[id]; if (!st) continue;
      const d = K.statusDef(id); const fx = EB.FX[id] || {};
      if (fx.endTurn) fx.endTurn(b, u, st);
      if (!u.statuses[id]) continue;
      if (st.fresh) { st.fresh = false; if (d.decay && K.isBoon(id) === false && !st.noDecay) { /* fresh afflictions still decay */ } }
      if (d.decay && !(u.flags.preventDecay && u.flags.preventDecay[id]) && !(b.flags.preventDecay && b.flags.preventDecay[id])) K.reduceStatus(b, u, id, d.decay);
      if (!u.statuses[id]) continue;
      if (typeof st.dur === 'number' && !K.fresh(st, u)) {
        st.dur -= 1 + (K.hasStatus(u, 'unmade') && K.isBoon(id) ? 1 : 0);
        if (st.dur <= 0) {
          if (id === 'doom') B.doomFalls(b, u, st);
          K.removeStatus(b, u, id);
        }
      }
    }
    // mods
    u.mods = u.mods.filter((m) => { if (typeof m.dur === 'number' && !K.fresh(m, u)) { m.dur--; return m.dur > 0; } return true; });
    u.shields = u.shields.filter((s) => { if (typeof s.dur === 'number' && !K.fresh(s, u)) { s.dur--; return s.dur > 0; } return true; });
    // hooks with durations
    const fireLater = [];
    u.hooks = u.hooks.filter((h) => {
      if (h.trigger === 'after_2_turns' && h.wait != null) { h.wait--; if (h.wait <= 0) { fireLater.push(h); return false; } }
      if (typeof h.dur === 'number' && !K.fresh(h, u)) { h.dur--; if (h.dur <= 0) return false; }
      return true;
    });
    for (const h of fireLater) { if (b.stats) b.stats[h.trigger] = (b.stats[h.trigger] || 0) + 1; X.runHook(b, u, h, {}); }
    // one-turn flags
    for (const k of ['dodgeNext', 'defending']) if (u.flags[k + 'Ttl'] != null) { u.flags[k + 'Ttl']--; if (u.flags[k + 'Ttl'] <= 0) u.flags[k] = null; }
    B.tickFlags(b, u);
    K.fire(b, u, 'end_of_turn', {});
    if (u.alive) u.t += delay;
    // zones & battle flags tied to this unit's turns
    for (const z of b.zones) if (z.src === u.id && typeof z.dur === 'number' && !K.fresh(z, u)) z.dur--;
    b.zones = b.zones.filter((z) => z.dur === 'combat' || z.dur > 0);
    if (u.flags.charmed) u.flags.charmed--;
    // AI plans next turn
    if (u.alive && !u.isPlayer && EB.AI) EB.AI.plan(b, u);
    // vows tick
    if (u.vows) B.tickVows(b, u);
    K.checkEnd(b);
  };

  // timed flags: u.timed = [{key, ttl, clear}]
  B.setTimed = function (u, key, value, ttl) {
    u.flags[key] = value;
    u.timed = u.timed || [];
    u.timed = u.timed.filter((t) => t.key !== key);
    if (ttl !== 'combat' && ttl !== 'scene') u.timed.push({ key, ttl: ttl == null ? 2 : ttl, stamp: K.stamp() });
  };
  B.tickFlags = function (b, u) {
    if (u.timers && u.timers.length) {
      const due = [];
      u.timers = u.timers.filter((t) => { if (K.fresh(t, u)) return true; t.ttl--; if (t.ttl <= 0) { due.push(t); return false; } return true; });
      for (const t of due) t.fn();
    }
    if (!u.timed) return;
    u.timed = u.timed.filter((t) => { if (K.fresh(t, u)) return true; t.ttl--; if (t.ttl <= 0) { u.flags[t.key] = null; return false; } return true; });
  };

  B.doomFalls = function (b, u, st) {
    const src = K.byId(b, st.src);
    let amt = (st.pow || 10) * 0.35 * st.stacks * (st.mult || 1);
    if (K.hpPct(u) < 0.5) amt *= 1.5;
    K.say(b, `Doom falls on ${u.name}.`, 'good');
    K.damage(b, src, u, amt, 'spirit', { canCrit: false });
  };

  B.tickVows = function (b, u) {
    for (const v of u.vows.slice()) {
      if (typeof v.dur === 'number') {
        v.dur--;
        if (v.dur <= 0) EB.OPS._resolveVow(b, u, v, 'expire');
      }
    }
  };

  // ---------- targeting ----------
  B.defaultTargets = function (b, u, def) {
    switch (def.target) {
      case 'self': return [u];
      case 'ally_all': return K.allies(b, u, true);
      case 'enemy_all': return K.enemies(b, u);
      case 'everyone': return K.alive(b);
      case 'ally_vanguard': return K.rowOf(b, u.side, 'vanguard');
      case 'ally_rear': return K.rowOf(b, u.side, 'rear');
      case 'ally_row': return K.rowOf(b, u.side, u.row);
      case 'enemy_vanguard': { const v = K.rowOf(b, K.opp(u.side), 'vanguard'); return v.length ? v : K.rowOf(b, K.opp(u.side), 'rear'); }
      case 'none': return [];
      default: return [u];
    }
  };

  B.needsTarget = (def) => ['enemy_single', 'ally_single', 'ally_or_self', 'ally_or_enemy', 'enemy_row', 'familiar_single'].includes(def.target);

  // units that can legally be chosen as the primary target
  B.validTargets = function (b, u, def) {
    const melee = B.isMelee(def, u);
    const reachAny = u.flags.reachAny || (u.item && u.item.mods && u.item.mods.reach === 'any') || (u.form && u.form.mods && u.form.mods.reach === 'any') || (def.tags || []).includes('Reach') || u.next.some((n) => n.reach === 'any' && B.nextMatches(n, def)) || K.stat(u, 'reach') > 0 || u.flags.flying;
    const canPick = (x) => {
      if (!x.alive || x.flags.banished) return false;
      if (K.isEnemy(u, x)) {
        if (K.hasStatus(x, 'stealthed') && !u.flags.seeStealth) return false;
        if (x.flags.untargetableBy && x.flags.untargetableBy.some((t) => u.tags.has(t))) return false;
        if (x.flags.cannotTargetBy && x.flags.cannotTargetBy[u.id]) return false;
        if (melee && !reachAny && x.row === 'rear' && (K.rowOf(b, x.side, 'vanguard').length || B.zoneHeld(b, 'zon_iron_barricade', x.side))) return false;
        if (x.row === 'rear' && B.isRanged(def, u) && B.zoneHeld(b, 'zon_stone_wall', x.side) && K.rowOf(b, x.side, 'vanguard').length) return false;
        if (x.flags.phased && !B.isSpell(def)) return false;
      }
      return true;
    };
    let list;
    switch (def.target) {
      case 'enemy_single': case 'enemy_row': list = K.enemies(b, u).filter(canPick); break;
      case 'ally_single': list = K.allies(b, u, true).filter(canPick); if (list.length > 1) list = list.filter((x) => x !== u).concat([u]); break;
      case 'ally_or_self': list = K.allies(b, u, true).filter(canPick); break;
      case 'familiar_single': list = K.allies(b, u).filter((x) => x.kind === 'familiar' || x.kind === 'conjuration'); if (!list.length) list = [u]; break;
      case 'ally_or_enemy': list = K.alive(b).filter(canPick); break;
      default: list = [];
    }
    // taunt: must target taunter if able
    if (def.target === 'enemy_single' && K.hasStatus(u, 'taunted')) {
      const tt = K.byId(b, u.statuses.taunted.src);
      if (tt && list.includes(tt)) list = [tt];
    }
    if (def.target === 'enemy_single' && u.flags.duelWith) { const d = K.byId(b, u.flags.duelWith); if (d && d.alive && list.includes(d)) list = [d]; }
    return list;
  };

  // Turn the chosen primary into the full target list, applying confusion, decoys and riders.
  B.resolveTargets = function (b, u, def, primary) {
    let list;
    if (B.needsTarget(def)) {
      const valid = B.validTargets(b, u, def);
      if (!primary || !valid.includes(primary)) primary = valid[0] || null;
      if (!primary) return [];
      // confusion / duped / charm retargeting (enemy single only)
      if (def.target === 'enemy_single') {
        let p = 0;
        if (K.hasStatus(u, 'confused')) p += 0.3;
        p += 0.05 * K.stacks(u, 'duped');
        if (u.flags.randomRetarget) p += u.flags.randomRetarget;
        if (u.flags.charmed) p = 1;
        if (p > 0 && b.rng() < p) {
          const pool = u.flags.charmed ? K.allies(b, u) : K.alive(b).filter((x) => x !== u && !x.flags.banished);
          if (pool.length) { primary = b.rng.pick(pool); K.say(b, `${u.name} lashes out at the wrong target: ${primary.name}!`, u.side === 'enemy' ? 'good' : 'bad'); }
        }
        // decoys draw attacks from the beguiled
        const decoys = K.allies(b, primary).filter((x) => x.kind === 'decoy');
        if (decoys.length && K.isEnemy(u, primary)) {
          let dp = 0.06 * K.stacks(u, 'beguiled') + (primary.flags.decoyPull || 0) + 0.25;
          if (b.rng() < dp) { primary = b.rng.pick(decoys); K.say(b, `${u.name} strikes a decoy.`, 'good'); }
        }
        // redirect / intercept by protectors
        const guards = K.allies(b, primary).filter((x) => x.flags.interceptFor && (x.flags.interceptFor === primary.id || x.flags.interceptFor === 'all') && x.alive);
        if (guards.length && K.isEnemy(u, primary)) { const g = guards[0]; K.say(b, `${g.name} intercepts the attack meant for ${primary.name}.`); primary = g; }
        const red = primary.flags.redirectAttacks;
        if (red && K.isEnemy(u, primary) && (!red.filter || (red.filter === 'spells' && B.isSpell(def)))) {
          const to = K.byId(b, red.to);
          if (to && to.alive && to !== primary && (red.chance == null || b.rng() < red.chance)) { K.say(b, `${to.name} draws the attack away from ${primary.name}.`); primary = to; }
        }
        if (K.isEnemy(u, primary) && b.rng() < (primary.flags.redirectToDecoy || 0)) {
          const ds = K.allies(b, primary).filter((x) => x.kind === 'decoy'); if (ds.length) primary = b.rng.pick(ds);
        }
      }
      list = def.target === 'enemy_row' ? K.rowOf(b, primary.side, primary.row) : [primary];
    } else {
      list = B.defaultTargets(b, u, def);
      if (def.target === 'enemy_all' || def.target === 'enemy_vanguard') list = list.filter((x) => !(x.flags.untargetableBy && x.flags.untargetableBy.some((t) => u.tags.has(t))));
    }
    if (u.flags.allAlliesTarget && (def.target === 'ally_single' || def.target === 'ally_or_self')) list = K.allies(b, u, true);
    // riders
    if (def.extraTargets && list.length === 1) {
      const pool = (K.isEnemy(u, list[0]) ? K.enemies(b, u) : K.allies(b, u, true)).filter((x) => !list.includes(x) && !K.hasStatus(x, 'stealthed'));
      for (let i = 0; i < def.extraTargets && pool.length; i++) list.push(pool.splice(b.rng.int(pool.length), 1)[0]);
    }
    return list;
  };

  // ---------- costs ----------
  B.cost = function (u, def) {
    const c = Object.assign({}, def.cost || {});
    let mult = def.costMult || 1;
    mult *= 1 + K.stat(u, 'ability_cost');
    for (const n of u.next) if (n.costMult != null && B.nextMatches(n, def)) mult *= n.costMult;
    if (c.mana) {
      let m = c.mana * mult * (1 + K.stat(u, 'mana_cost') + (B.isSpell(def) ? K.stat(u, 'spell_cost') : 0));
      if (B.isSpell(def) && u.flags.freeSpell) m = 0;
      c.mana = Math.max(0, Math.round(m));
    }
    if (c.stamina) c.stamina = Math.max(0, Math.round(c.stamina * mult));
    if (c.health) c.health = Math.max(0, Math.round(c.health * mult));
    return c;
  };
  B.canAfford = function (u, c) {
    if (c.mana && u.mana < c.mana) return false;
    if (c.stamina && u.sta < c.stamina) return false;
    if (c.health && u.hp <= c.health * K.maxHp(u) / 100 && c.health > 0) return false;
    return true;
  };

  // Why can't this ability be used right now? (null if it can)
  B.blocked = function (b, u, slot) {
    const d = slot.def;
    if (d.passive) return 'Passive';
    if (d.type === 'familiar' && u.isPlayer && K.allies(b, u).some((x) => x.template === B.familiarTemplate(d))) return 'Already with you';
    if (slot.cd > 0) return `Cooldown ${slot.cd}`;
    if (slot.locked) return 'Locked';
    if (B.isSpell(d) && (K.hasStatus(u, 'silenced') || u.flags.noSpells)) return 'Silenced';
    if (d.type === 'special_attack' && K.hasStatus(u, 'paralyzed')) return 'Paralyzed';
    if (u.flags.forbidType && u.flags.forbidType === d.type) return 'Forbidden';
    if (u.flags.declared && !u.flags.declared.includes(slot.id)) return 'Not declared';
    const tags = d.tags || [];
    if (u.flags.movementBlocked && tags.includes('Movement')) return 'Can\'t move';
    if (u.flags.transformBlocked && tags.includes('Transform')) return 'Can\'t transform';
    if (slot.on) return null; // toggles can always be switched off
    if (!B.canAfford(u, B.cost(u, d))) return 'Not enough ' + (B.cost(u, d).mana > u.mana ? 'mana' : B.cost(u, d).stamina > u.sta ? 'stamina' : 'health');
    if (B.needsTarget(d) && !B.validTargets(b, u, d).length) return 'No target';
    const oath = EB.OPS._oathBlocks && EB.OPS._oathBlocks(b, u, slot);
    if (oath) return oath;
    return null;
  };
  B.familiarTemplate = function (d) { let t = null; K.mapEffects(d.effects, (e) => { if (e.op === 'summon' && !t) t = e.template; return e; }); return t; };

  B.nextMatches = (n, def) => !n.filterTags || n.filterTags.some((t) => (def.tags || []).includes(t));

  // ---------- executing an ability ----------
  // opts: {passive, free, choice, power, echo, quiet}
  B.execute = function (b, u, slot, targets, opts) {
    opts = opts || {};
    const def = slot.def;
    const ctx = {
      b, actor: u, ab: def, slot, targets: targets.slice(), power: (def.powerMult || 1) * (opts.power || 1),
      critBonus: def.critBonus || 0, missed: new Set(), choice: opts.choice, passive: !!opts.passive,
      consumed: 0, dispelled: 0, kills: 0, echo: !!opts.echo,
    };
    // toggle: aura lifetime
    if (def.toggle) { ctx.auraKey = u.id + ':' + slot.id; }
    // next-ability mods
    if (!opts.passive && !opts.echo) {
      const used = [];
      for (const n of u.next) if (B.nextMatches(n, def)) { used.push(n); B.applyNextMod(ctx, n); }
      u.next = u.next.filter((n) => !used.includes(n) || n.persist);
      ctx.usedNext = used;
    }
    if (u.flags.guaranteedCrit && !opts.passive && /damage/.test(JSON.stringify(def.effects))) { ctx.forceCrit = true; u.flags.guaranteedCrit = false; }
    if (u.flags.fromStealth || K.hasStatus(u, 'stealthed')) { ctx.fromStealth = true; u.flags.fromStealth = false; }
    if (u.flags.buffNextAttack && def.type === 'special_attack') { ctx.buffStatus = u.flags.buffNextAttack; u.flags.buffNextAttack = null; }
    const hostile = B.hostileTarget(def.target) || (def.target === 'ally_or_enemy' && targets[0] && K.isEnemy(u, targets[0]));
    const melee = B.isMelee(def, u), ranged = B.isRanged(def, u), spell = B.isSpell(def);
    ctx.melee = melee; ctx.ranged = ranged; ctx.spell = spell;
    // spell fizzles (and oath-imposed failure)
    if (!opts.passive && !opts.echo && (spell || u.flags.failChance)) {
      const fizz = (spell ? K.stat(u, 'spell_fail_chance') + 0.04 * K.stacks(u, 'deafened') + 0.04 * K.stacks(u, 'hushed') * 0 : 0) + (u.flags.failChance || 0);
      if (fizz > 0 && b.rng() < fizz) { K.say(b, `${u.name}'s ${def.name} fizzles.`, u.side === 'ally' ? 'bad' : 'good'); return ctx; }
    }
    // hexed backfire
    if (!opts.passive && EB.FX.hexed && K.hasStatus(u, 'hexed') && b.rng() < EB.FX.hexed.backfire(u) && hostile) {
      K.say(b, `The hex twists ${u.name}'s ${def.name} back on itself!`, u.side === 'ally' ? 'bad' : 'good');
      ctx.targets = [u];
    }
    if (u.flags.backfire && b.rng() < u.flags.backfire && hostile) { K.say(b, `${def.name} backfires!`); ctx.targets = [u]; }
    // attack resolution
    if (hostile && !opts.passive) {
      const final = [];
      for (const t of ctx.targets) {
        if (!K.isEnemy(u, t)) { final.push(t); continue; }
        const r = X.attackRoll(ctx, t, { melee, ranged, spell, cantMiss: ctx.cantMiss || def.cantMiss });
        if (r.hit) final.push(r.target); else ctx.missed.add(r.target.id);
      }
      ctx.targets = final;
      ctx.hitTargets = final.filter((t) => K.isEnemy(u, t));
    }
    // row spill (rider)
    if (def.rowSpill && ctx.targets.length === 1 && K.isEnemy(u, ctx.targets[0])) ctx.spill = K.rowOf(b, ctx.targets[0].side, ctx.targets[0].row).filter((x) => x !== ctx.targets[0]);
    // run effects
    const effects = def.effects || [];
    for (const eff of effects) {
      if (!u.alive && !opts.passive) break;
      const defaults = B.effectDefaults(b, u, def, eff, ctx);
      X.runOne(ctx, eff, defaults);
      if (ctx.spill && ctx.spill.length && X.isHostile(eff) && !eff.to) {
        const sub = X.sub(ctx, { power: ctx.power * def.rowSpill, missed: new Set() });
        X.runOne(sub, eff, ctx.spill.filter((x) => x.alive));
      }
    }
    if (ctx.buffStatus) for (const t of ctx.hitTargets || []) K.applyStatus(b, u, t, ctx.buffStatus.status, ctx.buffStatus.add || 1);
    // ally share rider
    if (def.allyShare && B.friendlyTarget(def.target) && def.target === 'self') {
      const pool = K.allies(b, u); if (pool.length) {
        const ally = X.lowest(pool);
        const sub = X.sub(ctx, { power: ctx.power * def.allyShare, targets: [ally] });
        for (const eff of effects) if (['modify_stat', 'apply_status', 'shield', 'heal'].includes(eff.op) && !X.isHostile(eff)) X.runOne(sub, Object.assign({}, eff, { to: undefined }), [ally]);
      }
    }
    if (hostile && ctx.hitTargets) for (const t of ctx.hitTargets) if (t.alive || true) X.onHitEvents(ctx, t, { melee, ranged, spell });
    return ctx;
  };

  B.effectDefaults = function (b, u, def, eff, ctx) {
    const tg = ctx.targets.filter((x) => x.alive);
    if (eff.to) return tg;
    if (FLOW_OPS.has(eff.op)) return tg.length ? tg : B.defaultTargets(b, u, def);
    const hostile = X.isHostile(eff);
    const targetsEnemies = B.hostileTarget(def.target) || (tg[0] && K.isEnemy(u, tg[0]));
    if (targetsEnemies && !hostile && SELFISH.has(eff.op)) return [u];
    if (!targetsEnemies && hostile) {
      if (tg.some((x) => K.isEnemy(u, x))) return tg.filter((x) => K.isEnemy(u, x));
      if (ctx.missed.size) return [];
      const en = K.enemies(b, u).filter((x) => !K.hasStatus(x, 'stealthed'));
      if (!en.length) return [];
      if (def.target === 'everyone') return en;
      const pick = (u.intent && u.intent.target && K.byId(b, u.intent.target)) || b.rng.pick(en);
      return [pick && pick.alive && K.isEnemy(u, pick) ? pick : en[0]];
    }
    return tg;
  };

  B.applyNextMod = function (ctx, n) {
    if (n.powerMult) ctx.power *= n.powerMult;
    if (n.critBonus) ctx.critBonus += n.critBonus;
    if (n.cantMiss) ctx.cantMiss = true;
    if (n.guaranteedCrit) ctx.forceCrit = true;
    if (n.ignoreResist) ctx.ignoreResist = n.ignoreResist === true ? 0.5 : n.ignoreResist;
    if (n.stacksAdd) ctx.stacksAdd = (ctx.stacksAdd || 0) + n.stacksAdd;
    if (n.stacksMult) ctx.stacksMult = (ctx.stacksMult || 1) * n.stacksMult;
    if (n.healingMult) ctx.healingMult = (ctx.healingMult || 1) * n.healingMult;
    if (n.status) ctx.extraStatus = { status: n.status, stacks: n.stacksAdd || 1 };
    if (n.addStatus) ctx.extraStatus = { status: n.addStatus, stacks: 1, self: true };
    if (n.bonusPerAffliction) ctx.bonusPerAffliction = n.bonusPerAffliction;
    if (n.chainJumps) ctx.chainJumps = n.chainJumps;
    if (n.consumeBonus) ctx.consumeBonus = n.consumeBonus;
    if (n.executeThresholdAdd) ctx.executeAdd = n.executeThresholdAdd;
    if (n.hitsAllWithSignature) ctx.hitsAllWithSignature = true;
    if (n.noCooldown) ctx.noCooldown = true;
    if (n.vsAsleepMult) ctx.vsAsleepMult = n.vsAsleepMult;
    if (n.reach === 'any') ctx.reachAny = true;
    if (n.tempo) ctx.tempoOverride = n.tempo === 'normal' ? 'standard' : n.tempo;
    if (n.target === 'ally_all') ctx.allAllies = true;
  };

  // Full player/AI action. Returns the context or {error}.
  B.useAbility = function (b, u, slot, primary, opts) {
    opts = opts || {};
    const def = slot.def;
    if (slot.on && def.toggle) {
      slot.on = false;
      K.removeAura(b, u.id + ':' + slot.id);
      K.say(b, `${u.name} lets ${def.name} fade.`, 'dim');
      B.finishAction(b, u, 'quick', { ab: def });
      return { toggledOff: true };
    }
    const why = B.blocked(b, u, slot);
    if (why && !opts.force) return { error: why };
    const cost = B.cost(u, def);
    if (B.isSpell(def) && u.flags.freeSpell) u.flags.freeSpell = false;
    if (cost.mana) u.mana -= cost.mana;
    if (cost.stamina) u.sta -= cost.stamina;
    if (cost.health) { K.loseHp(b, u, K.maxHp(u) * cost.health / 100, u); if (u.flags.atonementOnHurt) K.applyStatus(b, u, u, 'atonement', 1); }
    if (u.flags.oath && u.flags.oath.restriction === 'every_ability_costs_1_coffers') K.reduceStatus(b, u, 'coffers', 1);
    if (slot.misfire && b.rng() < slot.misfire) {
      K.log(b, { k: 'use', src: u.id, name: def.name, ab: def.id, side: u.side });
      K.say(b, `${def.name} misfires: core-fed and unseasoned, it slips out of your grip.`, u.side === 'ally' ? 'bad' : 'good');
      slot.cd = Math.max(slot.cd, Math.min(1, def.cooldown || 0)); slot.uses++;
      b.lastMisfire = def.id;
      B.finishAction(b, u, def.tempo || 'standard', { ab: def, slot });
      return { misfire: true };
    }
    let targets = B.resolveTargets(b, u, def, primary);
    if (!targets.length && B.needsTarget(def)) return { error: 'No target' };
    K.log(b, { k: 'use', src: u.id, name: def.name, ab: def.id, tgt: targets[0] && targets[0].id, spell: B.isSpell(def), side: u.side });
    u.lastAbility = { slot, def, targets: targets.map((t) => t.id) };
    b.lastAbilityBy = b.lastAbilityBy || {}; b.lastAbilityBy[u.id] = u.lastAbility;
    u.history.push(def.id);
    // events before effects (counterspells etc.)
    const sev = B.preAct(b, u, def, targets);
    if (sev.cancelled) { K.say(b, `${u.name}'s ${def.name} is cancelled!`, u.side === 'ally' ? 'bad' : 'good'); B.finishAction(b, u, def.tempo || 'standard', { ab: def, slot }); return { cancelled: true }; }
    if (u.flags.cancelNext) { u.flags.cancelNext = false; K.say(b, `${u.name}'s action falls apart.`, 'good'); B.finishAction(b, u, def.tempo || 'standard', { ab: def, slot }); return { cancelled: true }; }
    const ctx = B.execute(b, u, slot, targets, opts);
    // traps & contracts triggered by acting
    EB.OPS._onActTriggers && EB.OPS._onActTriggers(b, u, def, ctx);
    // cooldown
    if (!ctx.noCooldown) slot.cd = Math.max(0, Math.round((def.cooldown || 0) * (1 - K.stat(u, 'cd_reduction'))));
    if (def.toggle) slot.on = true;
    slot.uses++;
    if (slot.usesLeft != null) { slot.usesLeft--; if (slot.usesLeft <= 0) u.abilities = u.abilities.filter((s) => s !== slot); }
    // kill refund rider
    if (def.onKill === 'refund' && (u.killsThisAction || 0) > 0) { slot.cd = 0; u.mana += Math.round((cost.mana || 0) / 2); u.sta += Math.round((cost.stamina || 0) / 2); }
    // echoes
    if (!opts.echo) B.runEchoes(b, u, slot, ctx);
    // signature & combo bookkeeping
    if ((def.tags || []).includes('Song')) K.applyStatus(b, u, u, 'verse', 1);
    if (def.tempo === 'quick' && u.flags.momentumOnQuick) K.applyStatus(b, u, u, 'momentum', 1);
    if (def.tempo === 'slow' && K.hasStatus(u, 'momentum')) K.reduceStatus(b, u, 'momentum', Math.ceil(K.stacks(u, 'momentum') / 2));
    if (u.flags.oath) EB.OPS._oathAfter(b, u, slot, ctx);
    B.finishAction(b, u, ctx.tempoOverride || def.tempo || 'standard', { ab: def, slot, ctx });
    return ctx;
  };

  B.preAct = function (b, u, def, targets) {
    const sev = { other: u, ability: def, target: targets[0], spell: B.isSpell(def) };
    for (const e of K.enemies(b, u)) {
      if (B.isSpell(def)) K.fire(b, e, 'enemy_casts_spell', sev);
      if (!def.weapon) K.fire(b, e, 'enemy_uses_ability', sev);
      if (b.zones.some((z) => K.byId(b, z.src) === e && B.inZone(b, z, u))) K.fire(b, e, 'enemy_acts_in_zone', sev);
      for (const h of e.hooks.slice()) if (h.trigger === 'first_enemy_action' && !h.firstDone) { h.firstDone = true; if (b.stats) b.stats.first_enemy_action = (b.stats.first_enemy_action || 0) + 1; X.runHook(b, e, h, sev); }
    }
    if (!sev.cancelled && B.actInZones(b, u)) sev.cancelled = true;
    return sev;
  };

  B.runEchoes = function (b, u, slot, ctx) {
    const ready = u.echoes.filter((e) => B.nextMatches(e, slot.def));
    u.echoes = u.echoes.filter((e) => !ready.includes(e));
    for (const e of ready) {
      if (e.chance != null && b.rng() >= e.chance) continue;
      let tg = ctx.targets.filter((t) => t.alive);
      if (e.target === 'random_enemy') { const en = K.enemies(b, u); tg = en.length ? [b.rng.pick(en)] : []; }
      else if (e.target === 'same_row' && tg[0]) tg = K.rowOf(b, tg[0].side, tg[0].row);
      else if (e.target === 'lowest_ally') { const l = X.lowest(K.allies(b, u, true)); tg = l ? [l] : []; }
      else if (e.target === 'chain_all_charged') tg = K.enemies(b, u).filter((x) => K.hasStatus(x, 'charged'));
      if (!tg.length && B.needsTarget(slot.def)) continue;
      K.say(b, `${slot.def.name} echoes.`, 'dim');
      B.execute(b, u, slot, tg, { echo: true, power: e.power || 0.5 });
    }
  };

  B.finishAction = function (b, u, tempo, info) {
    u.actedThisTurn = true;
    u.killsThisAction = 0;
    const ev = { other: u, ability: info && info.ab };
    K.fire(b, u, 'you_use_ability', ev);
    for (const a of K.allies(b, u)) K.fire(b, a, 'ally_acts', ev);
    for (const e of K.enemies(b, u)) {
      K.fire(b, e, 'enemy_acts', ev);
    }
    if (EB.FX.hexed && K.hasStatus(u, 'hexed')) EB.FX.hexed.onAct(b, u, u.statuses.hexed);
    if (u.flags.extraActions > 0) {
      u.flags.extraActions--;
      K.say(b, `${u.name} acts again!`, u.side === 'ally' ? 'good' : 'bad');
      u.t = b.time; // acts again immediately (no end of turn)
      b.pendingExtra = u.id;
      return;
    }
    B.endTurn(b, u, B.delay(b, u, tempo));
  };

  // ---------- previews (no randomness, no side effects) ----------
  // Expected outcome of `def` used by u on t: {dmg, crit, hit, heal, shield, sts:[{id,chance}], notes:[]}
  B.preview = function (b, u, def, t) {
    const out = { dmg: 0, crit: 0, hit: null, heal: 0, shield: 0, sts: [], notes: [] };
    if (!t || !t.alive) return out;
    const hostile = K.isEnemy(u, t);
    const melee = B.isMelee(def, u), ranged = B.isRanged(def, u), spell = B.isSpell(def);
    let power = def.powerMult || 1, critB = def.critBonus || 0, cantMiss = !!def.cantMiss;
    for (const n of u.next) if (B.nextMatches(n, def)) { if (n.powerMult) power *= n.powerMult; if (n.critBonus) critB += n.critBonus; if (n.cantMiss) cantMiss = true; }
    if (hostile) {
      let p = 0.95 + K.stat(u, 'accuracy') - K.stat(t, 'evasion') - 0.05 * K.stacks(u, 'jinxed');
      if (melee && t.flags.flying && !u.flags.flying) p -= 0.15;
      if (t.form && t.form.mods && t.form.mods.untargetable_chance) p -= t.form.mods.untargetable_chance;
      if (spell) p += 0.1;
      out.hit = cantMiss ? 1 : U.clamp(p, 0.15, 1);
      if (K.stacks(t, 'evasive') > 0 && !cantMiss) out.notes.push('Evasive: will dodge');
      if (melee && t.flags.parryNext) out.notes.push('Ready to parry');
      if (t.flags.invulnerable) out.notes.push('Invulnerable');
      out.crit = K.critChance(b, u, t, { critBonus: critB });
    }
    const walk = (effs, depth) => {
      for (const e of effs || []) {
        if (!e || typeof e !== 'object') continue;
        if (e.op === 'conditional') { let ok = false; try { ok = X.test({ b, actor: u, ab: def, targets: [t] }, e.test, t); } catch (x) {} walk(ok ? e.then : e.else, depth + 1); continue; }
        if (e.to && !['target', 'enemy_single', 'ally_single', 'enemy_row', 'enemy_all', 'ally_all'].includes(e.to)) continue;
        if (e.op === 'damage' && hostile) {
          const base = (e.power || 0) * power * K.hit(u, e.scale, e.type) * (e.hits || 1);
          out.dmg += K.computeDamage(b, u, t, base, e.type || 'physical', { melee, ranged, spell, weapon: !!(e.weapon || def.weapon), ignoreArmor: !!e.ignoreArmor });
        } else if (e.op === 'heal' && !hostile) {
          out.heal += e.percent ? K.maxHp(t) * e.percent / 100 : (e.power || 0) * power * K.hit(u, e.scale || { spirit: 1 }, 'holy') * (1 + K.stat(u, 'healing_done') + K.stat(t, 'healing_received'));
        } else if (e.op === 'shield' && !hostile) {
          out.shield += (e.power || 0) * power * K.hit(u, e.scale || { resilience: 1 }, 'force') * 1.2;
        } else if (e.op === 'apply_status' && e.status) {
          const boon = K.isBoon(e.status);
          if (hostile === boon) continue;
          let c = e.chance == null ? 1 : e.chance;
          if (!boon) { if (K.isImmune(b, t, e.status)) c = 0; else c *= 1 - K.resistChance(b, t, e.status, u); if (K.hasStatus(t, 'warded')) { c = 0; out.notes.push('Warded'); } }
          out.sts.push({ id: e.status, stacks: e.stacks || 1, chance: c });
        }
      }
    };
    walk(def.effects, 0);
    out.dmg = Math.round(out.dmg); out.heal = Math.round(out.heal); out.shield = Math.round(out.shield);
    out.lethal = hostile && out.dmg >= t.hp + K.shieldTotal(t);
    return out;
  };

  // ---------- controller ----------
  // Run exactly one turn (or one extra action). Returns {unit, awaitPlayer, skipped} or null when over.
  B.stepOne = function (b) {
    if (b.over) return null;
    let u;
    if (b.pendingExtra) { u = K.byId(b, b.pendingExtra); b.pendingExtra = null; if (!u || !u.alive) return { skipped: true }; }
    else {
      const r = B.advance(b);
      if (!r) return null;
      if (r.skipped) return { unit: r.unit, skipped: true };
      u = r.unit;
    }
    if (u.isPlayer && !u.flags.charmed && !b.autoPlayer) { b.awaiting = u; return { unit: u, awaitPlayer: true }; }
    EB.AI.act(b, u);
    return { unit: u };
  };
  B.runUntilPlayer = function (b, maxSteps) {
    for (let i = 0; i < (maxSteps || 500); i++) {
      const r = B.stepOne(b);
      if (!r) return null;
      if (r.awaitPlayer) return r.unit;
    }
    return null;
  };
  B.finish = function (b) {
    for (const u of b.units) if (u.flags.onBattleEnd) u.flags.onBattleEnd(b);
  };

  // ---------- basic actions ----------
  B.WEAPON = { id: 'weapon', name: 'Attack', type: 'special_attack', target: 'enemy_single', tempo: 'standard', weapon: true, cost: {}, tags: ['Weapon'], effects: [{ op: 'damage', type: 'physical', power: 0.7, scale: { might: 0.7, swiftness: 0.3 }, weapon: true }] };
  B.weaponDef = function (u) {
    const d = U.deepCopy(B.WEAPON);
    if (u.weaponDef) Object.assign(d, U.deepCopy(u.weaponDef));
    const im = u.item && u.item.mods;
    if (im) {
      d.name = u.item.name;
      if (im.weapon_damage_type) d.effects[0].type = im.weapon_damage_type;
      if (im.hits) d.effects[0].hits = im.hits;
      if (im.tempo) d.tempo = im.tempo === 'normal' ? 'standard' : im.tempo;
      if (im.ignore_armor) d.effects[0].ignoreArmor = true;
      if (im.armor_pierce) d.effects[0].armorPierce = im.armor_pierce;
      if (im.row_splash) d.effects.push({ op: 'damage', type: d.effects[0].type, power: 0.7 * im.row_splash, to: 'enemy_adjacent', weapon: true });
      if (im.range || im.reach === 'any') d.tags = d.tags.concat(['Ranged']);
    }
    d.powerMult = 1 + 0.1 * (u.rank || 0);
    return d;
  };
  B.attack = function (b, u, primary) {
    const def = B.weaponDef(u);
    const slot = { def, id: 'weapon', cd: 0, uses: 0, rank: 0 };
    const tg = B.resolveTargets(b, u, def, primary);
    if (!tg.length) return { error: 'No target' };
    K.log(b, { k: 'use', src: u.id, name: def.name, ab: 'weapon', tgt: tg[0].id, side: u.side });
    const sev = B.preAct(b, u, def, tg);
    if (sev.cancelled || !u.alive) { if (u.alive) K.say(b, `${u.name}'s attack falls apart.`, u.side === 'ally' ? 'bad' : 'good'); B.finishAction(b, u, def.tempo, { ab: def }); return { cancelled: true }; }
    const ctx = B.execute(b, u, slot, tg, {});
    if (u.item && u.item.mods && u.item.mods.breaks_after_hits) { u.item.hits = (u.item.hits || 0) + 1; if (u.item.hits >= u.item.mods.breaks_after_hits) { K.say(b, `${u.item.name} shatters.`); u.item = null; } }
    EB.OPS._onActTriggers && EB.OPS._onActTriggers(b, u, def, ctx);
    B.finishAction(b, u, def.tempo, { ab: def });
    return ctx;
  };
  B.defend = function (b, u) {
    K.applyStatus(b, u, u, 'guard', 2);
    K.addMod(b, u, 'damage_reduction', 0.25, 1, u);
    K.restore(b, u, 'stamina', Math.round(K.maxSta(u) * 0.25));
    K.restore(b, u, 'mana', Math.round(K.maxMana(u) * 0.08));
    K.log(b, { k: 'use', src: u.id, name: 'Defend', ab: 'defend', side: u.side });
    B.finishAction(b, u, 'quick', { ab: { name: 'Defend' } });
    return {};
  };
  B.canMove = (b, u) => !(K.stacks(u, 'hooked') >= 3 || b.zones.some((z) => (z.template === 'zon_fissure' || z.template === 'zon_web_field') && K.byId(b, z.src) && K.isEnemy(K.byId(b, z.src), u) && B.inZone(b, z, u)) || K.hasStatus(u, 'rooted') || K.hasStatus(u, 'paralyzed') || K.stacks(u, 'chained') >= 3 || K.stacks(u, 'heavy') >= 4 || K.stacks(u, 'constricted') >= 4 || u.flags.movementBlocked || u.flags.noRowChange);
  B.swapRow = function (b, u) {
    if (!B.canMove(b, u)) return { error: 'Can\'t move' };
    B.moveRow(b, u, u.row === 'vanguard' ? 'rear' : 'vanguard');
    if (u.flags.rowChangeCost) u.sta = Math.max(0, u.sta - u.flags.rowChangeCost);
    if (u.flags.canSwapFree && u.flags.freeSwapTurn !== u.turns) { u.flags.freeSwapTurn = u.turns; K.say(b, `${u.name} shifts position (free).`, 'dim'); return { free: true }; }
    B.finishAction(b, u, 'quick', { ab: { name: 'Move' } });
    return {};
  };
  B.moveRow = function (b, u, row, forced) {
    if (u.row === row) return false;
    if (forced && (u.flags.immuneForcedMove || K.stacks(u, 'deeproot') >= 3 || u.boss && b.rng() < 0.5)) return false;
    const from = u.row;
    u.row = row;
    K.log(b, { k: 'move', tgt: u.id, row });
    if (K.hasStatus(u, 'deeproot')) K.removeStatus(b, u, 'deeproot');
    if (u.flags.onMoveDamage) { const s = K.byId(b, u.flags.onMoveDamage.src); if (s) K.damage(b, s, u, K.hit(s) * u.flags.onMoveDamage.power, 'physical', {}); }
    EB.OPS._onMoveTriggers && EB.OPS._onMoveTriggers(b, u, from);
    return true;
  };
  B.flee = function (b, u) {
    if (b.env.noFlee || u.flags.blockFlee || b.zones.some((z) => z.template === 'zon_arena')) return { error: 'You can\'t flee this fight.' };
    if (K.enemies(b, u).some((e) => K.stacks(e, 'quarry') >= 4 && false)) return { error: 'Can\'t flee' };
    if (K.stacks(u, 'constricted') >= 4) return { error: 'Can\'t flee' };
    const foes = K.enemies(b, u);
    const fastest = Math.max(...foes.map((e) => K.attr(e, 'swiftness')), 1);
    let p = 0.45 + (K.attr(u, 'swiftness') - fastest) * 0.03 + (u.flags.fleeBonus || 0);
    if (u.row === 'rear') p += 0.1;
    p = U.clamp(p, 0.1, 0.95);
    if (b.rng() < p) { b.over = true; b.winner = 'fled'; K.say(b, 'You get away.', 'sys'); K.log(b, { k: 'end', winner: 'fled' }); return { fled: true }; }
    K.say(b, 'You try to run, and fail.', 'bad');
    B.finishAction(b, u, 'standard', { ab: { name: 'Flee' } });
    return { fled: false };
  };
  B.wait = function (b, u) { K.say(b, `${u.name} waits.`, 'dim'); B.finishAction(b, u, 'quick', { ab: { name: 'Wait' } }); return {}; };

  // consumables: item = {name, effects, target}
  B.useItem = function (b, u, item, primary) {
    const def = Object.assign({ id: 'item_' + item.id, type: 'special_ability', tempo: 'quick', cost: {}, tags: ['Item'] }, item);
    const slot = { def, id: def.id, cd: 0, uses: 0, rank: 0 };
    const tg = B.resolveTargets(b, u, def, primary);
    K.log(b, { k: 'use', src: u.id, name: item.name, ab: def.id, side: u.side });
    B.execute(b, u, slot, tg, {});
    if (!u.flags.freeItem) B.finishAction(b, u, 'quick', { ab: def }); else { u.flags.freeItem = false; }
    return {};
  };

  // Command a familiar: set its next action (free, once per turn)
  B.command = function (b, u, fam, actionIdx, target) {
    if (u.flags.commandedThisTurn === u.turns) return { error: 'Already commanded this turn' };
    fam.order = { idx: actionIdx, target: target && target.id };
    u.flags.commandedThisTurn = u.turns;
    K.say(b, `${u.name} gives ${fam.name} an order.`, 'dim');
    return {};
  };

  // Generic dispatcher used by UI and AI
  B.act = function (b, u, a) {
    if (b.over) return { error: 'over' };
    switch (a.type) {
      case 'ability': {
        const slot = typeof a.slot === 'number' ? u.abilities[a.slot] : u.abilities.find((s) => s.id === a.slot) || (u.actionSlots || []).find((s) => s.id === a.slot);
        if (!slot) return { error: 'No such ability' };
        return B.useAbility(b, u, slot, a.target ? K.byId(b, a.target) : null, { choice: a.choice });
      }
      case 'attack': return B.attack(b, u, a.target ? K.byId(b, a.target) : null);
      case 'defend': return B.defend(b, u);
      case 'swap': return B.swapRow(b, u);
      case 'flee': return B.flee(b, u);
      case 'item': return B.useItem(b, u, a.item, a.target ? K.byId(b, a.target) : null);
      default: return B.wait(b, u);
    }
  };
})(globalThis.EB = globalThis.EB || {});

;
(function (EB) {
  'use strict';
  const U = EB.U, K = EB.K, X = EB.X;
  const O = (EB.OPS = EB.OPS || {});
  const B = () => EB.B;

  // ---------- helpers ----------
  const each = (tg, fn) => { for (const t of tg) if (t && t.alive) fn(t); };
  const ttl = (d, def) => (d == null ? def : d === 'aura' || d === 'scene' ? 'combat' : d);
  O._h = {
    timed(u, key, value, d) { B().setTimed(u, key, value, ttl(d, 2)); },
    after(u, turns, fn) { u.timers = u.timers || []; u.timers.push({ ttl: turns, fn, stamp: K.stamp() }); },
  };
  const H = O._h;

  O._dmgOpts = function (ctx, eff, t, nTargets) {
    const a = ctx.actor, ab = ctx.ab || {};
    return {
      melee: ctx.melee, ranged: ctx.ranged, spell: ctx.spell,
      weapon: !!(eff.weapon || ab.weapon), aoe: nTargets > 1,
      canCrit: eff.canCrit !== false, ignoreArmor: !!eff.ignoreArmor, armorPierce: eff.armorPierce || 0,
      critBonus: (ctx.critBonus || 0) + (ctx.fromStealth ? 1 : 0), forceCrit: !!ctx.forceCrit,
      ignoreResist: ctx.ignoreResist || 0, dot: !!eff.dot,
    };
  };
  // Base damage for a damage effect against one target
  O._base = function (ctx, eff, t) {
    const a = ctx.actor;
    let p = (eff.power || 0) * (ctx.power || 1);
    if (ctx.vsAsleepMult && K.hasStatus(t, 'asleep')) p *= ctx.vsAsleepMult;
    if (ctx.bonusPerAffliction) p *= 1 + Math.min(0.5, ctx.bonusPerAffliction * K.afflictions(t).length);
    return p * K.hit(a, eff.scale, eff.type);
  };

  O.damage = function (ctx, eff, tg) {
    const b = ctx.b, a = ctx.actor;
    let list = tg;
    if (ctx.hitsAllWithSignature && list.length === 1) {
      const sig = (a.signatureStatuses || [])[0];
      if (sig) list = list.concat(K.enemies(b, a).filter((x) => x !== list[0] && K.hasStatus(x, sig)));
    }
    const hits = eff.hits || 1;
    const type = eff.type || 'physical';
    each(list, (t) => {
      for (let i = 0; i < hits && t.alive; i++) {
        const o = O._dmgOpts(ctx, eff, t, list.length);
        if (i > 0) o.forceCrit = false;
        K.damage(b, a, t, O._base(ctx, eff, t), type, o);
      }
      if (ctx.extraStatus && t.alive && !ctx.extraStatus.self) K.applyStatus(b, a, t, ctx.extraStatus.status, ctx.extraStatus.stacks);
      if (t.alive) {
        const sc = K.stat(a, 'stagger_chance'); if (sc > 0 && b.rng() < sc) K.applyStatus(b, a, t, 'staggered', 1);
        const st = K.stat(a, 'stun_chance'); if (st > 0 && b.rng() < st) K.applyStatus(b, a, t, 'stunned', 1, { dur: 1 });
      }
    });
    if (ctx.extraStatus && ctx.extraStatus.self) { K.applyStatus(b, a, a, ctx.extraStatus.status, ctx.extraStatus.stacks); ctx.extraStatus = null; }
    if (ctx.chainJumps) { O.chain(ctx, { jumps: ctx.chainJumps, power: (eff.power || 0.5) * 0.5 }, list); ctx.chainJumps = 0; }
  };

  O.heal = function (ctx, eff, tg) {
    const b = ctx.b, a = ctx.actor;
    each(tg, (t) => {
      if (t.flags.isolated && t !== a && !K.isEnemy(a, t)) return;
      let amt;
      if (eff.percent) amt = K.maxHp(t) * eff.percent / 100;
      else amt = (eff.power || 0) * (ctx.power || 1) * K.hit(a, eff.scale || { spirit: 1 }, 'holy');
      if (ctx.healingMult) amt *= ctx.healingMult;
      if (a.flags.critHeal && b.rng() < a.flags.critHeal) { amt *= 1.5; K.log(b, { k: 'text', msg: 'Critical heal!', cls: 'good' }); }
      if (t.flags.oath && t.flags.oath.restriction === 'never_heal_above_75_percent' && t === a) amt = Math.min(amt, Math.max(0, K.maxHp(t) * 0.75 - t.hp));
      K.heal(b, a, t, amt, { overhealToShield: eff.overhealToShield });
      if (t === a) a.flags.healedSelf = true;
      if (a.flags.shareHealing && !ctx.sharing) for (const x of K.allies(b, a, true)) if (x !== t) K.heal(b, a, x, amt * a.flags.shareHealing);
    });
  };

  O.shield = function (ctx, eff, tg) {
    const b = ctx.b, a = ctx.actor;
    each(tg, (t) => {
      if (t.flags.isolated && t !== a) return;
      const amt = (eff.power || 0) * (ctx.power || 1) * K.hit(a, eff.scale || { resilience: 1 }, 'force') * 1.2;
      K.addShield(b, t, amt, ttl(eff.duration, 2), a);
      if (t === a) a.flags.shieldedSelf = true;
    });
  };

  O.apply_status = function (ctx, eff, tg) {
    const b = ctx.b, a = ctx.actor;
    let stacks = eff.stacks == null ? 1 : eff.stacks;
    if (ctx.stacksAdd && !K.isBoon(eff.status)) stacks += ctx.stacksAdd;
    if (ctx.stacksMult) stacks *= ctx.stacksMult;
    each(tg, (t) => {
      if (t.flags.isolated && t !== a && !K.isEnemy(a, t) && K.isBoon(eff.status)) return;
      K.applyStatus(b, a, t, eff.status, stacks, { dur: eff.duration });
    });
  };
  O.give_status = O.apply_status;

  O.consume_status = function (ctx, eff, tg) {
    const b = ctx.b, a = ctx.actor;
    let total = 0;
    each(tg, (t) => {
      const st = t.statuses[eff.status];
      if (!st) return;
      let n = Math.floor(st.stacks);
      if (eff.max) n = Math.min(n, eff.max);
      if (n <= 0) return;
      K.reduceStatus(b, t, eff.status, n);
      total += n;
      if (eff.status === 'inscribed') K.fire(b, a, 'you_detonate_glyphs', { other: t, stacks: n });
      if (eff.status === 'bloom' || eff.status === 'burgeoning') { if (K.stacks(a, 'burgeoning') >= 10 || a.flags.matured) K.applyStatus(b, a, a, 'burgeoning', Math.floor(n / 2), { noEvents: true }); }
      const mult = ctx.consumeBonus ? 1 + ctx.consumeBonus : 1;
      const sub = X.sub(ctx, { power: ctx.power * mult, consumedFrom: t });
      for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) {
        for (const pe of eff.perStack || []) {
          const def = X.isHostile(pe) ? [t].filter((x) => x.alive && K.isEnemy(a, x)) : (pe.to ? [t] : [a]);
          const dft = X.isHostile(pe) && !K.isEnemy(a, t) ? K.enemies(b, a).slice(0, 1) : def;
          X.runOne(sub, pe, dft.length ? dft : [t]);
        }
      }
    });
    ctx.consumed = (ctx.consumed || 0) + total;
    if (ctx.addFraction) { for (const k in ctx.addFraction) { const f = ctx.addFraction[k]; const whole = Math.floor(f.acc / f.per); if (whole) K.applyStatus(b, a, f.to, k, whole); } ctx.addFraction = null; }
  };

  O.add_stack_fraction = function (ctx, eff, tg) {
    // Accumulates within a consume: 1 stack per `per` consumed
    ctx.addFraction = ctx.addFraction || {};
    const f = (ctx.addFraction[eff.status] = ctx.addFraction[eff.status] || { acc: 0, per: eff.per || 1, to: tg[0] || ctx.actor });
    f.acc += 1;
    if (!ctx.consumedFrom) { // not inside a consume loop: apply directly
      const whole = Math.floor(f.acc / f.per); if (whole) { K.applyStatus(ctx.b, ctx.actor, f.to, eff.status, whole, { dur: eff.duration }); f.acc -= whole * f.per; }
    }
  };

  O.spread_status = function (ctx, eff, tg, defaults) {
    const b = ctx.b, a = ctx.actor;
    const src = (defaults || ctx.targets || []).find((x) => x.alive && K.hasStatus(x, eff.status)) || K.enemies(b, a).find((x) => K.hasStatus(x, eff.status));
    if (!src) return;
    const have = K.stacks(src, eff.status);
    let n = eff.stacks === 'all' ? have : eff.stacks === 'half' ? Math.ceil(have / 2) : (eff.stacks || 1);
    let dest = tg.filter((x) => x !== src);
    if (eff.to === 'adjacent' || eff.to === 'enemy_adjacent' || !eff.to) dest = K.rowOf(b, src.side, src.row).filter((x) => x !== src);
    if (!dest.length) dest = K.allies(b, src).filter((x) => x !== src);
    if (eff.to === 'random_enemy' && dest.length) dest = [b.rng.pick(dest)];
    each(dest, (t) => K.applyStatus(b, a, t, eff.status, n));
  };

  O.cleanse = function (ctx, eff, tg) { each(tg, (t) => X.cleanse(ctx.b, t, eff.category || 'any', eff.count || 1)); };
  O.dispel = function (ctx, eff, tg) {
    const b = ctx.b;
    let list = tg;
    if (eff.to === 'enemy_strongest') list = [X.strongest(K.enemies(b, ctx.actor))].filter(Boolean);
    each(list, (t) => { ctx.dispelled = (ctx.dispelled || 0) + X.dispel(b, t, eff.count || 1); });
  };
  O.steal_boon = function (ctx, eff, tg) {
    const b = ctx.b, a = ctx.actor;
    let from = tg;
    if (eff.from) from = X.resolve(ctx, eff.from, tg, eff);
    each(from, (t) => {
      if (!K.isEnemy(a, t)) return;
      for (let i = 0; i < (eff.count || 1); i++) {
        const bn = K.boons(t).filter((id) => id !== 'moonphase')[0];
        if (!bn) break;
        const st = t.statuses[bn];
        K.removeStatus(b, t, bn);
        K.applyStatus(b, a, a, bn, st.stacks, { dur: st.dur });
        K.say(b, `${a.name} steals ${K.statusDef(bn).name} from ${t.name}.`, a.side === 'ally' ? 'good' : 'bad');
      }
    });
  };

  O.modify_stat = function (ctx, eff, tg) {
    const b = ctx.b, a = ctx.actor;
    let amt = eff.amount || 0;
    // aura strength and rank scaling apply to buffs/debuffs
    amt *= ctx.power || 1;
    let dur = eff.duration;
    let aura = null;
    if (dur === 'aura') { aura = ctx.auraKey || (a.id + ':aura'); dur = 'combat'; }
    if (dur == null) dur = ctx.passive ? 'combat' : 2;
    let list = tg;
    if (eff.to === 'linked' && ctx.linked) list = [ctx.linked];
    if (ctx.ab && ctx.ab.auraRows === 'all' && eff.to === 'ally_vanguard') list = K.allies(b, a, true);
    each(list, (t) => {
      if (t.flags.isolated && t !== a && !K.isEnemy(a, t)) return;
      let vs = null;
      if (eff.stat === 'damage_vs_target') vs = (ctx.targets || []).find((x) => K.isEnemy(a, x)) || (ctx.event && ctx.event.other);
      if (eff.stat === 'evasion_vs_actor') vs = ctx.event && ctx.event.other;
      if (eff.stat === 'all_attributes' || K.ATTRS.includes(eff.stat) || /resist|damage|reduction|evasion|accuracy|crit|speed/.test(eff.stat)) {}
      K.addMod(b, t, eff.stat, amt, dur, a, { aura, vs: vs ? vs.id : null });
      if (eff.stat === 'loot') b.lootBonus = (b.lootBonus || 0) + amt;
    });
  };

  O.resource = function (ctx, eff, tg) {
    const b = ctx.b;
    each(tg, (t) => {
      const max = eff.resource === 'health' ? K.maxHp(t) : eff.resource === 'mana' ? K.maxMana(t) : K.maxSta(t);
      const amt = Math.round(max * (eff.percent || 0) / 100);
      if (eff.resource === 'health') { if (amt >= 0) K.heal(b, ctx.actor, t, amt); else K.loseHp(b, t, -amt, ctx.actor); }
      else {
        const got = K.restore(b, t, eff.resource, amt);
        if (got) K.log(b, { k: 'res', tgt: t.id, res: eff.resource, amt: got });
      }
    });
  };

  O.timeline = function (ctx, eff, tg) {
    const b = ctx.b, a = ctx.actor;
    each(tg, (t) => {
      const amt = eff.amount || 30;
      if (eff.kind === 'delay') {
        if (t.boss) { t.t += Math.round(amt / 2); } else t.t += amt;
        K.log(b, { k: 'delay', tgt: t.id, amt });
      } else if (eff.kind === 'advance') {
        t.t = Math.max(b.time + 0.01, t.t - amt);
        K.log(b, { k: 'advance', tgt: t.id, amt });
      } else if (eff.kind === 'extra_turn') {
        if (t === a) a.flags.extraActions = (a.flags.extraActions || 0) + 1;
        else { t.t = b.time + 0.01; K.say(b, `${t.name} moves to the front of the line.`, 'dim'); }
      }
    });
  };

  // ---------- summons, zones, forms, items ----------
  O.summon = function (ctx, eff, tg) {
    const b = ctx.b, a = ctx.actor;
    const tpl = K.tpl(eff.template);
    if (!tpl) return;
    const n = eff.count || 1;
    const ab = ctx.ab || {};
    for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) {
      const isFam = tpl.kind === 'familiar';
      if (isFam && K.allies(b, a).some((x) => x.template === tpl.id && x.owner === a.id)) { K.say(b, `${tpl.name} is already at your side.`, 'dim'); return; }
      const mine = K.allies(b, a).filter((x) => x.owner === a.id && x.kind === 'conjuration');
      if (!isFam && mine.length >= 4) { const old = mine[0]; old.alive = false; K.log(b, { k: 'fade', tgt: old.id }); }
      const st = tpl.stats || {};
      const mult = (ab.summonStats || 1);
      const unit = K.makeUnit(b, {
        name: tpl.name + (n > 1 ? ' ' + (i + 1) : ''), side: a.side, row: st.row || (isFam ? 'vanguard' : 'vanguard'),
        kind: isFam ? 'familiar' : 'conjuration', owner: a.id, attrs: Object.assign({}, a.base), rank: a.rank,
        maxHp: Math.max(5, Math.round(K.maxHp(a) * (st.health || 0.5) * (isFam ? 0.9 : 0.6) * mult)),
        powerScale: (st.power || 0.4) * 2 * mult * (a.powerScale || 1), speedScale: st.speed || 1,
        duration: isFam ? null : (eff.duration || 3), template: tpl.id,
        tags: [].concat(st.construct ? ['construct'] : [], st.incorporeal ? ['incorporeal'] : [], isFam ? ['familiar'] : ['summon']),
        flying: !!st.flying,
      });
      unit.flags.summoned = true;
      if (st.grows) unit.flags.grows = true;
      // actions: familiars learn 2 at Iron, +1 per familiar rider
      const acts = tpl.actions || [];
      const count = isFam ? Math.min(acts.length, 2 + (ab.familiarUnlock || 0)) : acts.length;
      unit.actionSlots = acts.slice(0, count).map((ac, j) => ({
        id: tpl.id + '#' + j, cd: 0, uses: 0, rank: a.rank,
        def: Object.assign({ id: tpl.id + '#' + j, name: ac.name, type: /damage/.test(JSON.stringify(ac.effects)) ? 'special_attack' : 'special_ability', tempo: 'standard', cost: {}, tags: [tpl.name, 'Summon'], cooldown: ac.cooldown || 0, powerMult: 1 + 0.15 * (a.rank || 0) }, U.deepCopy(ac)),
      }));
      unit.abilities = unit.actionSlots;
      unit.upkeep = tpl.upkeep || 0;
      unit.stance = 'aggressive';
      B().addUnit(b, unit);
      unit.t = b.time + B().delay(b, unit, 'standard') * 0.5;
      K.log(b, { k: 'summon', tgt: unit.id, src: a.id, name: unit.name });
      if (!ctx.quiet) K.say(b, `${a.name} summons ${unit.name}.`, a.side === 'ally' ? 'good' : 'bad');
      if (EB.AI) EB.AI.plan(b, unit);
    }
  };
  O.chance_summon = function (ctx, eff, tg) { if (ctx.b.rng() < (eff.chance || 0.5)) O.summon(ctx, eff, tg); };
  O.raise_spirit = function (ctx, eff, tg) {
    const c = ctx.b.corpses.pop();
    if (!c) { K.say(ctx.b, 'There are no fallen to raise.', 'dim'); return; }
    O.summon(ctx, { template: eff.template, count: 1, duration: eff.duration || 3 }, tg);
  };

  O.zone = function (ctx, eff, tg) {
    const b = ctx.b, a = ctx.actor;
    const tpl = K.tpl(eff.template);
    if (!tpl) return;
    b.zones = b.zones.filter((z) => !(z.src === a.id && z.template === eff.template));
    const z = { stamp: K.stamp(), id: U.uid('z'), template: eff.template, name: tpl.name, row: eff.row || 'everyone', dur: ttl(eff.duration, 3), src: a.id, power: ctx.power || 1, aura: ctx.auraKey || null };
    if (ctx.auraKey) z.dur = 'combat';
    b.zones.push(z);
    K.log(b, { k: 'zone', name: tpl.name, src: a.id, row: z.row });
    K.say(b, `${tpl.name} spreads across the field.`, 'dim');
    if (eff.template === 'zon_banner' || /banner/.test(eff.template)) a.flags.bannerPlanted = true;
  };

  O._normMods = function (mods) {
    const o = {};
    for (const k in mods || {}) {
      const v = mods[k];
      const sk = U.snake(k);
      o[sk] = v;
    }
    // aliases
    if (o.regen != null) o.regeneration = (o.regeneration || 0) + o.regen;
    if (o.flying) o.flying = true;
    if (o.weapon_damage_type) o.weapon_damage_type = o.weapon_damage_type;
    if (o.healing_done == null && o.healing != null) o.healing_done = o.healing;
    if (o.all_mask_bonuses) o.mask_all = true;
    if (o.vs_asleep) o.vs_asleep = o.vs_asleep;
    return o;
  };

  O.transform = function (ctx, eff, tg) {
    const b = ctx.b, a = ctx.actor;
    const tpl = K.tpl(eff.form);
    if (!tpl) return;
    if (a.flags.transformBlocked) { K.say(b, `${a.name} can't change shape right now.`); return; }
    O._endForm(b, a);
    const mods = O._normMods(tpl.mods);
    a.form = { id: tpl.id, name: tpl.name, mods, key: a.id + ':form:' + tpl.id };
    if (mods.flying) a.flags.flying = true;
    if (mods.row) B().moveRow(b, a, mods.row);
    if (mods.construct) a.tags.add('construct');
    if (mods.incorporeal) a.tags.add('incorporeal');
    // form effects (usually reactions) last while the form does
    const sub = X.sub(ctx, { formHook: true, hookTag: a.form.key, targets: [a], ab: Object.assign({}, ctx.ab, { target: 'self' }) });
    X.run(sub, tpl.effects || [], [a]);
    const acts = (tpl.actions || []).slice(0, 1 + (ctx.ab && ctx.ab.formUnlock || 0) + (tpl.actions && tpl.actions.length > 1 && (a.rank || 0) >= 2 ? 1 : 0));
    a.formSlots = acts.map((ac, j) => ({ id: tpl.id + '#' + j, cd: 0, uses: 0, rank: a.rank, form: true, def: Object.assign({ id: tpl.id + '#' + j, name: ac.name, type: 'special_attack', tempo: 'standard', cost: {}, tags: [tpl.name, 'Form'], cooldown: ac.cooldown || 0, powerMult: K.RANK_POWER[a.rank || 0] }, U.deepCopy(ac)) }));
    for (const s of a.formSlots) a.abilities.push(s);
    a.hp = Math.min(a.hp, K.maxHp(a));
    const d = ttl(eff.duration, 3);
    a.form.dur = d;
    if (typeof d === 'number') H.after(a, d, () => O._endForm(b, a));
    K.log(b, { k: 'form', tgt: a.id, name: tpl.name });
    K.say(b, `${a.name} becomes ${tpl.name}.`, a.side === 'ally' ? 'good' : 'bad');
  };
  O._endForm = function (b, u) {
    if (!u.form) return;
    const key = u.form.key;
    u.hooks = u.hooks.filter((h) => h.tag !== key);
    u.abilities = u.abilities.filter((s) => !s.form);
    if (u.form.mods.flying && !u.flags.flyingTimed) u.flags.flying = false;
    if (u.form.mods.construct && u.kind !== 'conjuration') u.tags.delete('construct');
    if (u.form.mods.incorporeal) u.tags.delete('incorporeal');
    K.say(b, `${u.name} returns to normal.`, 'dim');
    u.form = null;
    u.hp = Math.min(u.hp, K.maxHp(u));
  };

  O.equip_conjured = function (ctx, eff, tg) {
    const b = ctx.b;
    const tpl = K.tpl(eff.item);
    if (!tpl) return;
    const holder = tg.find((x) => !K.isEnemy(ctx.actor, x)) || ctx.actor;
    const prev = holder.item;
    holder.item = { id: tpl.id, name: tpl.name, mods: O._normMods(tpl.mods), onHit: tpl.onHit || [], conjured: true, src: ctx.actor.id };
    if (holder.item.mods.reach === 'any') holder.flags.reachAny = true;
    K.log(b, { k: 'item', tgt: holder.id, name: tpl.name });
    K.say(b, `${holder.name} takes up ${tpl.name}.`, 'dim');
    const d = ttl(eff.duration, 'combat');
    if (typeof d === 'number') H.after(holder, d, () => { if (holder.item && holder.item.id === tpl.id) { holder.item = prev && !prev.conjured ? prev : null; holder.flags.reachAny = false; K.say(b, `${tpl.name} dissolves.`, 'dim'); } });
  };
  O.destroy_conjured_item = function (ctx, eff, tg) {
    each(tg, (t) => { if (t.item && t.item.conjured) { K.say(ctx.b, `${t.name}'s ${t.item.name} shatters.`); t.item = null; } });
  };

  // ---------- reactions & control flow ----------
  O.reaction = function (ctx, eff, tg) { X.installReaction(ctx, eff, tg); };

  O.conditional = function (ctx, eff, tg) {
    const b = ctx.b;
    const perTarget = /^target_/.test(eff.test || '');
    const runBranch = (ok, defaults) => {
      const br = ok ? eff.then : eff.else;
      if (!br) return;
      const sub = ok && ctx.ab && ctx.ab.condMult > 1 ? X.sub(ctx, { power: ctx.power * ctx.ab.condMult }) : ctx;
      X.run(sub, br, defaults);
    };
    if (perTarget) {
      const list = tg.length ? tg : (ctx.targets || []);
      if (!list.length) { runBranch(X.test(ctx, eff.test, null), tg); return; }
      for (const t of list) if (t.alive) runBranch(X.test(ctx, eff.test, t), [t]);
    } else runBranch(X.test(ctx, eff.test, tg[0]), tg);
  };

  O.option = function (ctx, eff, tg) { X.run(ctx, eff.effects, tg); };
  O.choose_one = function (ctx, eff, tg) {
    const opts = eff.options || [];
    if (!opts.length) return;
    let i = ctx.choice;
    if (i == null || !opts[i]) i = X.pickOption(ctx, opts);
    K.say(ctx.b, `${ctx.actor.name} chooses: ${opts[i].label || 'option ' + (i + 1)}.`, 'dim');
    X.run(ctx, opts[i].effects || [opts[i]], tg);
  };
  O.choose_vow = O.choose_one;
  X.pickOption = function (ctx, opts) {
    const a = ctx.actor;
    const s = opts.map((o) => {
      const j = JSON.stringify(o);
      let v = ctx.b.rng() * 0.5;
      if (K.hpPct(a) < 0.5 && /heal|shield|cleanse|Protect|Endure|Restore|Renew/.test(j)) v += 2;
      if (/damage|Slay|Destroy/.test(j)) v += 1;
      return v;
    });
    return s.indexOf(Math.max(...s));
  };
  O.random_effect = function (ctx, eff, tg) { const p = eff.pool || []; if (p.length) X.runOne(ctx, ctx.b.rng.pick(p), tg); };
  O.random_of = function (ctx, eff, tg) { const p = eff.options || []; if (p.length) X.runOne(ctx, ctx.b.rng.pick(p), tg); };

  O.delayed_effect = function (ctx, eff, tg) {
    const a = ctx.actor;
    K.addHook(ctx.b, a, { trigger: 'after_2_turns', wait: eff.turns || 2, effects: eff.effects, caster: a.id, ab: ctx.ab, power: ctx.power, fixedTargets: tg.map((t) => t.id), dur: 'combat' });
  };
  O.per_enemy_with = function (ctx, eff, tg) {
    const n = K.enemies(ctx.b, ctx.actor).filter((x) => K.hasStatus(x, eff.status)).length;
    for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) X.run(ctx, eff.effects, tg);
  };
  O.per_consumed = function (ctx, eff, tg) { for (let i = 0; i < (ctx.consumed || 0); i++) X.run(ctx, eff.effects, tg); };
  O.per_dispelled = function (ctx, eff, tg) { for (let i = 0; i < (ctx.dispelled || 0); i++) X.run(ctx, eff.effects, tg); };
  O.per_stack = function (ctx, eff, tg) {
    each(tg, (t) => { const n = Math.floor(K.stacks(t, eff.status)); for (let i = 0; i < n && t.alive; i++) X.run(ctx, eff.effects, [t]); });
  };
  O.consume_corpses = function (ctx, eff, tg) {
    const b = ctx.b;
    const n = b.corpses.length;
    if (!n) { K.say(b, 'No fallen to consume.', 'dim'); return; }
    b.corpses = [];
    K.say(b, `${ctx.actor.name} consumes ${n} of the fallen.`, 'dim');
    for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) X.run(ctx, eff.per || [], [ctx.actor]);
  };

  O.next_ability_mod = function (ctx, eff, tg) {
    const a = ctx.actor;
    const m = Object.assign({}, eff); delete m.op; delete m.to;
    const recips = tg.filter((t) => !K.isEnemy(a, t));
    for (const r of recips.length ? recips : [a]) r.next.push(Object.assign({}, m));
    K.log(ctx.b, { k: 'text', msg: `${a.name} readies something special.`, cls: 'dim' });
  };
  O.echo_next = function (ctx, eff, tg) {
    const m = Object.assign({}, eff); delete m.op;
    ctx.actor.echoes.push(m);
  };
  O.familiar_copies_next = function (ctx, eff) { ctx.actor.echoes.push({ power: eff.power || 0.5, target: 'same', byFamiliar: true }); };

  O.link = function (ctx, eff, tg) {
    const b = ctx.b, a = ctx.actor;
    const partner = tg.find((t) => t !== a) || tg[0];
    if (!partner) return;
    a.flags.links = (a.flags.links || []).concat([partner.id]);
    partner.flags.linkedTo = (partner.flags.linkedTo || []).concat([a.id]);
    K.say(b, `${a.name} links with ${partner.name}.`, 'dim');
    K.log(b, { k: 'link', src: a.id, tgt: partner.id });
    const sub = X.sub(ctx, { linked: partner, passive: eff.duration === 'combat' || ctx.passive });
    X.run(sub, eff.effects, [partner]);
  };
  O.link_all_allies = function (ctx, eff, tg) { for (const al of K.allies(ctx.b, ctx.actor)) O.link(ctx, { effects: eff.effects, duration: 'combat' }, [al]); };

  // ---------- reveal ----------
  O.reveal = function (ctx, eff, tg) {
    const b = ctx.b, a = ctx.actor;
    const what = eff.what || 'health';
    b.reveals = b.reveals || [];
    const list = tg.filter((t) => K.isEnemy(a, t));
    const targets = list.length ? list : K.enemies(b, a);
    switch (what) {
      case 'stealthed_enemies': case 'hidden_enemies': case 'stealthed': case 'illusions': case 'ambushes':
        for (const e of K.enemies(b, a)) { if (K.hasStatus(e, 'stealthed')) K.removeStatus(b, e, 'stealthed'); if (what === 'illusions' && e.kind === 'decoy') { e.alive = false; K.log(b, { k: 'fade', tgt: e.id }); } }
        a.flags.seeStealth = true; break;
      case 'intent': case 'turn_order': case 'turn_order_extended': b.intentsVisible = true; break;
      case 'is_corrupted': for (const t of targets) if (t.tags.has('corrupted')) K.say(b, `${t.name} reeks of corruption.`); break;
      case 'weakest_enemy': { const w = X.lowest(K.enemies(b, a)); if (w) { K.applyStatus(b, a, w, 'exposed', 1, { dur: 2 }); } break; }
      default: break;
    }
    for (const t of targets) { t.revealed = t.revealed || {}; t.revealed[what] = true; }
    b.reveals.push({ what, by: a.id, targets: targets.map((t) => t.id) });
    K.log(b, { k: 'reveal', what, src: a.id, tgts: targets.map((t) => t.id) });
  };
  O.share_info = function (ctx) { ctx.b.intentsVisible = true; for (const e of K.enemies(ctx.b, ctx.actor)) { e.revealed = e.revealed || {}; e.revealed.weaknesses = true; } };
  O.share_reveals = O.share_info;

  // ---------- tests ----------
  X.test = function (ctx, test, t) {
    const b = ctx.b, a = ctx.actor;
    const tt = t || (ctx.targets || [])[0] || (ctx.event && ctx.event.other);
    const sig = (a.signatureStatuses || [])[0] || (ctx.ab && ctx.ab.essence && EB.content.essences[ctx.ab.essence] && EB.content.essences[ctx.ab.essence].signatureStatus);
    switch (test) {
      case 'target_has_more_health_than_self': return !!tt && K.maxHp(tt) > K.maxHp(a);
      case 'chance_50': return b.rng() < 0.5 + 0.03 * K.stacks(a, 'fortune');
      case 'coffers_spent_at_least_5': return (a.flags.coffersSpent || 0) >= 5 || K.stacks(a, 'coffers') >= 5;
      case 'enemy_count_bleeding_at_least_1': return K.enemies(b, a).some((e) => K.hasStatus(e, 'bleeding'));
      case 'phase_full': return (a.statuses.moonphase && a.statuses.moonphase.phase === 1) || !!a.flags.phaseFull;
      case 'phase_new': return a.statuses.moonphase && a.statuses.moonphase.phase === 3;
      case 'self_fortune_at_least_5': return K.stacks(a, 'fortune') >= 5;
      case 'self_has_familiar': return K.allies(b, a).some((x) => x.kind === 'familiar' && x.owner === a.id);
      case 'self_health_below_40': return K.hpPct(a) < 0.4;
      case 'self_health_below_50': return K.hpPct(a) < 0.5;
      case 'self_ire_at_least_5': return K.stacks(a, 'ire') >= 5;
      case 'self_stealthed': return K.hasStatus(a, 'stealthed') || !!ctx.fromStealth;
      case 'tide_high': return !!a.flags.tideHigh || (!a.flags.tide && K.stacks(a, 'swell') >= 3);
      case 'tide_low': return a.flags.tide ? !a.flags.tideHigh : K.stacks(a, 'swell') < 3;
    }
    if (!tt) return false;
    switch (test) {
      case 'target_acted_before_you': return tt.turns > a.turns || (tt.lastActTime || 0) > (a.lastActTime || 0);
      case 'target_asleep': return K.hasStatus(tt, 'asleep');
      case 'target_damaged_you_last': return a.lastDamage[0] && a.lastDamage[0].src === tt.id;
      case 'target_frozen': return K.hasStatus(tt, 'frozen') || K.stacks(tt, 'chilled') >= 3;
      case 'target_guilt_at_least_3': return K.stacks(tt, 'guilt') >= 3;
      case 'target_has_affliction': return K.afflictions(tt).length > 0;
      case 'target_has_boon': return K.boons(tt).length > 0;
      case 'target_has_signature': return sig ? K.hasStatus(tt, sig) : K.afflictions(tt).length > 0;
      case 'target_signature_at_least_5': return sig ? K.stacks(tt, sig) >= 5 : false;
      case 'target_health_below_30': return K.hpPct(tt) < 0.3;
      case 'target_health_below_50': return K.hpPct(tt) < 0.5;
      case 'target_is_ally': return !K.isEnemy(a, tt);
      case 'target_is_alone': return K.allies(b, tt).filter((x) => x.kind !== 'decoy').length === 0 || K.rowOf(b, tt.side, tt.row).length === 1;
      case 'target_is_beast': return tt.tags.has('beast');
      case 'target_is_construct': return tt.tags.has('construct');
      case 'target_is_corrupted': return tt.tags.has('corrupted');
      case 'target_is_leader': return tt.tags.has('leader') || tt.boss;
      case 'target_is_undead': return tt.tags.has('undead');
      case 'target_marked': return K.hasStatus(tt, 'marked') || K.hasStatus(tt, 'quarry');
      case 'target_targeting_ally': return !!(tt.intent && tt.intent.target && K.byId(b, tt.intent.target) && !K.isEnemy(a, K.byId(b, tt.intent.target)) && tt.intent.target !== a.id);
    }
    b.unknownTests = b.unknownTests || {}; b.unknownTests[test] = 1;
    return false;
  };
})(globalThis.EB = globalThis.EB || {});

;
(function (EB) {
  'use strict';
  const K = EB.K, X = EB.X;
  const O = EB.OPS;
  const H = O._h;
  const tags = (d) => (d && d.tags) || [];
  const has = (d, ...t) => tags(d).some((x) => t.includes(x));
  const effJson = (d) => JSON.stringify((d && d.effects) || []);
  const hostileDef = (d) => d && (['enemy_single', 'enemy_all', 'enemy_row', 'enemy_vanguard'].includes(d.target) || d.weapon);
  const STATUS_ONLY = { only_attack_marked_targets: ['marked', 'quarry'], only_attack_your_quarry: ['quarry', 'marked'], judge_fairly_attack_only_condemned: ['condemned'], only_damage_starlit_enemies: ['starlit'], never_attack_unwebbed_target: ['webbed'], no_direct_attacks_on_unbefogged: ['befogged'] };
  const ALIAS = { always_act_within_quick_window: 'only_quick_tempo_actions', never_use_stealth: 'never_use_stealth_or_hide', never_use_stealth_or_deception: 'never_use_stealth_or_hide', never_use_stealth_or_shadow: 'never_use_stealth_or_hide', only_attack_feud_target_until_dead: 'attack_only_one_target_until_dead', damage_only_oath_target_until_it_dies: 'attack_only_one_target_until_dead', must_kill_named_target: 'attack_only_one_target_until_dead', never_receive_healing_from_others: 'no_healing_from_others', no_ally_may_fall: 'never_let_an_ally_fall', never_heal_enemies_or_spare: 'always_cackle_and_never_heal_enemies' };

  // Called after an action: did it break the oath?
  O._oathViolation = function (b, u, d, ctx) {
    const o = u.flags.oath; if (!o) return null;
    const r = ALIAS[o.restriction] || o.restriction;
    const tgts = (ctx && ctx.targets) || [];
    const enemiesHit = tgts.filter((t) => K.isEnemy(u, t));
    const hostile = hostileDef(d) || enemiesHit.length > 0;
    const dealt = u.directThisAction || 0;
    const prev = o.lastDef;
    switch (r) {
      case 'only_melee_attacks': if (hostile && !EB.B.isMelee(d, u)) return 'used a ranged or magical attack'; break;
      case 'unarmed_and_physical_only': if (hostile && (EB.B.isSpell(d) || /"type": "(fire|cold|lightning|arcane|holy|radiant|necrotic|psychic|spirit|void|poison|nature|sound|decay|disease|light|magic)"/.test(effJson(d)))) return 'used something other than fists and force'; break;
      case 'no_dialogue_and_no_physical_attacks': if (hostile && /"type": "physical"/.test(effJson(d))) return 'struck physically'; break;
      case 'only_cast_spells': if (!EB.B.isSpell(d)) return 'did something other than cast a spell'; break;
      case 'no_dialogue_no_spells': if (EB.B.isSpell(d)) return 'cast a spell'; break;
      case 'only_fire_abilities_and_weapon': if (!d.weapon && !has(d, 'Fire', 'Ember', 'Ash', 'Heat', 'Dragon')) return 'used a non-fire ability'; break;
      case 'only_space_abilities': if (!has(d, 'Space')) return 'used a non-Space ability'; break;
      case 'only_quick_tempo_actions': if ((d.tempo || 'standard') !== 'quick') return 'took a slow action'; break;
      case 'never_use_quick_actions': if (d.tempo === 'quick') return 'took a quick action'; break;
      case 'never_take_slow_actions': if (d.tempo === 'slow') return 'took a slow action'; break;
      case 'only_slow_attacks': if (hostile && d.tempo !== 'slow') return 'attacked without patience'; break;
      case 'never_use_same_ability_twice_in_fight': if (d.id && u.history.filter((x) => x === d.id).length > 1) return 'repeated an ability'; break;
      case 'never_use_same_ability_twice_in_a_row': case 'use_different_ability_each_turn': if (prev && prev === d.id) return 'repeated an ability'; break;
      case 'never_target_allies': if (tgts.some((t) => t !== u && !K.isEnemy(u, t)) && d.target !== 'ally_all') return 'targeted an ally'; break;
      case 'never_target_allies_with_afflictions': if (tgts.some((t) => !K.isEnemy(u, t)) && /apply_status/.test(effJson(d)) && tgts.some((t) => !K.isEnemy(u, t) && K.afflictions(t).length)) return 'afflicted an ally'; break;
      case 'deal_no_direct_damage': case 'never_deal_direct_damage': if (dealt > 0) return 'dealt direct damage'; break;
      case 'no_direct_damage_over_0_5': { let big = false; K.mapEffects(d.effects, (e) => { if (e.op === 'damage' && (e.power || 0) > 0.5) big = true; return e; }); if (big) return 'struck too hard'; break; }
      case 'never_use_stealth_or_hide': if (has(d, 'Stealth', 'Shadow', 'Illusion', 'Deceit') || /stealthed/.test(effJson(d))) return 'used stealth or trickery'; break;
      case 'never_use_curse_or_blood_abilities': if (has(d, 'Curse', 'Hex', 'Blood')) return 'used a curse or blood ability'; break;
      case 'never_use_light_abilities_or_items': if (has(d, 'Light', 'Holy', 'Radiant', 'Sun')) return 'used light'; break;
      case 'never_use_weapons_or_armor_abilities': if (d.weapon || has(d, 'Weapon', 'Armor')) return 'used a weapon'; break;
      case 'never_use_shields': if (/"op": "shield"/.test(effJson(d))) return 'raised a shield'; break;
      case 'never_use_conjured_weapons_from_others': if (u.item && u.item.conjured && u.item.src !== u.id) return 'took up another\'s weapon'; break;
      case 'never_heal_self': case 'never_heal_this_combat': if (u.flags.healedSelf) return 'healed'; break;
      case 'no_shields_or_healing_on_self': if (u.flags.healedSelf || u.flags.shieldedSelf) return 'protected themselves'; break;
      case 'never_heal_or_cleanse_allies': if (tgts.some((t) => t !== u && !K.isEnemy(u, t)) && /"op": "(heal|cleanse)"/.test(effJson(d))) return 'healed an ally'; break;
      case 'always_cackle_and_never_heal_enemies': if (enemiesHit.length && /"op": "heal"/.test(effJson(d))) return 'healed an enemy'; break;
      case 'attack_only_one_target_until_dead': {
        const ot = o.target && K.byId(b, o.target);
        if (ot && ot.alive && enemiesHit.some((t) => t !== ot)) return 'attacked someone other than the sworn target';
        if ((!ot || !ot.alive) && enemiesHit[0] && !o.target) o.target = enemiesHit[0].id;
        break;
      }
      case 'must_finish_targets_below_30': { const low = K.enemies(b, u).filter((e) => K.hpPct(e) < 0.3); if (hostile && low.length && !enemiesHit.some((t) => low.includes(t))) return 'left a wounded foe standing'; break; }
      case 'never_damage_waking_targets_first': { const sl = K.enemies(b, u).filter((e) => K.hasStatus(e, 'asleep')); if (hostile && sl.length && !enemiesHit.some((t) => sl.includes(t))) return 'attacked a waking foe first'; break; }
      case 'never_attack_from_the_front': if (hostile && EB.B.isMelee(d, u) && u.row === 'vanguard' && !ctx.fromStealth) return 'attacked from the front'; break;
      case 'alternate_attack_and_support': case 'heal_before_attacking_each_turn': if (prev != null && o.lastHostile === hostile) return 'failed to alternate'; break;
      case 'attack_at_high_heal_at_low': if (K.hpPct(u) >= 0.5 && !hostile) return 'didn\'t attack while strong'; if (K.hpPct(u) < 0.5 && hostile) return 'attacked while weak'; break;
      case 'no_damage_first_2_turns': if (u.turns <= 2 && dealt > 0) return 'struck too soon'; break;
      case 'never_act_first_in_round': case 'never_attack_first_each_round': if (hostile && (b.lastEnemyAct || 0) < (u.prevTurnTime || 0)) return 'struck first'; break;
      case 'never_change_mask': if (/change_mask/.test(effJson(d))) return 'changed masks'; break;
      default:
        if (STATUS_ONLY[r] && hostile && enemiesHit.some((t) => !STATUS_ONLY[r].some((s) => K.hasStatus(t, s)))) return 'attacked an unworthy target';
    }
    return null;
  };

  O._oathEndCheck = function (b, u) {
    const o = u.flags.oath; if (!o) return null;
    const r = ALIAS[o.restriction] || o.restriction;
    switch (r) {
      case 'attack_every_turn': if (!u.attackedThisTurn) return 'went a turn without attacking'; break;
      case 'never_end_turn_below_30_stamina': if (u.sta < K.maxSta(u) * 0.3) return 'ran out of breath'; break;
      case 'keep_every_ally_kindled': if (K.allies(b, u, true).some((x) => !K.hasStatus(x, 'kindled'))) return 'let a flame go out'; break;
      case 'always_have_a_construct': if (!K.allies(b, u).some((x) => x.tags.has('construct'))) return 'stood without a construct'; break;
      case 'always_have_a_decoy': if (!K.allies(b, u).some((x) => x.kind === 'decoy')) return 'stood without a decoy'; break;
      case 'always_have_a_summon': if (!K.allies(b, u).some((x) => x.owner === u.id)) return 'stood alone'; break;
      case 'never_end_turn_in_same_row_twice': if (o.lastRow === u.row) return 'stayed put'; o.lastRow = u.row; break;
      case 'never_end_turn_unhidden_or_evasive': if (!K.hasStatus(u, 'stealthed') && !K.hasStatus(u, 'evasive')) return 'stood exposed'; break;
      case 'never_end_turn_unstealthed_twice': if (!K.hasStatus(u, 'stealthed')) { if (o.unhid) return 'stood exposed twice'; o.unhid = true; } else o.unhid = false; break;
      case 'toll_every_3_turns': o.sinceToll = (o.sinceToll || 0) + 1; if (u.lastAbility && has(u.lastAbility.def, 'Bell')) o.sinceToll = 0; if (o.sinceToll > 3) return 'let the bell fall silent'; break;
      case 'use_rune_ability_every_turn': if (!(u.lastAbility && has(u.lastAbility.def, 'Rune') && u.actedThisTurn)) return 'let the runes go cold'; break;
      case 'use_song_ability_every_turn': if (!(u.lastAbility && has(u.lastAbility.def, 'Song') && u.actedThisTurn)) return 'stopped singing'; break;
      case 'never_be_mind_controlled': if (u.flags.charmed || K.hasStatus(u, 'confused')) return 'lost their mind'; break;
      case 'never_receive_boons': if (K.boons(u).some((id) => u.statuses[id].src && u.statuses[id].src !== u.id)) return 'accepted help'; break;
      case 'pay_health_each_turn': K.loseHp(b, u, K.maxHp(u) * 0.04, u); break;
    }
    return null;
  };

  O._oathMoveCheck = function (b, u, from) {
    const o = u.flags.oath; if (!o) return null;
    const r = o.restriction;
    if (['never_move_rows', 'never_change_rows', 'never_flee_never_change_rows', 'never_move_or_fly'].includes(r)) return 'moved';
    if (['stay_in_rear_row'].includes(r) && u.row !== 'rear') return 'left the rear';
    if (['stay_in_vanguard'].includes(r) && u.row !== 'vanguard') return 'left the front';
    if (['never_retreat_to_rear_row', 'never_flee_or_retreat_rows', 'never_drop_the_banner_or_retreat'].includes(r) && u.row === 'rear') return 'retreated';
    return null;
  };

  O._oathBreak = function (b, u, why) {
    const o = u.flags.oath;
    if (!o) return;
    u.flags.oath = null;
    K.say(b, `${u.name} ${why}, breaking the oath.`, u.side === 'ally' ? 'bad' : 'good');
    K.log(b, { k: 'oath_break', tgt: u.id });
    u.mods = u.mods.filter((m) => m.tag !== o.key);
    u.hooks = u.hooks.filter((h) => h.tag !== o.key);
    if (o.restriction === 'every_ability_has_25pct_fail_chance') u.flags.failChance = 0;
    if (K.hasStatus(u, 'sworn')) K.removeStatus(b, u, 'sworn');
    X.run({ b, actor: u, power: 1, targets: [u] }, o.breakPenalty || [], [u]);
    if (o.declared) u.flags.declared = null;
  };
  O._oathBlocks = function () { return null; };
  O._oathAfter = function (b, u, slot, ctx) {
    const o = u.flags.oath; if (!o) return;
    const why = O._oathViolation(b, u, slot.def, ctx);
    o.lastDef = slot.def.id; o.lastHostile = hostileDef(slot.def) || (ctx.targets || []).some((t) => K.isEnemy(u, t));
    if (why) O._oathBreak(b, u, why);
  };

  O.oath = function (ctx, eff) {
    const b = ctx.b, u = ctx.actor;
    if (u.flags.oath) O._oathBreak(b, u, 'swore a new oath over the old');
    let r = eff.restriction;
    if (r === 'choose_one_of_three_lifelong_restrictions') r = b.rng.pick(['never_flee_or_retreat_rows', 'only_melee_attacks', 'never_use_stealth_or_hide']);
    const key = u.id + ':oath:' + (ctx.ab && ctx.ab.id);
    const o = { restriction: r, breakPenalty: eff.breakPenalty || [], key, target: null };
    const t0 = (ctx.targets || []).find((t) => K.isEnemy(u, t));
    if (t0) o.target = t0.id;
    const ally = (ctx.targets || []).find((t) => t !== u && !K.isEnemy(u, t));
    if (ally) o.ward = ally.id;
    u.flags.oath = o;
    const m0 = u.mods.length, h0 = u.hooks.length;
    X.run(X.sub(ctx, { passive: true }), eff.bonus || [], [u]);
    for (let i = m0; i < u.mods.length; i++) u.mods[i].tag = key;
    for (let i = h0; i < u.hooks.length; i++) u.hooks[i].tag = key;
    if (r === 'every_ability_has_25pct_fail_chance') u.flags.failChance = 0.25;
    if (r === 'declare_three_abilities_use_only_those') {
      const pool = u.abilities.filter((s) => !s.def.passive && s.def.type !== 'familiar' && !s.form);
      u.flags.declared = pool.slice(0, 3).map((s) => s.id).concat(ctx.slot ? [ctx.slot.id] : []);
      o.declared = true;
    }
    if (r === 'doom_self_at_end') H.after(u, 5, () => { if (u.flags.oath && u.flags.oath.key === key) K.applyStatus(b, u, u, 'doom', 4, { dur: 1, force: true }); });
    if (r === 'store_healing' || r === 'never_heal_this_combat') {}
    if (r === 'never_take_damage_from_spells_unwarded') K.addHook(b, u, { trigger: 'when_take_damage', caster: u.id, tag: key, dur: 'combat', fn: (bb, uu, ev) => { if (ev.spell && !K.hasStatus(uu, 'warded') && uu.flags.oath && uu.flags.oath.key === key) O._oathBreak(bb, uu, 'was struck by an unwarded spell'); } });
    K.log(b, { k: 'oath', tgt: u.id, r });
    K.say(b, `${u.name} swears an oath: ${r.replace(/_/g, ' ')}.`, 'sys');
  };

  // ---------- vows ----------
  O.vow = function (ctx, eff) {
    const b = ctx.b, u = ctx.actor;
    u.vows = u.vows || [];
    const t = (ctx.targets || [])[0];
    const v = { condition: eff.condition, dur: eff.duration === 'combat' ? 'combat' : (eff.duration || 3), reward: eff.reward || [], target: t ? t.id : null, start: u.turns, power: ctx.power };
    if (eff.condition === 'shield_holds') v.shieldWatch = true;
    u.vows.push(v);
