    K.say(b, `${u.name} vows: ${eff.condition.replace(/_/g, ' ')}.`, 'sys');
    if (!b.vowWatch) {
      b.vowWatch = true;
      const prev = b.onDeathCheck;
      b.onDeathCheck = (dead) => { if (prev) prev(dead); O._vowDeath(b, dead); };
    }
  };
  O._vowDeath = function (b, dead) {
    for (const u of b.units) {
      if (!u.vows || !u.alive) continue;
      for (const v of u.vows.slice()) {
        if (v.condition === 'target_dies' && v.target === dead.id) O._resolveVow(b, u, v, 'ok');
        else if (v.condition === 'marked_enemy_dies' && K.isEnemy(u, dead) && (dead.statuses.marked || dead.statuses.quarry || true)) O._resolveVow(b, u, v, 'ok');
        else if (v.condition === 'ally_not_downed' && !K.isEnemy(u, dead) && dead !== u && dead.kind !== 'decoy' && dead.kind !== 'conjuration') O._resolveVow(b, u, v, 'fail');
        else if ((v.condition === 'target_not_downed' || v.condition === 'target_survives') && v.target === dead.id) O._resolveVow(b, u, v, 'fail');
      }
    }
    // oaths about allies falling
    for (const u of b.units) {
      const o = u.flags.oath; if (!o || !u.alive || K.isEnemy(u, dead)) continue;
      const r = ALIAS[o.restriction] || o.restriction;
      if ((r === 'never_let_an_ally_fall' && dead !== u && dead.kind !== 'decoy' && dead.kind !== 'conjuration') ||
        ((r === 'protected_ally_must_not_fall' || r === 'chosen_ally_must_not_fall') && o.ward === dead.id) ||
        (r === 'never_let_familiar_fall' && dead.kind === 'familiar')) O._oathBreak(b, u, `let ${dead.name} fall`);
    }
  };
  O._resolveVow = function (b, u, v, how) {
    if (!u.vows || !u.vows.includes(v)) return;
    let ok = how === 'ok';
    if (how === 'expire') {
      ok = ['ally_not_downed', 'self_not_below_50', 'shield_holds', 'target_not_downed', 'target_survives'].includes(v.condition) && !v.failed;
      if (v.condition === 'attack_next_turn') ok = !!v.attacked;
      if (v.condition === 'next_ability_hits') ok = !!v.hit;
    }
    u.vows = u.vows.filter((x) => x !== v);
    if (ok) {
      K.say(b, `${u.name} keeps the vow.`, 'good');
      X.run({ b, actor: u, power: v.power || 1, targets: [u] }, v.reward, [u]);
    } else {
      K.say(b, `${u.name}'s vow is broken.`, 'bad');
      if (K.hasStatus(u, 'sworn')) K.removeStatus(b, u, 'sworn');
    }
  };
  O.end_vow_without_penalty = function (ctx) { const u = ctx.actor; if (u.vows && u.vows.length) { u.vows = []; K.say(ctx.b, `${u.name} sets the vow down, honorably.`, 'dim'); } };

  // per-action vow and trap/contract triggers
  O._onActTriggers = function (b, u, def, ctx) {
    b.lastEnemyAct = b.lastEnemyAct || 0;
    if (u.side === 'enemy') b.lastEnemyAct = b.time;
    u.prevTurnTime = b.time;
    // vows
    if (u.vows) for (const v of u.vows.slice()) {
      if (v.condition === 'attack_next_turn' && u.turns > v.start) { v.attacked = hostileDef(def); O._resolveVow(b, u, v, 'expire'); }
      if (v.condition === 'next_ability_hits' && u.turns >= v.start && def.id !== (ctx.slot && ctx.slot.id && v.fromId)) { if (hostileDef(def)) { v.hit = (ctx.hitTargets || []).length > 0; O._resolveVow(b, u, v, 'expire'); } }
      if (v.condition === 'self_not_below_50' && K.hpPct(u) < 0.5) { v.failed = true; O._resolveVow(b, u, v, 'fail'); }
    }
    // shield watch
    for (const x of b.units) if (x.vows) for (const v of x.vows.slice()) if (v.condition === 'shield_holds' && v.hadShield && !x.shields.length) O._resolveVow(b, x, v, 'fail'); else if (v.condition === 'shield_holds' && x.shields.length) v.hadShield = true;
    // traps on the actor
    const hostile = hostileDef(def) || (ctx && (ctx.targets || []).some((t) => K.isEnemy(u, t)));
    if (u.traps && u.traps.length) {
      for (const tr of u.traps.slice()) {
        const fire = tr.trigger === 'target_acts' || tr.trigger === 'target_moves_or_acts' || tr.trigger === 'enemy_moves_or_attacks' && hostile || (tr.trigger === 'target_attacks' && hostile);
        if (!fire) continue;
        if (tr.chance != null && b.rng() >= tr.chance) continue;
        u.traps = u.traps.filter((x) => x !== tr);
        const src = K.byId(b, tr.src);
        if (!src) continue;
        K.say(b, `A trap springs on ${u.name}!`, u.side === 'enemy' ? 'good' : 'bad');
        X.run({ b, actor: src, power: tr.power || 1, targets: [u], posthumous: true }, tr.effects, [u]);
      }
    }
    // contracts
    if (u.flags.contract && hostile) {
      const c = u.flags.contract; const src = K.byId(b, c.src);
      if (src && (ctx.targets || []).includes(src)) {
        K.say(b, `${u.name} breaks the contract and pays the price.`, 'good');
        X.run({ b, actor: src, power: c.power || 1, targets: [u], posthumous: true }, c.penalty, [u]);
      }
    }
    // penalty for tricks
    if (u.flags.trickPenalty && has(def, 'Stealth', 'Illusion', 'Deceit', 'Shadow', 'Mask')) K.applyStatus(b, K.byId(b, u.flags.trickPenalty.src), u, u.flags.trickPenalty.status, u.flags.trickPenalty.stacks);
    // oath end-of-turn checks happen here (one action per turn)
    if (u.flags.oath) { const why = O._oathEndCheck(b, u); if (why) O._oathBreak(b, u, why); }
    u.directThisAction = 0;
    u.lastActTime = b.time;
  };
  O._onMoveTriggers = function (b, u, from) {
    if (u.traps && u.traps.length) {
      for (const tr of u.traps.slice()) {
        if (!['target_moves', 'target_moves_or_acts', 'enemy_moves_or_attacks'].includes(tr.trigger)) continue;
        u.traps = u.traps.filter((x) => x !== tr);
        const src = K.byId(b, tr.src); if (!src) continue;
        K.say(b, `A trap springs on ${u.name}!`, u.side === 'enemy' ? 'good' : 'bad');
        X.run({ b, actor: src, power: tr.power || 1, targets: [u], posthumous: true }, tr.effects, [u]);
      }
    }
    const why = O._oathMoveCheck(b, u, from);
    if (why) O._oathBreak(b, u, why);
  };
})(globalThis.EB = globalThis.EB || {});

;
(function (EB) {
  'use strict';
  const U = EB.U, K = EB.K, X = EB.X;
  const O = EB.OPS;
  const H = O._h;
  const B = () => EB.B;
  const each = (tg, fn) => { for (const t of tg) if (t && t.alive) fn(t); };
  const D = (d, def) => (d == null ? def : d === 'aura' || d === 'scene' || d === 'form' ? 'combat' : d);
  const timed = (u, key, val, d) => B().setTimed(u, key, val, D(d, 2));
  const enemiesOf = (ctx, tg) => { const e = tg.filter((t) => K.isEnemy(ctx.actor, t)); return e; };
  const alliesOf = (ctx, tg) => tg.filter((t) => !K.isEnemy(ctx.actor, t));
  const say = (ctx, m, c) => K.say(ctx.b, m, c);
  const ev = (ctx) => ctx.event || {};
  const shareStacks = (ctx, tg, id, frac) => {
    const a = ctx.actor; const n = Math.max(1, Math.ceil(K.stacks(a, id) * (frac || 0.5)));
    if (!K.stacks(a, id)) return;
    const list = alliesOf(ctx, tg).filter((t) => t !== a);
    for (const t of list.length ? list : K.allies(ctx.b, a)) K.applyStatus(ctx.b, a, t, id, n);
  };
  const addPower = (ctx, amt) => { ctx.power = (ctx.power || 1) * (1 + amt); };

  // ---------- defensive reactions ----------
  O.parry = function (ctx, eff, tg) {
    const e = ev(ctx);
    if (e.other && ctx.hook) { if (ctx.b.rng() < (eff.chance == null ? 1 : eff.chance) && !e.other.flags.blockParry) { e.negated = true; K.log(ctx.b, { k: 'miss', src: e.other.id, tgt: ctx.hookListener.id, why: 'parried' }); } return; }
    each(alliesOf(ctx, tg).length ? alliesOf(ctx, tg) : [ctx.actor], (t) => timed(t, 'parryNext', { chance: eff.chance == null ? 1 : eff.chance }, 2));
  };
  O.dodge = function (ctx, eff, tg) { const e = ev(ctx); if (ctx.hook && e.other) { e.dodged = true; return; } each(alliesOf(ctx, tg).length ? alliesOf(ctx, tg) : [ctx.actor], (t) => timed(t, 'dodgeNext', true, 2)); };
  O.negate = function (ctx, eff, tg) { const e = ev(ctx); if (ctx.hook && e.other) { e.negated = true; e.cancelled = true; return; } each([ctx.actor], (t) => timed(t, 'dodgeNext', true, 2)); };
  O.phase_dodge = O.dodge;
  O.dodge_chance = function (ctx, eff, tg) { each(alliesOf(ctx, tg).length ? alliesOf(ctx, tg) : [ctx.actor], (t) => K.addMod(ctx.b, t, 'evasion', (eff.percent || 20) / 100, ctx.passive ? 'combat' : 2, ctx.actor)); };
  O.phase_dodge_chance = function (ctx, eff) { timed(ctx.actor, 'phaseDodge', (eff.percent || 25) / 100, ctx.passive ? 'combat' : 2); };
  O.phase = function (ctx, eff, tg) { each(alliesOf(ctx, tg).length ? alliesOf(ctx, tg) : [ctx.actor], (t) => { timed(t, 'phased', true, D(eff.duration, 1)); say(ctx, `${t.name} turns intangible.`, 'dim'); }); };
  O.dodge_if_seen_before = function (ctx) { ctx.actor.flags.dodgeSeen = true; };
  O.parry_bonus_if_seen_before = function (ctx, eff) { ctx.actor.flags.parrySeen = (eff.percent || 20) / 100; };
  O.intercept = function (ctx, eff, tg) {
    const e = ev(ctx);
    if (ctx.hook && e.other && ctx.hookListener && ctx.hookListener !== ctx.actor && ctx.actor.alive) { e.redirect = ctx.actor; return; }
    const ally = alliesOf(ctx, tg).find((t) => t !== ctx.actor);
    timed(ctx.actor, 'interceptFor', ally ? ally.id : 'all', 2);
  };
  O.redirect = function (ctx, eff, tg) {
    const e = ev(ctx), b = ctx.b, a = ctx.actor;
    if (eff.chance != null && b.rng() >= eff.chance) return;
    let to = null;
    switch (eff.to) {
      case 'self': to = a; break;
      case 'attacker': to = e.other; break;
      case 'decoy': to = K.allies(b, a).find((x) => x.kind === 'decoy'); break;
      case 'random_enemy': { const p = e.other ? K.allies(b, e.other) : K.enemies(b, a); to = p.length ? b.rng.pick(p) : null; break; }
      case 'random_familiar': { const p = K.allies(b, a, true).filter((x) => x.kind === 'familiar'); to = p.length ? b.rng.pick(p) : null; break; }
      default: { const p = K.alive(b).filter((x) => x !== e.other); to = b.rng.pick(p); }
    }
    if (!to) return;
    if (ctx.hook && e.other) { e.redirect = to; return; }
    each(alliesOf(ctx, tg).length ? alliesOf(ctx, tg) : [a], (t) => timed(t, 'redirectAttacks', { to: to.id }, 2));
  };
  O.redirect_attacks = function (ctx, eff, tg) {
    const b = ctx.b, a = ctx.actor;
    let to = a;
    if (eff.to === 'decoy') to = K.allies(b, a).find((x) => x.kind === 'decoy') || a;
    let from = eff.from ? X.resolve(ctx, eff.from === 'summoner' ? 'owner' : eff.from === 'target' ? 'target' : eff.from, tg, eff) : alliesOf(ctx, tg);
    for (const f of from) if (f !== to) timed(f, 'redirectAttacks', { to: to.id, filter: eff.filter || null }, D(eff.duration, 2));
  };
  O.redirect_attacks_away = function (ctx, eff, tg) { each(tg, (t) => { const p = K.allies(ctx.b, t).filter((x) => x !== t); if (p.length) timed(t, 'redirectAttacks', { to: ctx.b.rng.pick(p).id, chance: eff.chance }, D(eff.duration, 2)); }); };
  O.redirect_to_decoy = function (ctx, eff, tg) { for (const t of K.allies(ctx.b, ctx.actor, true)) timed(t, 'redirectToDecoy', eff.chance || 0.5, 3); };
  O.redirect_next_attack = function (ctx, eff, tg) { each(enemiesOf(ctx, tg), (t) => timed(t, 'randomRetarget', 1, 1)); };
  O.pull_ranged_attacks_to_self = function (ctx) { for (const t of K.allies(ctx.b, ctx.actor)) timed(t, 'redirectAttacks', { to: ctx.actor.id, filter: 'ranged' }, 2); };
  O.deflect_projectile = function (ctx, eff) {
    const e = ev(ctx);
    if (e.other && e.ranged) { e.negated = true; K.damage(ctx.b, ctx.actor, e.other, K.hit(ctx.actor) * 0.5, 'physical', { reflected: true }); say(ctx, `${ctx.actor.name} sends it back!`, 'good'); }
    else timed(ctx.actor, 'dodgeNext', true, 2);
  };
  O.counter = function (ctx, eff, tg) {
    const e = ev(ctx), a = ctx.actor;
    if (ctx.hook && e.other && K.isEnemy(a, e.other)) { X.counter(ctx, a, e.other, (eff.power || 0.5) * (ctx.power || 1)); return; }
    a.flags.counterPower = (eff.power || 0.5) * (ctx.power || 1);
    K.addHook(ctx.b, a, { trigger: 'when_hit_melee', caster: a.id, once: true, dur: 3, fn: (b, u, e2) => { if (e2.other && e2.other.alive) X.counter({ b, actor: u }, u, e2.other, u.flags.counterPower || 0.5); } });
  };
  O.reflect = function (ctx, eff, tg) {
    const a = ctx.actor;
    const pct = (eff.percent || 30) / 100;
    const e = ev(ctx);
    if (ctx.hook && e.other && e.amount == null && K.isEnemy(a, e.other) && (e.ranged || e.spell)) {
      e.negated = true; say(ctx, `${a.name} sends it back at ${e.other.name}!`, 'good');
      K.damage(ctx.b, a, e.other, K.hit(e.other) * 0.8 * (ctx.power || 1), eff.type || 'force', { reflected: true, canCrit: false });
      return;
    }
    if (ctx.hook && e.other && e.amount && K.isEnemy(a, e.other)) {
      if (eff.chance == null || ctx.b.rng() < eff.chance) K.damage(ctx.b, a, e.other, e.amount * pct, eff.type || e.type || 'physical', { reflected: true, canCrit: false, raw: true });
      return;
    }
    for (const t of alliesOf(ctx, tg).length ? alliesOf(ctx, tg) : [a]) K.addHook(ctx.b, t, { trigger: 'when_take_damage', caster: a.id, dur: D(eff.duration, ctx.passive ? 'combat' : 3), fn: (b, u, e2) => { if (e2.other && e2.other.alive && K.isEnemy(u, e2.other) && !e2.dot && (eff.chance == null || b.rng() < eff.chance)) K.damage(b, u, e2.other, e2.amount * pct, eff.type || e2.type || 'physical', { reflected: true, canCrit: false, raw: true }); } });
  };
  O.reflect_next = function (ctx, eff, tg) { each(alliesOf(ctx, tg).length ? alliesOf(ctx, tg) : [ctx.actor], (t) => timed(t, 'reflectNext', { cat: eff.category || 'any' }, D(eff.duration, 3))); };
  O.absorb_spell = function (ctx) { const e = ev(ctx); if (e.other && e.spell) { e.cancelled = true; e.negated = true; K.restore(ctx.b, ctx.actor, 'mana', Math.round(K.maxMana(ctx.actor) * 0.2)); say(ctx, `${ctx.actor.name} drinks the spell down.`, 'good'); } else timed(ctx.actor, 'reflectNext', { cat: 'magic' }, 2); };
  O.counter_spell = function (ctx, eff, tg) { const e = ev(ctx); if (e.other && e.spell) { e.cancelled = true; say(ctx, `${ctx.actor.name} counters the spell!`, 'good'); } else each(enemiesOf(ctx, tg), (t) => timed(t, 'cancelNext', true, 2)); };
  O.interrupt = function (ctx, eff, tg) {
    const e = ev(ctx), b = ctx.b;
    if (eff.chance != null && b.rng() >= eff.chance) return;
    if (ctx.hook && e.other && e.ability) { e.cancelled = true; return; }
    each(enemiesOf(ctx, tg), (t) => { t.flags.cancelNext = true; t.t += 20; K.log(b, { k: 'delay', tgt: t.id, amt: 20 }); });
  };
  O.cancel_next_action = function (ctx, eff, tg) { each(enemiesOf(ctx, tg), (t) => { t.flags.cancelNext = true; }); };
  O.negate_effects = function (ctx, eff, tg) { const e = ev(ctx); if (ctx.hook && e.other) { e.negated = true; return; } each(alliesOf(ctx, tg).length ? alliesOf(ctx, tg) : [ctx.actor], (t) => K.applyStatus(ctx.b, ctx.actor, t, 'warded', 1)); };
  O.negate_damage = function (ctx, eff, tg) { each(alliesOf(ctx, tg).length ? alliesOf(ctx, tg) : [ctx.actor], (t) => timed(t, 'negateDamagePct', (eff.percent || 50) / 100, 1)); };
  O.immune_damage_once = function (ctx, eff, tg) { each(alliesOf(ctx, tg).length ? alliesOf(ctx, tg) : [ctx.actor], (t) => { t.flags.immuneDamageOnce = true; }); };
  O.invulnerable = function (ctx, eff, tg) { each(alliesOf(ctx, tg).length ? alliesOf(ctx, tg) : [ctx.actor], (t) => { timed(t, 'invulnerable', true, D(eff.duration, 1)); say(ctx, `${t.name} becomes untouchable.`, 'good'); }); };
  O.cannot_drop_below = function (ctx, eff, tg) {
    each(alliesOf(ctx, tg).length ? alliesOf(ctx, tg) : [ctx.actor], (t) => {
      const h = eff.health || 1;
      const frac = h <= 1 && h !== 1 ? h : 1 / Math.max(1, K.maxHp(t)) * h;
      timed(t, 'cannotDropBelow', Math.max(frac, 0.0001), eff.until === 'target_dies' ? 'combat' : D(eff.duration, 1));
    });
  };
  O.self_revive = function (ctx, eff) { ctx.actor.flags.selfRevive = { uses: eff.uses || 1, health: eff.health || 0.3 }; };
  O.self_revive_once = function (ctx, eff) { ctx.actor.flags.selfRevive = { uses: 1, health: eff.health || 0.3 }; };
  O.prevent_rise = function (ctx, eff, tg) { for (const e of K.enemies(ctx.b, ctx.actor)) e.flags.selfRevive = null; ctx.b.flags.preventRise = true; };
  O.revive = function (ctx, eff) {
    const b = ctx.b, a = ctx.actor;
    const dead = b.units.filter((u) => !u.alive && u.side === a.side && (u.kind === 'hero' || u.kind === 'familiar' || u.kind === 'enemy') && u !== a);
    if (dead.length) {
      const d = dead[dead.length - 1];
      d.alive = true; d.hp = Math.max(1, Math.round(K.maxHp(d) * (eff.health || 0.3)));
      d.statuses = {}; d.t = b.time + 30;
      K.log(b, { k: 'revive', tgt: d.id }); say(ctx, `${d.name} is brought back!`, a.side === 'ally' ? 'good' : 'bad');
    } else if (eff.or_heal) {
      const l = X.lowest(K.allies(b, a, true)); if (l) K.heal(b, a, l, K.maxHp(l) * (eff.or_heal > 1 ? eff.or_heal / 100 : eff.or_heal));
    }
  };

  // ---------- status manipulation ----------
  O.remove_status = function (ctx, eff, tg) {
    each(tg, (t) => {
      if (!K.hasStatus(t, eff.status)) return;
      if (eff.all || !eff.count) K.removeStatus(ctx.b, t, eff.status); else K.reduceStatus(ctx.b, t, eff.status, eff.count);
    });
  };
  O.prevent_decay = function (ctx, eff, tg) {
    const b = ctx.b;
    b.flags.preventDecay = b.flags.preventDecay || {};
    b.flags.preventDecay[eff.status] = true;
    const turns = ctx.passive ? 999 : 3;
    H.after(ctx.actor, turns, () => { if (b.flags.preventDecay) delete b.flags.preventDecay[eff.status]; });
  };
  O.rallied_no_decay = function (ctx) { for (const t of K.allies(ctx.b, ctx.actor, true)) t.flags.ralliedNoDecay = true; };
  O.rampage_no_decay = function (ctx) { ctx.actor.flags.rampageNoDecay = true; };
  O.keep_signature = function (ctx, eff) { timed(ctx.actor, eff.status === 'rampage' ? 'rampageNoDecay' : 'keep_' + eff.status, true, D(eff.duration, 3)); };
  O.aim_cannot_break = function (ctx) { ctx.actor.flags.aimCannotBreak = true; };
  O.status_immunity = function (ctx, eff, tg) {
    each(alliesOf(ctx, tg).length ? alliesOf(ctx, tg) : [ctx.actor], (t) => {
      t.flags.immune = Object.assign({}, t.flags.immune || {}, { [eff.status]: true });
      if (K.hasStatus(t, eff.status)) K.removeStatus(ctx.b, t, eff.status);
      const d = D(eff.duration, ctx.passive ? 'combat' : 3);
      if (typeof d === 'number') H.after(t, d, () => { if (t.flags.immune) delete t.flags.immune[eff.status]; });
    });
  };
  O.immune_status = O.status_immunity;
  O.immune_to_lock = function (ctx) { O.status_immunity(ctx, { status: 'locked', duration: 'combat' }, [ctx.actor]); };
  O.immune_forced_movement = function (ctx, eff, tg) { each(alliesOf(ctx, tg).length ? alliesOf(ctx, tg) : [ctx.actor], (t) => timed(t, 'immuneForcedMove', true, D(eff.duration, ctx.passive ? 'combat' : 3))); };
  O.status_duration_bonus = function (ctx, eff) { const f = (ctx.actor.flags.statusDur = ctx.actor.flags.statusDur || {}); f[eff.status] = (f[eff.status] || 0) + (eff.turns || 1); };
  O.status_gain_bonus = function (ctx, eff) { const f = (ctx.actor.flags.statusGain = ctx.actor.flags.statusGain || {}); f[eff.status] = (f[eff.status] || 0) + (eff.percent || 25) / 100; };
  O.status_potency = function (ctx, eff) { const f = (ctx.actor.flags.potency = ctx.actor.flags.potency || {}); f[eff.status] = (f[eff.status] || 0) + (eff.percent || 25) / 100; };
  O.status_tick_bonus = function (ctx, eff) { const f = (ctx.actor.flags.statusTickBonus = ctx.actor.flags.statusTickBonus || {}); f[eff.status] = (f[eff.status] || 0) + (eff.percent || 25) / 100; };
  O.max_stacks = function (ctx, eff) { const f = (ctx.actor.flags.maxStacks = ctx.actor.flags.maxStacks || {}); f[eff.status] = (f[eff.status] || 0) + (eff.add || 2); };
  O.modify_applications = function (ctx, eff) { const f = (ctx.actor.flags.modApps = ctx.actor.flags.modApps || {}); f[eff.status] = (f[eff.status] || 0) + (eff.add || 1); };
  O.asleep_not_broken_by_your_damage = function (ctx) { ctx.actor.flags.sleepSafe = true; };
  O.asleep_duration_min = function (ctx, eff, tg) { each(tg, (t) => { if (t.statuses.asleep) { t.statuses.asleep.minTurns = eff.turns || 1; if (typeof t.statuses.asleep.dur === 'number') t.statuses.asleep.dur = Math.max(t.statuses.asleep.dur, eff.turns || 1); } }); };
  O.block_healing_removal = function (ctx, eff, tg) { each(enemiesOf(ctx, tg), (t) => timed(t, 'blockBleedHealRemoval', true, D(eff.duration, 3))); };
  O.block_cleanse = function (ctx, eff, tg) { each(enemiesOf(ctx, tg), (t) => timed(t, 'blockCleanse', true, D(eff.duration, 3))); };
  O.block_cleanse_status = function (ctx, eff, tg) { each(enemiesOf(ctx, tg), (t) => { t.flags.blockCleanseStatus = Object.assign({}, t.flags.blockCleanseStatus || {}, { [eff.status]: true }); }); };
  O.hide_status = function (ctx, eff, tg) { each(tg, (t) => { t.flags.hiddenStatus = Object.assign({}, t.flags.hiddenStatus || {}, { [eff.status]: true }); }); };
  O.transfer_afflictions = function (ctx, eff, tg) {
    const b = ctx.b, a = ctx.actor;
    const from = eff.from === 'self' ? [a] : (eff.from === 'target' ? tg : tg);
    let dest;
    if (eff.to === 'void') dest = null;
    else if (eff.to === 'self') dest = a;
    else if (eff.to === 'random_enemy' || eff.to === 'enemy_single') { const en = K.enemies(b, a); dest = tg.find((t) => K.isEnemy(a, t)) || (en.length ? b.rng.pick(en) : null); }
    else dest = tg.find((t) => K.isEnemy(a, t)) || null;
    const srcList = eff.from ? from : alliesOf(ctx, tg).length ? alliesOf(ctx, tg) : [a];
    let n = 0;
    for (const s of srcList) {
      for (const id of K.afflictions(s)) {
        if (n >= (eff.count || 1)) break;
        const d = K.statusDef(id);
        if (eff.category && eff.category !== 'any' && d.category !== eff.category) continue;
        const st = s.statuses[id];
        K.removeStatus(b, s, id); n++;
        if (dest && dest !== s) K.applyStatus(b, a, dest, id, st.stacks, { dur: st.dur });
      }
    }
  };
  O.convert_boons = function (ctx, eff, tg) {
    each(enemiesOf(ctx, tg), (t) => {
      for (let i = 0; i < (eff.count || 1); i++) { const bn = K.boons(t)[0]; if (!bn) break; K.removeStatus(ctx.b, t, bn); K.applyStatus(ctx.b, ctx.actor, t, eff.into, eff.stacks || 1, { force: true }); }
    });
  };
  O.convert_boon_to = function (ctx, eff, tg) {
    each(enemiesOf(ctx, tg), (t) => { const bn = K.boons(t)[0]; if (bn) { K.removeStatus(ctx.b, t, bn); K.applyStatus(ctx.b, ctx.actor, t, eff.status, eff.stacks || 1, { force: true }); } });
  };
  O.convert_status = function (ctx, eff, tg) {
    each(tg.length ? tg : [ctx.actor], (t) => { const n = K.stacks(t, eff.from); if (n) { K.removeStatus(ctx.b, t, eff.from); K.applyStatus(ctx.b, ctx.actor, t, eff.to, n); } });
  };
  O.convert_fortified_to_empowered = function (ctx, eff, tg) { O.convert_status(ctx, { from: 'fortified', to: 'empowered' }, [ctx.actor]); };
  O.swap_status = function (ctx, eff, tg) {
    const a = ctx.actor; const n = K.stacks(a, eff.status);
    if (!n) return;
    K.removeStatus(ctx.b, a, eff.status);
    each(enemiesOf(ctx, tg).slice(0, 1), (t) => K.applyStatus(ctx.b, a, t, eff.status, n, { force: true }));
  };
  O.steal_status = function (ctx, eff, tg) {
    each(enemiesOf(ctx, tg), (t) => {
      const have = K.stacks(t, eff.status); if (!have) return;
      const n = eff.all ? have : Math.min(have, eff.count || 1);
      K.reduceStatus(ctx.b, t, eff.status, n);
      K.applyStatus(ctx.b, ctx.actor, ctx.actor, eff.status, n);
    });
  };
  O.copy_afflictions = function (ctx, eff, tg) {
    const b = ctx.b, a = ctx.actor;
    const from = K.enemies(b, a).sort((x, y) => K.afflictions(y).length - K.afflictions(x).length)[0];
    if (!from) return;
    const ids = K.afflictions(from).slice(0, eff.count || 2);
    for (const t of K.enemies(b, a)) if (t !== from) for (const id of ids) K.applyStatus(b, a, t, id, from.statuses[id].stacks, { dur: from.statuses[id].dur });
  };
  O.copy_to_source = function (ctx, eff) { const e = ev(ctx); if (e.other && e.status && e.other.alive) K.applyStatus(ctx.b, ctx.actor, e.other, e.status, 1); };
  O.copy_enemy_boon_to_ally = function (ctx, eff, tg) {
    const b = ctx.b, a = ctx.actor;
    const en = enemiesOf(ctx, tg)[0] || K.enemies(b, a).find((x) => K.boons(x).length);
    if (!en) return;
    const bn = K.boons(en)[0]; if (!bn) return;
    const ally = X.lowest(K.allies(b, a, true));
    K.applyStatus(b, a, ally, bn, en.statuses[bn].stacks);
  };
  O.reapply_last_removed = function (ctx, eff, tg) {
    each(tg, (t) => { const lr = (t.lastRemoved || []).find((r) => !eff.category || K.statusDef(r.id).category === eff.category); if (lr) K.applyStatus(ctx.b, ctx.actor, t, lr.id, lr.stacks, { force: true }); });
  };
  O.extend_afflictions = function (ctx, eff, tg) { each(tg, (t) => { for (const id of K.afflictions(t)) if (typeof t.statuses[id].dur === 'number') t.statuses[id].dur += eff.turns || 1; }); };
  O.shorten_afflictions = function (ctx, eff, tg) { each(alliesOf(ctx, tg).length ? alliesOf(ctx, tg) : [ctx.actor], (t) => { for (const id of K.afflictions(t)) { const st = t.statuses[id]; if (typeof st.dur === 'number') { st.dur -= eff.turns || 1; if (st.dur <= 0) K.removeStatus(ctx.b, t, id); } else K.reduceStatus(ctx.b, t, id, eff.turns || 1); } }); };
  O.extend_boons = function (ctx, eff, tg) { each(tg.length ? tg : [ctx.actor], (t) => { for (const id of K.boons(t)) if (typeof t.statuses[id].dur === 'number') t.statuses[id].dur += eff.turns || 1; for (const m of t.mods) if (typeof m.dur === 'number' && m.amt > 0) m.dur += eff.turns || 1; }); };
  O.shorten_boons = function (ctx, eff, tg) { each(enemiesOf(ctx, tg), (t) => { for (const id of K.boons(t)) { const st = t.statuses[id]; if (typeof st.dur === 'number') { st.dur -= eff.turns || 1; if (st.dur <= 0) K.removeStatus(ctx.b, t, id); } else K.reduceStatus(ctx.b, t, id, eff.turns || 1); } for (const m of t.mods) if (typeof m.dur === 'number' && m.amt > 0) m.dur = Math.max(1, m.dur - (eff.turns || 1)); }); };
  O.shorten_boons_aura = function (ctx, eff) {
    K.addHook(ctx.b, ctx.actor, { trigger: 'enemy_start_of_turn', caster: ctx.actor.id, dur: 'combat', aura: ctx.auraKey, fn: (b, u, e) => { if (e.other) O.shorten_boons({ b, actor: u }, { turns: eff.turns || 1 }, [e.other]); } });
  };
  O.shorten_status = function (ctx, eff, tg) { each(tg, (t) => { const st = t.statuses[eff.status]; if (st && typeof st.dur === 'number') st.dur = Math.max(1, st.dur - (eff.turns || 1)); }); };
  O.suppress_boons = function (ctx, eff, tg) { each(enemiesOf(ctx, tg), (t) => timed(t, 'suppressBoons', true, D(eff.duration, 2))); };
  O.block_boons = function (ctx, eff, tg) { each(enemiesOf(ctx, tg), (t) => timed(t, 'blockBoons', true, D(eff.duration, 2))); };
  O.block_boons_chance = function (ctx, eff, tg) { each(enemiesOf(ctx, tg).length ? enemiesOf(ctx, tg) : K.enemies(ctx.b, ctx.actor), (t) => timed(t, 'blockBoonsChance', (eff.percent || 25) / 100, ctx.passive ? 'combat' : 3)); };
  O.detonate_status = function (ctx, eff, tg) {
    const b = ctx.b, a = ctx.actor;
    each(tg, (t) => {
      const st = t.statuses[eff.status]; if (!st) return;
      const n = st.stacks;
      K.removeStatus(b, t, eff.status);
      if (eff.status === 'inscribed') K.fire(b, a, 'you_detonate_glyphs', { other: t, stacks: n });
      if (eff.status === 'doom') { EB.B.doomFalls(b, t, st); return; }
      const type = eff.status === 'resonance' ? 'sound' : eff.status === 'condemned' ? 'holy' : eff.status === 'heavy' ? 'force' : 'force';
      K.damage(b, a, t, (eff.powerPerStack || 0.15) * n * (ctx.power || 1) * K.hit(a, null, type), type, { canCrit: false });
    });
  };
  O.burst_status_now = function (ctx, eff, tg) {
    each(tg.length ? tg : [ctx.actor], (t) => { const fx = EB.FX[eff.status]; if (K.hasStatus(t, eff.status) && fx && fx.at) { const st = t.statuses[eff.status]; delete t.statuses[eff.status]; fx.at.fn(ctx.b, t, ctx.actor, st); } });
  };
  O.status_tick_now = function (ctx, eff, tg) { each(tg, (t) => EB.B.tick(ctx.b, t, eff.status, eff.times || 1)); };
  O.trigger_ticks = function (ctx, eff, tg) { each(tg, (t) => EB.B.tick(ctx.b, t, eff.status, eff.times || 1)); };
  O.tick_all_afflictions = function (ctx, eff, tg) { each(tg, (t) => { for (const id of K.afflictions(t)) if (EB.FX[id] && EB.FX[id].tickDmg) EB.B.tick(ctx.b, t, id, eff.times || 1); }); };
  O.spread_on_turn = function (ctx, eff, tg) {
    each(enemiesOf(ctx, tg), (t) => K.addHook(ctx.b, t, { trigger: 'start_of_turn', caster: ctx.actor.id, dur: 3, fn: (b, u) => { const p = K.allies(b, u); if (p.length && K.hasStatus(u, eff.status)) K.applyStatus(b, K.byId(b, u.statuses[eff.status].src), b.rng.pick(p), eff.status, eff.stacks || 1); } }));
  };
  O.on_hit_apply = function (ctx, eff, tg) {
    const holder = ctx.linked || ctx.actor;
    holder.flags.onHitApply = (holder.flags.onHitApply || []).concat([{ status: eff.status, stacks: eff.stacks || 1, chance: eff.chance }]);
    if (!ctx.passive && !ctx.linked) H.after(holder, 3, () => { holder.flags.onHitApply = (holder.flags.onHitApply || []).filter((x) => x.status !== eff.status); });
  };
  O.buff_next_attack = function (ctx, eff, tg) {
    const who = eff.to === 'linked' && ctx.linked ? ctx.linked : eff.to === 'owner' ? (K.byId(ctx.b, ctx.actor.owner) || ctx.actor) : (alliesOf(ctx, tg)[0] || ctx.actor);
    who.flags.buffNextAttack = { status: eff.status, add: eff.add || 1 };
  };
  O.guaranteed_crit = function (ctx, eff, tg) { each(alliesOf(ctx, tg).length ? alliesOf(ctx, tg) : [ctx.actor], (t) => { t.flags.guaranteedCrit = true; }); };
  O.guaranteed_crit_if_spent = function (ctx) { if ((ctx.consumed || 0) > 0) ctx.forceCrit = true; };
  O.crit_chance_bonus = function (ctx, eff) { const n = eff.per ? K.stacks(ctx.actor, eff.per) : 1; K.addMod(ctx.b, ctx.actor, 'crit', (eff.amount || 0.02) * n, ctx.passive ? 'combat' : 2, ctx.actor); };
  O.crit_heal_chance = function (ctx, eff) { const n = eff.per ? K.stacks(ctx.actor, eff.per) : 1; ctx.actor.flags.critHeal = Math.min(0.8, (eff.amount || 0.03) * Math.max(1, n)); };

  // ---------- signature sharing ----------
  O.share_signature = function (ctx, eff, tg) { shareStacks(ctx, tg, eff.status, 0.5); };
  O.share_signature_once = function (ctx, eff, tg) { for (const t of alliesOf(ctx, tg).length ? alliesOf(ctx, tg) : K.allies(ctx.b, ctx.actor)) K.applyStatus(ctx.b, ctx.actor, t, eff.status, eff.stacks || 1); };
  for (const [op, id] of [['share_bone_plating', 'bone_plating'], ['share_grit', 'grit'], ['share_kindled', 'kindled'], ['share_courage', 'courage'], ['share_static', 'static'], ['share_momentum', 'momentum'], ['share_ire', 'ire'], ['share_fortune', 'fortune'], ['share_coffers', 'coffers'], ['share_guard', 'guard'], ['share_regeneration', 'regenerating'], ['share_warded', 'warded'], ['share_fortified', 'fortified']]) {
    O[op] = function (ctx, eff, tg) {
      // linked sharing: give now and keep giving each turn the link lasts
      shareStacks(ctx, tg, id, 0.5);
      if (ctx.linked) K.addHook(ctx.b, ctx.actor, { trigger: 'start_of_turn', caster: ctx.actor.id, dur: 'combat', fn: (b, u) => { const l = ctx.linked; if (l && l.alive && K.stacks(u, id)) K.applyStatus(b, u, l, id, 1); } });
    };
  }
  O.share_stamina_regen = function (ctx, eff, tg) { for (const t of ctx.linked ? [ctx.linked] : K.allies(ctx.b, ctx.actor)) K.addMod(ctx.b, t, 'stamina_regen', 0.3, 'combat', ctx.actor); };
  O.share_tide_bonus = function (ctx, eff, tg) { for (const t of ctx.linked ? [ctx.linked] : K.allies(ctx.b, ctx.actor)) { t.flags.tide = true; t.flags.tideHigh = ctx.actor.flags.tideHigh; } };
  O.share_mask_bonus = function (ctx, eff, tg) { for (const t of ctx.linked ? [ctx.linked] : K.allies(ctx.b, ctx.actor)) O._applyMask(ctx.b, t, ctx.actor.flags.mask || 'grace', 0.5); };
  O.share_phase_bonus = function (ctx, eff, tg) { for (const t of ctx.linked ? [ctx.linked] : K.allies(ctx.b, ctx.actor)) { const ph = ctx.actor.statuses.moonphase; if (ph) K.applyStatus(ctx.b, ctx.actor, t, 'moonphase', 1); if (t.statuses.moonphase && ph) t.statuses.moonphase.phase = ph.phase; } };
  O.share_lifesteal = function (ctx, eff, tg) { for (const t of ctx.linked ? [ctx.linked] : K.allies(ctx.b, ctx.actor, true)) K.addMod(ctx.b, t, 'lifesteal', (eff.percent || 10) / 100, ctx.passive ? 'combat' : 3, ctx.actor); };
  O.lifesteal_from_all = function (ctx, eff) {
    const a = ctx.actor;
    for (const t of K.allies(ctx.b, a)) K.addHook(ctx.b, t, { trigger: 'when_you_hit', caster: a.id, dur: ctx.passive ? 'combat' : 3, fn: (b, u, e) => { if (a.alive) K.heal(b, a, a, K.hit(u) * (eff.percent || 10) / 100); } });
    K.addMod(ctx.b, a, 'lifesteal', (eff.percent || 10) / 100, ctx.passive ? 'combat' : 3, a);
  };
  O.share_healing = function (ctx, eff) { ctx.actor.flags.shareHealing = (eff.percent || 25) / 100; };
  O.share_mana_pool = function (ctx, eff, tg) {
    const p = ctx.linked || alliesOf(ctx, tg).find((t) => t !== ctx.actor);
    if (!p) return;
    const avg = Math.round((ctx.actor.mana + p.mana) / 2);
    ctx.actor.mana = Math.min(K.maxMana(ctx.actor), avg); p.mana = Math.min(K.maxMana(p), avg);
    K.addHook(ctx.b, ctx.actor, { trigger: 'start_of_turn', caster: ctx.actor.id, dur: 'combat', fn: (b, u) => { if (p.alive) { const m = Math.round((u.mana + p.mana) / 2); u.mana = Math.min(K.maxMana(u), m); p.mana = Math.min(K.maxMana(p), m); } } });
  };
  O.share_damage = function (ctx, eff, tg) {
    const e = ev(ctx), a = ctx.actor;
    const pct = (eff.percent || 30) / 100;
    if (ctx.hook && e.amount && ctx.hookListener && ctx.hookListener !== a) {
      const part = Math.round(e.amount * pct);
      K.heal(ctx.b, a, ctx.hookListener, part);
      K.loseHp(ctx.b, a, part, e.other);
      return;
    }
    for (const t of alliesOf(ctx, tg).filter((x) => x !== a)) t.flags.shareDamage = (t.flags.shareDamage || []).concat([{ with: a.id, pct }]);
  };
  O.share_damage_among_allies = function (ctx, eff) {
    const al = K.allies(ctx.b, ctx.actor, true);
    const pct = (eff.percent || 50) / 100 / Math.max(1, al.length - 1);
    for (const t of al) t.flags.shareDamage = al.filter((x) => x !== t).map((x) => ({ with: x.id, pct }));
    H.after(ctx.actor, 3, () => { for (const t of al) t.flags.shareDamage = null; });
  };
  O.share_damage_taken = function (ctx, eff, tg) {
    const w = enemiesOf(ctx, tg)[0] || tg[0];
    if (!w) return;
    ctx.actor.flags.shareDamage = (ctx.actor.flags.shareDamage || []).concat([{ with: w.id, pct: (eff.percent || 30) / 100 }]);
  };
  O.link_damage = function (ctx, eff, tg) {
    const a = ctx.actor, w = enemiesOf(ctx, tg)[0] || tg.find((x) => x !== a);
    if (!w) return;
    K.addHook(ctx.b, a, { trigger: 'when_take_damage', caster: a.id, dur: D(eff.duration, 3), fn: (b, u, e) => { if (w.alive && !e.dot) K.damage(b, u, w, e.amount * (eff.percent || 50) / 100, e.type || 'physical', { raw: true, canCrit: false, dot: true }); } });
  };
  O.chained_full_share = function (ctx, eff) { ctx.b.flags.chainedFull = true; H.after(ctx.actor, D(eff.duration, 2), () => { ctx.b.flags.chainedFull = false; }); };
  O.spill_full_damage_to_chained = function (ctx) { ctx.b.flags.chainedFull = true; H.after(ctx.actor, 1, () => { ctx.b.flags.chainedFull = false; }); };
  O.transfer_signature = function (ctx, eff, tg) {
    const a = ctx.actor; const n = K.stacks(a, eff.status); if (!n) return;
    K.removeStatus(ctx.b, a, eff.status);
    each(tg.filter((t) => t !== a).slice(0, 1), (t) => K.applyStatus(ctx.b, a, t, eff.status, n));
  };
  O.lose_signature = function (ctx, eff) { if (K.hasStatus(ctx.actor, eff.status)) K.removeStatus(ctx.b, ctx.actor, eff.status); };
  O.pay_signature = function (ctx, eff, tg) {
    const from = eff.from === 'target' ? (tg.find((t) => !K.isEnemy(ctx.actor, t)) || ctx.actor) : ctx.actor;
    const n = Math.min(K.stacks(from, eff.status), eff.count || 1);
    if (n > 0) K.reduceStatus(ctx.b, from, eff.status, n);
    ctx.paid = n;
  };
  O.double_signature = function (ctx, eff) { const a = ctx.actor; const n = K.stacks(a, eff.status); if (n) K.applyStatus(ctx.b, a, a, eff.status, Math.min(n, (eff.max || 10) - n)); };
  O.spend_signature_for_extra_action = function (ctx, eff) {
    const a = ctx.actor; const n = eff.count || 2;
    const holder = (ctx.targets || []).find((t) => !K.isEnemy(a, t) && K.stacks(t, eff.status) >= n) || (K.stacks(a, eff.status) >= n ? a : null);
    if (!holder) return;
    K.reduceStatus(ctx.b, holder, eff.status, n);
    holder.flags.extraActions = (holder.flags.extraActions || 0) + 1;
    if (holder !== a) holder.t = ctx.b.time + 0.01;
  };

  // ---------- bonuses that scale the rest of the ability ----------
  O.bonus_per_status_count = function (ctx, eff, tg) {
    const b = ctx.b, a = ctx.actor;
    const scope = eff.scope === 'enemy_all' ? K.enemies(b, a) : eff.scope === 'target' ? tg : [a];
    const id = eff.status || eff.signature;
    let n = 0; for (const u of scope) n += K.stacks(u, id);
    n = Math.floor(n / (eff.per && eff.per > 1 && !eff.resource ? eff.per : 1));
    if (eff.power) addPower(ctx, Math.min(eff.max || 1, eff.power * n));
    if (eff.resource) { const amt = Math.min(eff.max || 99, (eff.per || 1) * n); K.restore(b, a, eff.resource, Math.round((eff.resource === 'mana' ? K.maxMana(a) : K.maxSta(a)) * amt / 100)); }
    if (eff.shield) K.addShield(b, a, K.hit(a) * eff.shield * n, 2, a);
    if (eff.signature) K.applyStatus(b, a, a, eff.signature, Math.min(eff.max || 5, n));
  };
  O.bonus_per_affliction_count = function (ctx, eff) {
    const a = ctx.actor;
    let n = 0; for (const u of K.enemies(ctx.b, a)) n += K.afflictions(u).length;
    const amt = Math.min(eff.max || 20, (eff.per || 1) * n);
    if (eff.resource) K.restore(ctx.b, a, eff.resource, Math.round((eff.resource === 'mana' ? K.maxMana(a) : K.maxSta(a)) * amt / 100));
  };
  O.bonus_per_affliction = function (ctx, eff, tg) { const t = tg[0]; if (t) addPower(ctx, Math.min(eff.max || 0.5, (eff.power || 0.1) * K.afflictions(t).length)); };
  O.bonus_per_chained_enemy = function (ctx, eff) { const n = K.enemies(ctx.b, ctx.actor).filter((x) => K.hasStatus(x, 'chained')).length; addPower(ctx, Math.min(eff.max || 0.5, (eff.power || 0.1) * n)); };
  O.bonus_per_turn_elapsed = function (ctx, eff) { addPower(ctx, Math.min(eff.max || 0.5, (eff.power || 0.05) * ctx.actor.turns)); };
  O.bonus_per_missing_health = function (ctx, eff) { const miss = (1 - K.hpPct(ctx.actor)) * 100; addPower(ctx, Math.min(eff.max || 0.6, (eff.power || 0.1) * Math.floor(miss / (eff.per || 10)))); };
  O.heal_bonus_per_relationship = function (ctx) { addPower(ctx, 0.05 * (ctx.b.env.bonds || 2)); };

  // ---------- execute ----------
  O.execute = function (ctx, eff, tg) {
    const b = ctx.b, a = ctx.actor;
    each(enemiesOf(ctx, tg), (t) => {
      const th = (eff.threshold || 0.2) + K.stat(a, 'execute_threshold') + (ctx.executeAdd || 0);
      if (K.hpPct(t) > th) return;
      if (t.boss) { K.damage(b, a, t, K.maxHp(t) * 0.2, 'physical', { raw: true, canCrit: false }); return; }
      K.say(b, `${a.name} executes ${t.name}!`, a.side === 'ally' ? 'good' : 'bad');
      K.damage(b, a, t, t.hp + K.shieldTotal(t) + 1, 'physical', { raw: true, canCrit: false, bypassShield: true });
    });
  };
  O.execute_if_below = function (ctx, eff, tg) {
    each(enemiesOf(ctx, tg), (t) => {
      let pct = (eff.percent_base || 15) + (eff.per_stack ? K.stacks(t, eff.per_stack) * (eff.per_stack_percent || 3) : 0);
      O.execute(ctx, { threshold: pct / 100 }, [t]);
    });
  };

  // ---------- movement ----------
  O.swap_row = function (ctx, eff, tg) {
    const list = eff.to === 'target' ? tg : [ctx.actor];
    each(list, (t) => { if (eff.optional && t === ctx.actor && t.row === 'rear' && false) return; if (!EB.B.canMove(ctx.b, t) && t === ctx.actor) return; EB.B.moveRow(ctx.b, t, t.row === 'vanguard' ? 'rear' : 'vanguard', K.isEnemy(ctx.actor, t)); });
  };
  O.move_to_row = function (ctx, eff, tg) {
    const list = eff.to === 'target' ? tg : [ctx.actor];
    each(list, (t) => {
      let row = eff.row;
      if (row === 'any') row = t.row === 'vanguard' ? 'rear' : 'vanguard';
      if (row === 'enemy_vanguard' || row === 'target_row') { const tt = (ctx.targets || [])[0]; row = row === 'target_row' && tt ? tt.row : 'vanguard'; }
      if (t === ctx.actor && !EB.B.canMove(ctx.b, t)) return;
      EB.B.moveRow(ctx.b, t, row, K.isEnemy(ctx.actor, t));
    });
  };
  O.teleport = function (ctx, eff) { const a = ctx.actor; if (a.flags.noTeleport) return; EB.B.moveRow(ctx.b, a, a.row === 'vanguard' ? 'rear' : 'vanguard'); };
  O.push = function (ctx, eff, tg) {
    const list = eff.target === 'attacker' ? [ev(ctx).other].filter(Boolean) : eff.target === 'actor' ? [ctx.actor] : enemiesOf(ctx, tg);
    each(list, (t) => EB.B.moveRow(ctx.b, t, eff.to || 'rear', true));
  };
  O.push_row = function (ctx, eff, tg) {
    if (eff.chance != null && ctx.b.rng() >= eff.chance) return;
    const list = eff.target === 'attacker' ? [ev(ctx).other].filter(Boolean) : enemiesOf(ctx, tg);
    each(list, (t) => EB.B.moveRow(ctx.b, t, eff.to || 'rear', true));
  };
  O.pull_row = function (ctx, eff, tg) {
    const list = eff.target === 'attacker' ? [ev(ctx).other].filter(Boolean) : eff.target === 'enemy_all' ? K.enemies(ctx.b, ctx.actor) : enemiesOf(ctx, tg);
    each(list, (t) => { if (eff.chance != null && ctx.b.rng() >= eff.chance) return; EB.B.moveRow(ctx.b, t, eff.to || 'vanguard', true); });
  };
  O.pull_to_vanguard = function (ctx, eff, tg) { each(enemiesOf(ctx, tg), (t) => { if (eff.chance == null || ctx.b.rng() < eff.chance) EB.B.moveRow(ctx.b, t, 'vanguard', true); }); };
  O.swap_positions = function (ctx, eff, tg) {
    const b = ctx.b;
    each(enemiesOf(ctx, tg), (t) => {
      const other = eff.with === 'self' ? ctx.actor : b.rng.pick(K.allies(b, t).filter((x) => x.row !== t.row)) || null;
      if (!other) return;
      if (other === ctx.actor) { const r = t.row; EB.B.moveRow(b, t, ctx.actor.row, true); EB.B.moveRow(b, ctx.actor, r); }
      else { const r = t.row; EB.B.moveRow(b, t, other.row, true); EB.B.moveRow(b, other, r, true); }
    });
  };
  O.swap_places = function (ctx, eff, tg) { const t = tg.find((x) => x !== ctx.actor); if (!t) return; const r = ctx.actor.row; EB.B.moveRow(ctx.b, ctx.actor, t.row); EB.B.moveRow(ctx.b, t, r, K.isEnemy(ctx.actor, t)); };
  O.move_next_to = function (ctx, eff, tg) { const t = tg[0]; if (t && !K.isEnemy(ctx.actor, t)) EB.B.moveRow(ctx.b, ctx.actor, t.row); else if (t) EB.B.moveRow(ctx.b, ctx.actor, 'vanguard'); };
  O.move_behind = function (ctx, eff, tg) { const t = enemiesOf(ctx, tg)[0]; if (!t) return; ctx.actor.flags.guaranteedCrit = true; K.applyStatus(ctx.b, ctx.actor, t, 'exposed', 1, { dur: 1 }); say(ctx, `${ctx.actor.name} slips behind ${t.name}.`, 'dim'); };
  O.shuffle_rows = function (ctx, eff, tg) { const b = ctx.b; const side = (enemiesOf(ctx, tg)[0] || K.enemies(b, ctx.actor)[0] || {}).side; if (!side) return; for (const u of b.units) if (u.alive && u.side === side) EB.B.moveRow(b, u, b.rng() < 0.5 ? 'vanguard' : 'rear', true); say(ctx, 'The enemy ranks are thrown into disarray.', 'good'); };
  O.rearrange_rows = function (ctx) { for (const u of K.allies(ctx.b, ctx.actor, true)) EB.B.moveRow(ctx.b, u, K.hpPct(u) < 0.5 || u.kind === 'familiar' && u.row === 'rear' ? 'rear' : 'vanguard'); };
  O.return_to_position = function (ctx, eff) { const a = ctx.actor; const r = (a.rowHistory || [])[eff.turnsAgo || 1]; if (r) EB.B.moveRow(ctx.b, a, r); for (const id of ['rooted', 'webbed']) if (K.hasStatus(a, id)) K.removeStatus(ctx.b, a, id); };
  O.shuffle_self_with_decoys = function (ctx) { const a = ctx.actor; if (K.allies(ctx.b, a).some((x) => x.kind === 'decoy')) { K.applyStatus(ctx.b, a, a, 'evasive', 1); EB.B.moveRow(ctx.b, a, ctx.b.rng() < 0.5 ? 'rear' : 'vanguard'); } };
  O.block_movement = function (ctx, eff, tg) { each(enemiesOf(ctx, tg), (t) => timed(t, 'movementBlocked', true, D(eff.duration, 2))); };
  O.block_retreat = O.block_movement;
  O.block_teleport = function (ctx, eff, tg) { each(enemiesOf(ctx, tg).length ? enemiesOf(ctx, tg) : K.enemies(ctx.b, ctx.actor), (t) => timed(t, 'noTeleport', true, D(eff.duration, 3))); };
  O.lock_teleport = O.block_teleport; O.block_teleport_into = O.block_teleport;
  O.fly = function (ctx, eff, tg) { each(alliesOf(ctx, tg).length ? alliesOf(ctx, tg) : [ctx.actor], (t) => { if (t.flags.noFly) return; timed(t, 'flying', true, D(eff.duration, 2)); t.flags.flyingTimed = true; say(ctx, `${t.name} takes to the air.`, 'dim'); }); };
  O.block_flight = function (ctx, eff, tg) { each(enemiesOf(ctx, tg), (t) => { t.flags.flying = false; timed(t, 'noFly', true, D(eff.duration, 2)); }); };
  O.remove_gravity = function (ctx, eff, tg) { each(enemiesOf(ctx, tg), (t) => { t.flags.flying = false; timed(t, 'movementBlocked', true, D(eff.duration, 1)); K.addMod(ctx.b, t, 'evasion', -0.3, D(eff.duration, 1), ctx.actor); t.t += 15; }); };
  O.row_change_cost = function (ctx, eff, tg) { each(enemiesOf(ctx, tg).length ? enemiesOf(ctx, tg) : K.enemies(ctx.b, ctx.actor), (t) => timed(t, 'rowChangeCost', eff.stamina || 10, 3)); };
  O.on_move_damage = function (ctx, eff, tg) { each(enemiesOf(ctx, tg).length ? enemiesOf(ctx, tg) : K.enemies(ctx.b, ctx.actor), (t) => timed(t, 'onMoveDamage', { src: ctx.actor.id, power: (eff.power || 0.4) * (ctx.power || 1) }, 3)); };
  O.can_swap_free = function (ctx) { ctx.actor.flags.canSwapFree = true; };

  // ---------- control ----------
  O.control = function (ctx, eff, tg) { each(enemiesOf(ctx, tg), (t) => { if (t.boss) { K.applyStatus(ctx.b, ctx.actor, t, 'confused', 1, { dur: 1 }); return; } t.flags.charmed = D(eff.duration, 1); say(ctx, `${ctx.actor.name} seizes control of ${t.name}.`, 'good'); }); };
  O.mind_control = O.control;
  O.skip_turn = function (ctx, eff, tg) { each(tg, (t) => { t.flags.skipNext = true; }); };
  O.lose_action_chance = function (ctx, eff, tg) { each(enemiesOf(ctx, tg), (t) => timed(t, 'loseActionChance', { p: (eff.percent || 25) / 100 }, D(eff.duration, 2))); };
  O.force_attack_ally = function (ctx, eff, tg) {
    each(enemiesOf(ctx, tg), (t) => {
      if (eff.chance != null && ctx.b.rng() >= eff.chance) return;
      const p = K.allies(ctx.b, t); if (!p.length) return;
      const v = ctx.b.rng.pick(p);
      say(ctx, `${t.name} turns on ${v.name}!`, 'good');
      K.damage(ctx.b, t, v, K.hit(t, null, 'physical') * 0.7, 'physical', {});
    });
  };
  O.random_retarget_chance = function (ctx, eff, tg) { each(enemiesOf(ctx, tg).length ? enemiesOf(ctx, tg) : K.enemies(ctx.b, ctx.actor), (t) => timed(t, 'randomRetarget', (eff.percent || 20) / 100, ctx.passive ? 'combat' : 2)); };
  O.taunt = function (ctx, eff, tg) { each(enemiesOf(ctx, tg), (t) => K.applyStatus(ctx.b, ctx.actor, t, 'taunted', 1, { dur: D(eff.duration, 2) })); };
  O.taunt_to_self = O.taunt;
  O.challenge = function (ctx, eff, tg) { each(enemiesOf(ctx, tg), (t) => { K.applyStatus(ctx.b, ctx.actor, t, 'taunted', 1, { dur: D(eff.duration, 2) }); K.addMod(ctx.b, ctx.actor, 'damage_vs_target', 0.15, D(eff.duration, 2), ctx.actor, { vs: t.id }); }); };
  O.challenge_on_behalf = function (ctx, eff, tg) {
    const ally = alliesOf(ctx, tg).find((x) => x !== ctx.actor) || ctx.actor;
    for (const e of K.enemies(ctx.b, ctx.actor)) if (e.intent && e.intent.target === ally.id) K.applyStatus(ctx.b, ctx.actor, e, 'taunted', 1, { dur: D(eff.duration, 2) });
  };
  O.drop_aggro = function (ctx) { timed(ctx.actor, 'untargetableByAI', true, 2); };
  O.drop_aggro_from = function (ctx) { for (const e of K.enemies(ctx.b, ctx.actor)) K.applyStatus(ctx.b, ctx.actor, e, 'taunted', 1, { dur: 1 }); };
  O.taunt_reverse = function (ctx, eff) { timed(ctx.actor, 'untargetableByAI', true, D(eff.duration, 2)); };
  O.untargetable_by_ai = function (ctx, eff, tg) { each(alliesOf(ctx, tg).length ? alliesOf(ctx, tg) : [ctx.actor], (t) => timed(t, 'untargetableByAI', true, D(eff.duration, 2))); };
  O.pull_aggro_to_nothing = function (ctx, eff) { for (const e of K.enemies(ctx.b, ctx.actor)) timed(e, 'attacksMiss', true, D(eff.duration, 1)); say(ctx, 'The enemy swings at shadows.', 'good'); };
  O.cannot_target = function (ctx, eff, tg) {
    const a = ctx.actor;
    each(enemiesOf(ctx, tg), (t) => { a.flags.cannotTargetBy = Object.assign({}, a.flags.cannotTargetBy || {}, { [t.id]: true }); H.after(a, D(eff.duration, 2) === 'combat' ? 99 : D(eff.duration, 2), () => { if (a.flags.cannotTargetBy) delete a.flags.cannotTargetBy[t.id]; }); });
  };
  O.untargetable_by = function (ctx, eff) { ctx.actor.flags.untargetableBy = (ctx.actor.flags.untargetableBy || []).concat([eff.creatureType]); };
  O.appear_as_enemy_ally = function (ctx, eff) { timed(ctx.actor, 'untargetableByAI', true, D(eff.duration, 2)); };
  O.disguise_as = O.appear_as_enemy_ally;
  O.appear_dead = function (ctx, eff) { timed(ctx.actor, 'untargetableByAI', true, D(eff.duration, 2)); K.applyStatus(ctx.b, ctx.actor, ctx.actor, 'stealthed', 1, { dur: D(eff.duration, 2) }); say(ctx, `${ctx.actor.name} plays dead.`, 'dim'); };
  O.swap_appearance = function (ctx, eff, tg) { const w = enemiesOf(ctx, tg)[0] || K.enemies(ctx.b, ctx.actor)[0]; if (w) timed(ctx.actor, 'untargetableByAI', true, 2); };
  O.falsify_intent = function (ctx, eff) { timed(ctx.actor, 'falseIntent', true, D(eff.duration, 2)); for (const e of K.enemies(ctx.b, ctx.actor)) if (e.intent && e.intent.target === ctx.actor.id) EB.AI && EB.AI.plan(ctx.b, e, { avoid: ctx.actor.id }); };
  O.duel = function (ctx, eff, tg) { const t = enemiesOf(ctx, tg)[0]; if (!t) return; timed(ctx.actor, 'duelWith', t.id, D(eff.duration, 3)); timed(t, 'duelWith', ctx.actor.id, D(eff.duration, 3)); say(ctx, `${ctx.actor.name} and ${t.name} square off.`, 'sys'); };
  O.isolate = function (ctx, eff, tg) { each(enemiesOf(ctx, tg), (t) => { timed(t, 'isolated', true, D(eff.duration, 2)); K.addMod(ctx.b, t, 'damage_taken', 0.1, D(eff.duration, 2), ctx.actor); }); };
  O.banish = function (ctx, eff, tg) { each(enemiesOf(ctx, tg), (t) => { if (t.boss && eff.excludeBosses !== false) { t.t += 40; return; } X.banish(ctx.b, t, D(eff.duration, 1) === 'combat' ? 3 : D(eff.duration, 1)); }); };
  O.polymorph = function (ctx, eff, tg) {
    each(enemiesOf(ctx, tg), (t) => {
      if (t.boss || (eff.chance != null && ctx.b.rng() >= eff.chance)) return;
      const d = D(eff.duration, 2);
      K.addMod(ctx.b, t, 'damage', -0.8, d, ctx.actor); K.addMod(ctx.b, t, 'damage_taken', 0.2, d, ctx.actor);
      timed(t, 'polymorphed', eff.into || 'toad', d); timed(t, 'noSpells', true, d);
      say(ctx, `${t.name} becomes a ${String(eff.into || 'toad').replace(/_/g, ' ')}!`, 'good');
    });
  };
  O.calm = function (ctx, eff, tg) {
    each(enemiesOf(ctx, tg), (t) => { if (t.boss || (eff.chance != null && ctx.b.rng() >= eff.chance)) { K.applyStatus(ctx.b, ctx.actor, t, 'drowsy', 1); return; } t.flags.skipNext = true; say(ctx, `${t.name} calms and lowers its guard.`, 'good'); });
  };
  O.silence_strongest = function (ctx, eff) { const t = X.strongest(K.enemies(ctx.b, ctx.actor)); if (t) K.applyStatus(ctx.b, ctx.actor, t, 'silenced', 1, { dur: D(eff.duration, 2) }); };
  O.forbid_ability_type = function (ctx, eff, tg) { each(enemiesOf(ctx, tg), (t) => timed(t, 'forbidType', eff.type, D(eff.duration, 2))); };
  O.block_transform = function (ctx, eff, tg) { each(enemiesOf(ctx, tg), (t) => { O._endForm(ctx.b, t); timed(t, 'transformBlocked', true, D(eff.duration, 2)); }); };
  O.block_parry = function (ctx, eff, tg) { timed(ctx.actor, 'blockParry', 1, D(eff.duration, 1)); each(enemiesOf(ctx, tg), (t) => { t.flags.parryNext = null; timed(t, 'noParry', true, D(eff.duration, 1)); }); };
  O.block_flee = function (ctx, eff, tg) { for (const t of K.enemies(ctx.b, ctx.actor)) t.flags.blockFlee = true; };
  O.flee_bonus = function (ctx, eff) { ctx.actor.flags.fleeBonus = (ctx.actor.flags.fleeBonus || 0) + (eff.amount || 0.2); };
  O.increase_cooldowns = function (ctx, eff, tg) { each(enemiesOf(ctx, tg), (t) => { for (const s of t.abilities) if (!s.def.passive) s.cd += eff.turns || 1; }); };
  O.increase_cooldowns_aura = function (ctx, eff) { for (const e of K.enemies(ctx.b, ctx.actor)) K.addMod(ctx.b, e, 'cd_reduction', -(eff.percent || 25) / 100, 'combat', ctx.actor, { aura: ctx.auraKey }); };
  O.increase_spell_costs = function (ctx, eff, tg) { each(enemiesOf(ctx, tg).length ? enemiesOf(ctx, tg) : K.enemies(ctx.b, ctx.actor), (t) => K.addMod(ctx.b, t, 'spell_cost', (eff.percent || 25) / 100, D(eff.duration, 3), ctx.actor)); };
  O.backfire_chance = function (ctx, eff, tg) { each(enemiesOf(ctx, tg), (t) => timed(t, 'backfire', (eff.percent || 20) / 100, D(eff.duration, 2))); };
  O.penalty_for_tricks = function (ctx, eff, tg) { each(enemiesOf(ctx, tg).length ? enemiesOf(ctx, tg) : K.enemies(ctx.b, ctx.actor), (t) => { t.flags.trickPenalty = { src: ctx.actor.id, status: eff.status || 'guilt', stacks: eff.stacks || 1 }; }); };
  O.contract = function (ctx, eff, tg) { each(enemiesOf(ctx, tg), (t) => { timed(t, 'contract', { src: ctx.actor.id, penalty: eff.penalty || [], terms: eff.terms, power: ctx.power }, D(eff.duration, 3)); say(ctx, `${t.name} is bound by contract.`, 'dim'); }); };
  O.offer_deal = function (ctx, eff, tg) {
    const b = ctx.b;
    each(enemiesOf(ctx, tg), (t) => {
      const desperate = K.hpPct(t) < 0.5;
      const accept = !t.boss && b.rng() < (desperate ? 0.65 : 0.3) + 0.03 * K.stacks(t, 'temptation') + 0.04 * K.stacks(t, 'duped');
      say(ctx, `${ctx.actor.name} offers ${t.name} a deal. It ${accept ? 'accepts' : 'refuses'}.`, accept ? 'good' : 'dim');
      const sub = X.sub(ctx, { targets: [t], dealTarget: t });
      X.run(sub, (accept ? eff.accept : eff.refuse) || [], [t]);
    });
  };
  O.flee_combat = function (ctx, eff, tg) {
    const b = ctx.b;
    const who = ctx.dealTarget || (tg[0] && K.isEnemy(ctx.actor, tg[0]) ? tg[0] : ctx.actor);
    if (who.isPlayer) { b.over = true; b.winner = 'fled'; K.log(b, { k: 'end', winner: 'fled' }); return; }
    who.alive = false; who.fled = true;
    K.log(b, { k: 'fade', tgt: who.id }); say(ctx, `${who.name} flees the fight.`, 'dim');
    K.checkEnd(b);
  };
  O.trap = function (ctx, eff, tg) {
    each(enemiesOf(ctx, tg).length ? enemiesOf(ctx, tg) : K.enemies(ctx.b, ctx.actor).slice(0, 1), (t) => { t.traps = (t.traps || []).concat([{ trigger: eff.trigger, effects: eff.effects, src: ctx.actor.id, power: ctx.power, chance: eff.percent != null ? eff.percent / 100 : null }]); });
    K.log(ctx.b, { k: 'text', msg: 'A trap is set.', cls: 'dim' });
  };
  O.trap_chance = function (ctx, eff, tg) { O.trap(ctx, { trigger: 'target_moves_or_acts', effects: eff.effects, percent: eff.percent || 30 }, tg); };
  O.trap_row = function (ctx, eff, tg) { const t = enemiesOf(ctx, tg)[0]; const row = t ? K.rowOf(ctx.b, t.side, t.row) : K.enemies(ctx.b, ctx.actor); O.trap(ctx, { trigger: eff.trigger, effects: eff.effects }, row); };
  O.terrain = function (ctx, eff) {
    const b = ctx.b;
    const effects = eff.kind === 'web' ? [{ op: 'apply_status', status: 'webbed', stacks: 1 }] : [{ op: 'apply_status', status: 'staggered', stacks: 1 }, { op: 'damage', type: 'physical', power: 0.1, scale: { might: 1 } }];
    b.zones.push({ id: U.uid('z'), inline: { name: eff.kind === 'web' ? 'Webbing' : 'Rubble', trigger: 'enemy_start_of_turn', effects }, name: eff.kind === 'web' ? 'Webbing' : 'Rubble', row: 'enemy_all', dur: D(eff.duration, 3), src: ctx.actor.id, power: ctx.power || 1, terrain: true });
    say(ctx, eff.kind === 'web' ? 'Webs cover the ground.' : 'Rubble litters the field.', 'dim');
  };
  O.terrain_destroy = function (ctx) { const b = ctx.b; b.zones = b.zones.filter((z) => { const s = K.byId(b, z.src); return !s || !K.isEnemy(ctx.actor, s); }); say(ctx, 'The enemy\'s ground is torn up.', 'good'); };
  O.suppress_enemy_auras = function (ctx, eff) {
    const b = ctx.b, a = ctx.actor;
    for (const u of K.allies(b, a, true)) u.mods = u.mods.filter((m) => { const s = K.byId(b, m.src); return !(m.aura && s && K.isEnemy(a, s)); });
    b.zones = b.zones.filter((z) => { const s = K.byId(b, z.src); return !s || !K.isEnemy(a, s); });
    say(ctx, 'Enemy auras gutter out.', 'good');
  };
  O.dispel_illusions = function (ctx, eff, tg) {
    const b = ctx.b;
    const list = eff.to === 'enemy_all' ? K.enemies(b, ctx.actor) : (enemiesOf(ctx, tg).length ? enemiesOf(ctx, tg) : [ev(ctx).other].filter(Boolean));
    for (const u of b.units) if (u.alive && u.kind === 'decoy' && K.isEnemy(ctx.actor, u)) { u.alive = false; K.log(b, { k: 'fade', tgt: u.id }); }
    each(list, (t) => { if (K.hasStatus(t, 'stealthed')) K.removeStatus(b, t, 'stealthed'); t.flags.untargetableByAI = null; t.flags.redirectToDecoy = 0; });
    K.checkEnd(b);
  };
  O.block_stealth = function (ctx, eff, tg) { each(enemiesOf(ctx, tg).length ? enemiesOf(ctx, tg) : K.enemies(ctx.b, ctx.actor), (t) => { if (K.hasStatus(t, 'stealthed')) K.removeStatus(ctx.b, t, 'stealthed'); timed(t, 'noStealth', true, D(eff.duration, 3)); }); };
  O.prevent_stealth = O.block_stealth; O.block_stealth_enemies = O.block_stealth;
  O.reveal_when_stealthed = function (ctx, eff) { ctx.actor.flags.seeStealth = true; O.block_stealth(ctx, eff, K.enemies(ctx.b, ctx.actor)); };

  // ---------- defensive resources ----------
  O.armor_break = function (ctx, eff, tg) { each(enemiesOf(ctx, tg).length ? enemiesOf(ctx, tg) : tg, (t) => { if (K.stacks(t, 'tempered') >= 8) return; K.addMod(ctx.b, t, 'armor', -(eff.percent || 10) / 100, D(eff.duration, 3), ctx.actor); }); };
  O.shield_strip = function (ctx, eff, tg) { each(enemiesOf(ctx, tg), (t) => { for (const s of t.shields) s.amt = Math.round(s.amt * (1 - (eff.percent || 50) / 100)); t.shields = t.shields.filter((s) => s.amt > 0); }); };
  O.remove_shields = function (ctx, eff, tg) { each(enemiesOf(ctx, tg), (t) => { if (t.shields.length) { t.shields = []; say(ctx, `${t.name}'s shields shatter.`, 'good'); } }); };
  O.restore_last_shield = function (ctx, eff) { const a = ctx.actor; if (a.lastShield) K.addShield(ctx.b, a, a.lastShield * (eff.power || 1), 2, a); };
  O.shield_grows = function (ctx, eff) { K.addHook(ctx.b, ctx.actor, { trigger: 'start_of_turn', caster: ctx.actor.id, dur: D(eff.duration, 3), fn: (b, u) => { for (const s of u.shields) s.amt = Math.round(s.amt * (1 + (eff.percent || 20) / 100)); } }); };
  O.regen_shield = function (ctx, eff) { K.addHook(ctx.b, ctx.actor, { trigger: 'start_of_turn', caster: ctx.actor.id, dur: D(eff.duration, 3), fn: (b, u) => K.addShield(b, u, K.maxHp(u) * (eff.percent || 5) / 100, 1, u) }); };
  O.regen = function (ctx, eff, tg) { each(alliesOf(ctx, tg).length ? alliesOf(ctx, tg) : [ctx.actor], (t) => timed(t, 'regenPct', (eff.percent || 5) / 100, D(eff.duration, 3))); };
  O.pay_health = function (ctx, eff, tg) {
    const a = ctx.actor;
    K.loseHp(ctx.b, a, K.maxHp(a) * (eff.percent || 5) / 100, a);
    if ((a.signatureStatuses || []).includes('bloodprice')) K.applyStatus(ctx.b, a, a, 'bloodprice', 1);
    if ((a.signatureStatuses || []).includes('atonement')) K.applyStatus(ctx.b, a, a, 'atonement', 1);
  };
  O.pay_resource = function (ctx, eff) { const a = ctx.actor; K.restore(ctx.b, a, eff.resource, -Math.round((eff.resource === 'mana' ? K.maxMana(a) : K.maxSta(a)) * (eff.percent || 10) / 100)); };
  O.heal_per_status = function (ctx, eff, tg) { const a = ctx.actor; let n = 0; for (const u of K.alive(ctx.b)) n += K.stacks(u, eff.status); K.heal(ctx.b, a, a, K.maxHp(a) * (eff.percent || 2) / 100 * n); };
  O.heal_per_affliction = function (ctx, eff) { const a = ctx.actor; let n = 0; for (const u of K.enemies(ctx.b, a)) n += K.afflictions(u).length; n = Math.min(n, eff.max || 10); K.heal(ctx.b, a, a, K.maxHp(a) * (eff.percent || 2) / 100 * n); };
  O.restore_health_to_turn = function (ctx, eff, tg) {
    each(alliesOf(ctx, tg).length ? alliesOf(ctx, tg) : [ctx.actor], (t) => {
      const past = (t.hpAtTurnStart || [])[eff.turnsAgo || 1];
      if (past != null && past > t.hp) K.heal(ctx.b, ctx.actor, t, Math.min(past - t.hp, K.maxHp(t) * (eff.max || 0.5)));
    });
  };
  O.undo_damage = function (ctx, eff, tg) { each(alliesOf(ctx, tg).length ? alliesOf(ctx, tg) : [ctx.actor], (t) => { const ld = t.lastDamage[0]; if (ld) K.heal(ctx.b, ctx.actor, t, ld.amt * (eff.percent || 50) / 100); }); };
  O.reset_last_damage_taken = O.undo_damage;
  O.repeat_last_damage_taken = function (ctx, eff, tg) {
    const a = ctx.actor; const ld = a.lastDamageTaken; if (!ld || !ld.amt) return;
    const t = enemiesOf(ctx, tg)[0] || ev(ctx).other || K.enemies(ctx.b, a)[0]; if (!t) return;
    K.damage(ctx.b, a, t, ld.amt * (eff.percent || 50) / 100, eff.type || ld.type, { raw: true, canCrit: false });
  };
  O.convert_damage_to_heal = function (ctx, eff, tg) {
    const e = ev(ctx);
    if (ctx.hook && e.amount) { K.heal(ctx.b, ctx.actor, ctx.hookListener || ctx.actor, e.amount * (eff.percent || 50) / 100); return; }
    K.addHook(ctx.b, ctx.actor, { trigger: 'when_take_damage', caster: ctx.actor.id, dur: 1, once: true, fn: (b, u, e2) => K.heal(b, u, u, e2.amount * (eff.percent || 50) / 100) });
  };
  O.convert_to_status = function (ctx, eff, tg) { const e = ev(ctx); const t = e.other && K.isEnemy(ctx.actor, e.other) ? e.other : enemiesOf(ctx, tg)[0]; if (t) K.applyStatus(ctx.b, ctx.actor, t, eff.status, Math.max(1, Math.round((e.amount || 10) / 10))); };
  O.store_healing = function (ctx) {
    const a = ctx.actor; a.flags.storeHealing = true;
    a.flags.onBattleEnd = (b) => { if (a.flags.stored) { a.flags.storeHealing = false; a.hp = Math.min(K.maxHp(a), a.hp + a.flags.stored); } };
  };
  O.repair_constructs = function (ctx, eff) { for (const u of K.allies(ctx.b, ctx.actor, true)) if (u.tags.has('construct')) K.heal(ctx.b, ctx.actor, u, K.maxHp(u) * (eff.percent || 20) / 100); };
  O.steal_stat = function (ctx, eff, tg) {
    each(enemiesOf(ctx, tg), (t) => {
      const p = (eff.percent || 10) / 100; const d = D(eff.duration, 3);
      K.addMod(ctx.b, t, eff.stat, -p, d, ctx.actor); K.addMod(ctx.b, ctx.actor, eff.stat, p, d, ctx.actor);
    });
  };
  O.copy_stats = function (ctx, eff, tg) {
    const t = enemiesOf(ctx, tg)[0] || tg[0]; if (!t) return;
    const a = ctx.actor;
    const stats = eff.stats || K.ATTRS;
    for (const s of stats) if (K.ATTRS.includes(s)) { const diff = K.attr(t, s) / Math.max(1, a.base[s]) - 1 - K.stat(a, s); if (diff > 0) K.addMod(ctx.b, a, s, diff, D(eff.duration, 3), a); }
  };

  // ---------- cooldowns & turns ----------
  O.reduce_cooldowns = function (ctx, eff, tg) {
    if (eff.chance != null && ctx.b.rng() >= eff.chance) return;
    const list = alliesOf(ctx, tg).length ? alliesOf(ctx, tg) : [ctx.actor];
    each(list, (t) => { for (const s of t.abilities) { if (eff.filterTags && !eff.filterTags.some((x) => (s.def.tags || []).includes(x))) continue; if (s.cd > 0) s.cd = Math.max(0, s.cd - (eff.amount || eff.turns || 1)); } });
  };
  O.reset_cooldown = function (ctx, eff, tg) {
    const list = eff.to === 'ally_random' ? [ctx.b.rng.pick(K.allies(ctx.b, ctx.actor, true))] : alliesOf(ctx, tg).length ? alliesOf(ctx, tg) : [ctx.actor];
    each(list, (t) => { const s = t.abilities.filter((x) => x.cd > 0 && (!ctx.slot || x !== ctx.slot)).sort((x, y) => y.cd - x.cd); for (const x of s.slice(0, eff.count || 1)) x.cd = 0; });
  };
  O.act_first_next_round = function (ctx, eff, tg) { each(alliesOf(ctx, tg).length ? alliesOf(ctx, tg) : [ctx.actor], (t) => { t.flags.actFirst = true; }); };
  O.act_now = function (ctx, eff, tg) { each(alliesOf(ctx, tg).filter((t) => t !== ctx.actor), (t) => { t.t = ctx.b.time + 0.01; say(ctx, `${t.name} springs into action.`, 'good'); }); };
  O.extra_turn = function (ctx, eff, tg) { each(alliesOf(ctx, tg).length ? alliesOf(ctx, tg) : [ctx.actor], (t) => { if (t === ctx.actor) t.flags.extraActions = (t.flags.extraActions || 0) + 1; else t.t = ctx.b.time + 0.01; }); };
  O.extra_action = function (ctx, eff, tg) {
    const b = ctx.b, a = ctx.actor;
    const list = alliesOf(ctx, tg).length ? alliesOf(ctx, tg) : [a];
    for (const t of list) {
      if (t === a) { a.flags.extraActions = (a.flags.extraActions || 0) + 1; continue; }
      // immediate action at reduced power
      O._actNow(b, t, eff.power || 0.5, eff.filter === 'attack');
    }
  };
  O._actNow = function (b, t, power, attackOnly) {
    if (!t.alive || !EB.AI) return;
    const plan = EB.AI.choose(b, t, { attackOnly });
    if (!plan) return;
    K.say(b, `${t.name} acts on the order!`, t.side === 'ally' ? 'good' : 'bad');
    if (plan.type === 'ability') {
      const slot = t.abilities[plan.slot];
      const tg = EB.B.resolveTargets(b, t, slot.def, plan.target ? K.byId(b, plan.target) : null);
      EB.B.execute(b, t, slot, tg, { power });
      slot.cd = slot.def.cooldown || 0;
    } else if (plan.type === 'attack') {
      const def = EB.B.weaponDef(t); const slot = { def, id: 'weapon', cd: 0 };
      EB.B.execute(b, t, slot, EB.B.resolveTargets(b, t, def, plan.target ? K.byId(b, plan.target) : null), { power });
    }
  };
  O.familiars_act_now = function (ctx, eff) { for (const f of K.allies(ctx.b, ctx.actor).filter((x) => x.owner === ctx.actor.id && (x.kind === 'familiar' || x.kind === 'conjuration'))) O._actNow(ctx.b, f, eff.power || 1); };
  O.summons_act_now = O.familiars_act_now;
  O.act_together = function (ctx) { O.familiars_act_now(ctx, { power: 0.75 }); };
  O.familiar_follow_up = function (ctx, eff, tg) {
    const t = enemiesOf(ctx, tg)[0] || K.enemies(ctx.b, ctx.actor)[0]; if (!t) return;
    for (const f of K.allies(ctx.b, ctx.actor).filter((x) => x.owner === ctx.actor.id && x.kind === 'familiar')) { K.say(ctx.b, `${f.name} follows up.`, 'dim'); K.damage(ctx.b, f, t, K.hit(f) * (eff.power || 0.5), 'physical', { melee: true }); }
  };
  O.familiars_focus = function (ctx, eff, tg) { const t = enemiesOf(ctx, tg)[0]; if (!t) return; for (const f of K.allies(ctx.b, ctx.actor).filter((x) => x.owner === ctx.actor.id)) { f.order = { focus: t.id }; timed(f, 'focus', t.id, D(eff.duration, 3)); } };
  O.copy_last_ability = function (ctx, eff, tg) {
    const b = ctx.b, a = ctx.actor;
    const src = tg.find((t) => K.isEnemy(a, t)) || tg[0];
    const last = src && src.lastAbility; if (!last) return;
    const def = last.def;
    const slot = { def, id: 'copy', cd: 0 };
    const t2 = EB.B.resolveTargets(b, a, def, EB.B.hostileTarget(def.target) ? src : a);
    K.say(b, `${a.name} mimics ${def.name}!`, 'good');
    EB.B.execute(b, a, slot, t2, { power: eff.power || 0.75 });
  };
  O.recast_last = function (ctx, eff) {
    const a = ctx.actor;
    const last = (a.prevAbility && a.prevAbility.def !== ctx.ab) ? a.prevAbility : null;
    const prevDef = a.history.length >= 2 ? EB.content.abilities[a.history[a.history.length - 2]] : null;
    const slot = prevDef && a.abilities.find((s) => s.id === prevDef.id);
    if (!slot) return;
    const tg = EB.B.resolveTargets(ctx.b, a, slot.def, (ctx.targets || [])[0]);
    K.say(ctx.b, `${slot.def.name} rings out again.`, 'dim');
    EB.B.execute(ctx.b, a, slot, tg, { power: eff.power || 0.5, echo: true });
  };
  O.repeat_target_last_action = function (ctx, eff, tg) {
    const b = ctx.b;
    each(enemiesOf(ctx, tg), (t) => {
      const last = t.lastAbility; if (!last) return;
      const p = K.allies(b, t); if (!p.length) return;
      const v = b.rng.pick(p);
      K.say(b, `${t.name} repeats ${last.def.name} — at ${v.name}!`, 'good');
      EB.B.execute(b, t, { def: last.def, id: 'repeat', cd: 0 }, [v], { power: 0.75 });
    });
  };
  O.copy_ability = function (ctx, eff, tg) {
    const a = ctx.actor;
    const pool = []; for (const e of K.enemies(ctx.b, a)) for (const s of e.abilities) if (!s.def.passive && s.def.type !== 'familiar' && !s.form) pool.push(s.def);
    if (!pool.length) return;
    const d = ctx.b.rng.pick(pool);
    const slot = EB.K.learn(a, d, a.rank);
    slot.usesLeft = eff.uses || 1; slot.copied = true;
    K.say(ctx.b, `${a.name} learns ${d.name} for a moment.`, 'good');
  };

  // ---------- damage variants ----------
  O.chain = function (ctx, eff, tg) {
    const b = ctx.b, a = ctx.actor;
    let type = 'lightning'; K.mapEffects((ctx.ab && ctx.ab.effects) || [], (e) => { if (e.op === 'damage' && type === 'lightning' && e.type) type = e.type; return e; });
    let pool = K.enemies(b, a).filter((x) => !tg.includes(x));
    if (eff.prefer === 'charged') pool.sort((x, y) => K.stacks(y, 'charged') - K.stacks(x, 'charged'));
    else pool = b.rng.shuffle(pool);
    for (const t of pool.slice(0, eff.jumps || 2)) K.damage(b, a, t, (eff.power || 0.4) * (ctx.power || 1) * K.hit(a, null, type), type, { spell: true, canCrit: true });
  };
  O.chain_from_corpse = function (ctx, eff, tg) {
    if (!ctx.b.corpses.length) return;
    const pool = ctx.b.rng.shuffle(K.enemies(ctx.b, ctx.actor)).slice(0, eff.jumps || 2);
    for (const t of pool) K.damage(ctx.b, ctx.actor, t, (eff.power || 0.4) * (ctx.power || 1) * K.hit(ctx.actor), 'necrotic', {});
  };
  O.pierce_to_row_behind = function (ctx, eff, tg) {
    const t = enemiesOf(ctx, tg)[0]; if (!t || t.row !== 'vanguard') return;
    for (const r of K.rowOf(ctx.b, t.side, 'rear')) K.damage(ctx.b, ctx.actor, r, (eff.power || 0.5) * (ctx.power || 1) * K.hit(ctx.actor, null, 'physical'), 'physical', { ranged: true });
  };
  O.random_damage = function (ctx, eff, tg) {
    const p = (eff.min || 0.2) + ctx.b.rng() * ((eff.max || 1) - (eff.min || 0.2));
    O.damage(ctx, { type: eff.type || 'force', power: p }, tg);
  };
  O.devour_summon = function (ctx, eff, tg) {
    const b = ctx.b, a = ctx.actor;
    const s = K.enemies(b, a).find((x) => x.kind === 'conjuration' || x.kind === 'decoy') || K.allies(b, a).find((x) => x.kind === 'conjuration' && x.owner === a.id);
    if (!s) return;
    const hp = s.hp; s.hp = 0; s.alive = false; K.log(b, { k: 'death', tgt: s.id, src: a.id });
    K.heal(b, a, a, hp); say(ctx, `${a.name} devours ${s.name}.`, 'dim'); K.checkEnd(b);
  };
  O.sacrifice_summon = function (ctx, eff) {
    const b = ctx.b, a = ctx.actor;
    const mine = K.allies(b, a).filter((x) => x.owner === a.id && (x.kind === 'conjuration' || x.kind === 'decoy'));
    let n = 0;
    for (const s of mine.slice(0, eff.count || 1)) { s.alive = false; K.log(b, { k: 'fade', tgt: s.id }); n++; }
    if (n) { say(ctx, `${a.name} spends ${n === 1 ? 'a summon' : n + ' summons'}.`, 'dim'); addPower(ctx, 0.25 * n); }
  };
  O.self_destroy_and_rebuild = function (ctx, eff) {
    const b = ctx.b, a = ctx.actor;
    for (const e of K.enemies(b, a)) K.damage(b, a, e, K.hit(a) * 0.8 * (ctx.power || 1), 'force', { aoe: true });
    X.banish(b, a, eff.turns || 1);
    a.flags.rebuild = true;
    H.after(a, (eff.turns || 1) + 1, () => { a.hp = K.maxHp(a); say(ctx, `${a.name} reassembles.`, 'dim'); });
  };
  O.split = function (ctx, eff) {
    const b = ctx.b, a = ctx.actor;
    for (let i = 0; i < (eff.count || 2); i++) {
      const u = K.makeUnit(b, { name: a.name + ' (copy)', side: a.side, row: a.row, kind: 'conjuration', owner: a.id, attrs: Object.assign({}, a.base), maxHp: Math.round(K.maxHp(a) * 0.25), powerScale: 0.5, duration: D(eff.duration, 3), tags: ['summon'] });
      u.flags.summoned = true;
      EB.B.addUnit(b, u); u.t = b.time + 30;
    }
    say(ctx, `${a.name} splits apart.`, 'dim');
  };
  O.create_decoy = function (ctx, eff, tg) {
    const b = ctx.b, a = ctx.actor;
    const forU = eff.for === 'target' ? (alliesOf(ctx, tg)[0] || a) : a;
    for (let i = 0; i < (eff.count || 1); i++) {
      const u = K.makeUnit(b, { name: (eff.lookslike === 'dead_enemy' ? 'Lurching Corpse' : forU.name) + ' (decoy)', side: a.side, row: forU.row, kind: 'decoy', owner: a.id, attrs: { might: 1, swiftness: 1, spirit: 1, resilience: 1 }, maxHp: 1, duration: D(eff.duration, 2), tags: ['decoy'] });
      u.onDeath = eff.onDeath || null;
      u.flags.summoned = true; u.decoyOf = forU.id;
      EB.B.addUnit(b, u); u.t = Infinity;
    }
    forU.flags.redirectToDecoy = Math.max(forU.flags.redirectToDecoy || 0, 0.35);
    say(ctx, 'Decoys shimmer into being.', 'dim');
  };
  O.create_decoy_of = function (ctx, eff, tg) { O.create_decoy(ctx, { count: 1, duration: eff.duration, for: 'target' }, tg); };

  // ---------- misc / utility ----------
  O.change_mask = function (ctx, eff) {
    const b = ctx.b, a = ctx.actor;
    let to = eff.to || (eff.random ? b.rng.pick(['grace', 'shadow', 'war']) : null);
    if (!to) { const order = ['grace', 'shadow', 'war']; to = order[(order.indexOf(a.flags.mask || 'war') + 1) % 3]; }
    const persona = K.stacks(a, 'persona');
    if (a.flags.oath && a.flags.oath.restriction === 'never_change_mask') O._oathBreak(b, a, 'changed masks');
    if (persona && !eff.keepPersona) {
      // burst from the new mask
      if (to === 'war') for (const e of K.enemies(b, a).slice(0, 1)) K.damage(b, a, e, K.hit(a) * 0.15 * persona, 'physical', {});
      if (to === 'grace') K.heal(b, a, a, K.maxHp(a) * 0.03 * persona);
      if (to === 'shadow') K.applyStatus(b, a, a, 'evasive', Math.min(3, Math.ceil(persona / 3)));
      K.removeStatus(b, a, 'persona');
    }
    O._applyMask(b, a, to, 1);
    say(ctx, `${a.name} dons the mask of ${to}.`, 'sys');
  };
  O._applyMask = function (b, u, mask, scale) {
    u.mods = u.mods.filter((m) => m.tag !== 'mask');
    u.flags.mask = mask;
    const p = (1 + 0.05 * K.stacks(u, 'persona')) * (u.flags.maskDouble ? 2 : 1) * (scale || 1);
    const sets = { grace: { evasion: 0.1, healing_done: 0.15 }, shadow: { crit: 0.12, evasion: 0.06 }, war: { damage: 0.15, damage_reduction: 0.05 } };
    for (const k in sets[mask] || {}) K.addMod(b, u, k, sets[mask][k] * p, 'combat', u, { tag: 'mask' });
  };
  O.mask_bonus_double = function (ctx) { timed(ctx.actor, 'maskDouble', true, 3); if (ctx.actor.flags.mask) O._applyMask(ctx.b, ctx.actor, ctx.actor.flags.mask, 1); };
  O.set_phase = function (ctx, eff) {
    const a = ctx.actor; if (!a.statuses.moonphase) K.applyStatus(ctx.b, a, a, 'moonphase', 1);
    const map = { new: 3, full: 1 };
    const ph = eff.to === 'choose' ? (K.hpPct(a) < 0.4 ? 3 : 1) : map[eff.to] != null ? map[eff.to] : 1;
    a.statuses.moonphase.phase = ph; if (ph === 1) K.fire(ctx.b, a, 'phase_becomes_full', {});
  };
  O.advance_phase = function (ctx, eff) { const a = ctx.actor; if (!a.statuses.moonphase) K.applyStatus(ctx.b, a, a, 'moonphase', 1); const st = a.statuses.moonphase; st.phase = ((st.phase || 0) + (eff.steps || 1)) % 4; if (st.phase === 1) K.fire(ctx.b, a, 'phase_becomes_full', {}); };
  O.phase_double_effect = function (ctx) { const st = ctx.actor.statuses.moonphase; if (st && (st.phase === 1 || st.phase === 3)) addPower(ctx, 1); };
  O.flip_tide = function (ctx) { const a = ctx.actor; a.flags.tide = true; a.flags.tideHigh = !a.flags.tideHigh; say(ctx, `The tide turns ${a.flags.tideHigh ? 'high' : 'low'}.`, 'dim'); };
  O.random_item = function (ctx, eff) {
    const b = ctx.b, a = ctx.actor;
    const pool = eff.pool || 'gadgets';
    const r = b.rng.int(3);
    if (pool === 'food_and_herbs') { if (r === 0) K.heal(b, a, a, K.maxHp(a) * 0.15); else if (r === 1) K.restore(b, a, 'mana', Math.round(K.maxMana(a) * 0.2)); else X.cleanse(b, a, 'any', 1); }
    else if (pool === 'poisons_and_cures') { const e = K.enemies(b, a)[0]; if (r === 0 && e) K.applyStatus(b, a, e, 'poisoned', 3); else if (r === 1) X.cleanse(b, a, 'poison', 2); else if (e) K.applyStatus(b, a, e, 'drowsy', 2); }
    else { const e = b.rng.pick(K.enemies(b, a)); if (!e) return; if (r === 0) K.damage(b, a, e, K.hit(a) * 0.8, 'fire', {}); else if (r === 1) K.applyStatus(b, a, e, 'stunned', 1, { dur: 1 }); else K.applyStatus(b, a, e, 'webbed', 3); }
    say(ctx, `${a.name} rummages and finds something useful.`, 'dim');
  };
  O.fetch_item = function (ctx) { O.random_item(ctx, { pool: 'food_and_herbs' }); };
  O.use_item_free = function (ctx) { ctx.actor.flags.freeItem = true; };
  O.loot_bonus = function (ctx, eff) { ctx.b.lootBonus = (ctx.b.lootBonus || 0) + (eff.percent || 10) / 100; };
  O.bonus_loot_chance = function (ctx, eff) { ctx.b.lootBonus = (ctx.b.lootBonus || 0) + (eff.amount || 0.1); };
  O.skip_ambush = function (ctx) { ctx.b.flags.skipAmbush = true; (ctx.b.socialFlags = ctx.b.socialFlags || {}).skipAmbush = true; };
  O.hide_essences = function (ctx) { (ctx.b.socialFlags = ctx.b.socialFlags || {}).hideEssences = true; ctx.actor.flags.hiddenEssences = true; };
  O.disguise_essences = function (ctx) { (ctx.b.socialFlags = ctx.b.socialFlags || {}).disguiseEssences = true; ctx.actor.flags.hiddenEssences = true; };
  O.store_disguise = function (ctx) { (ctx.b.socialFlags = ctx.b.socialFlags || {}).storedDisguise = true; };
  O.hide_from = function (ctx) { (ctx.b.socialFlags = ctx.b.socialFlags || {}).hiddenFromDetection = true; };
  O.disable_lights = function (ctx) { for (const u of K.allies(ctx.b, ctx.actor, true)) K.addMod(ctx.b, u, 'evasion', 0.1, 'combat', ctx.actor); for (const e of K.enemies(ctx.b, ctx.actor)) K.addMod(ctx.b, e, 'accuracy', -0.1, 'combat', ctx.actor); say(ctx, 'The lights die.', 'dim'); };
  O.hide_health = function (ctx, eff) { timed(ctx.actor, 'hideHealth', true, D(eff.duration, 3)); };
  O.suppress_boons_noop = function () {};
  O.copy_last = O.copy_last_ability;
  O.all_allies_target = function (ctx, eff) { timed(ctx.actor, 'allAlliesTarget', true, D(eff.duration, 2)); };
  O.block_parry_noop = function () {};
})(globalThis.EB = globalThis.EB || {});

;
(function (EB) {
  'use strict';
  const K = EB.K, X = EB.X;
  const AI = (EB.AI = {});
  const B = () => EB.B;

  // Rough value of an ability's effects
  AI.profile = function (def) {
    const p = { dmg: 0, heal: 0, shield: 0, buff: 0, debuff: 0, summon: 0, control: 0, cleanse: 0, setup: 0 };
    K.mapEffects(def.effects || [], (e) => {
      switch (e.op) {
        case 'damage': p.dmg += (e.power || 0) * (e.hits || 1); break;
        case 'heal': p.heal += e.power || (e.percent ? e.percent / 50 : 0); break;
        case 'shield': p.shield += e.power || 0.3; break;
        case 'apply_status': if (K.isBoon(e.status)) p.buff += 0.15 * (e.stacks || 1); else p.debuff += 0.12 * Math.min(4, e.stacks || 1) + (['stunned', 'frozen', 'asleep', 'feared', 'silenced', 'confused'].includes(e.status) ? 0.5 : 0); break;
        case 'modify_stat': if (e.amount > 0 && !/taken|cost|fail/.test(e.stat)) p.buff += Math.min(0.6, e.amount * 2); else p.debuff += Math.min(0.6, Math.abs(e.amount) * 2); break;
        case 'summon': case 'create_decoy': p.summon += 1; break;
        case 'zone': case 'transform': case 'equip_conjured': p.setup += 1; break;
        case 'cleanse': p.cleanse += 0.5; break;
        case 'timeline': if (e.kind === 'delay') p.control += 0.3; else p.buff += 0.3; break;
        case 'execute': case 'execute_if_below': p.dmg += 0.6; break;
        case 'detonate_status': case 'consume_status': p.dmg += 0.4; break;
        case 'reaction': p.setup += 0.4; break;
        case 'banish': case 'control': case 'mind_control': case 'polymorph': p.control += 1; break;
        default: break;
      }
      return e;
    });
    return p;
  };

  AI.slots = (u) => u.abilities;

  AI.pickEnemyTarget = function (b, u, list, opts) {
    if (!list.length) return null;
    if (u.flags.focus) { const f = K.byId(b, u.flags.focus); if (f && list.includes(f)) return f; }
    let pool = list.filter((x) => !x.flags.untargetableByAI && x.id !== (opts && opts.avoid));
    if (!pool.length) pool = list;
    const nonDecoy = pool.filter((x) => x.kind !== 'decoy');
    if (nonDecoy.length) pool = nonDecoy;
    // weight: low health, player hero, marked/exposed
    const w = pool.map((x) => {
      let s = 1 + (1 - K.hpPct(x)) * 1.5;
      if (x.isPlayer) s += 0.35;
      if (K.hasStatus(x, 'exposed') || K.hasStatus(x, 'marked')) s += 0.3;
      if (x.kind === 'familiar') s += 0.2;
      return s * (0.7 + b.rng() * 0.6);
    });
    return pool[w.indexOf(Math.max(...w))];
  };

  AI.score = function (b, u, slot, opts) {
    const d = slot.def;
    const p = AI.profile(d);
    let s = 0, target = null;
    const enemies = K.enemies(b, u);
    const allies = K.allies(b, u, true);
