    const hostile = B().hostileTarget(d.target);
    if (B().needsTarget(d)) {
      const valid = B().validTargets(b, u, d);
      if (!valid.length) return null;
      if (d.target === 'enemy_single' || d.target === 'enemy_row') target = AI.pickEnemyTarget(b, u, valid, opts);
      else if (d.target === 'ally_or_enemy') target = p.dmg || p.debuff ? AI.pickEnemyTarget(b, u, valid.filter((x) => K.isEnemy(u, x)), opts) : X.lowest(valid.filter((x) => !K.isEnemy(u, x)));
      else target = X.lowest(valid);
    }
    const nT = d.target === 'enemy_all' ? enemies.length : d.target === 'enemy_row' && target ? K.rowOf(b, target.side, target.row).length : 1;
    s += p.dmg * Math.max(1, nT * 0.8);
    if (target && hostile && K.hpPct(target) < 0.3) s += p.dmg * 0.6 + (p.dmg > 0 ? 0.3 : 0);
    s += p.debuff * Math.max(1, nT * 0.7) * (target && target.boss ? 0.6 : 1);
    s += p.control * (target && target.boss ? 0.3 : 1);
    const hurt = allies.filter((x) => K.hpPct(x) < 0.6);
    const lowest = X.lowest(allies);
    if (p.heal) s += p.heal * (lowest ? (1 - K.hpPct(lowest)) * 4 : 0) - (hurt.length ? 0 : 0.5);
    if (p.shield) s += p.shield * (K.hpPct(u) < 0.7 ? 1.5 : 0.4);
    if (p.cleanse) s += allies.some((x) => K.afflictions(x).length) ? 0.8 : -0.3;
    // buffs: don't spam
    if (p.buff) s += p.buff * (slot.uses ? 0.5 : 1);
    if (p.summon) s += K.allies(b, u).filter((x) => x.owner === u.id).length >= 2 ? -0.5 : 1.0;
    if (p.setup) s += slot.uses ? 0.1 : 0.9;
    if (d.toggle) s = slot.on ? -9 : 1.0;
    if (d.type === 'familiar') s = -9;
    // costs: keep a reserve
    const c = B().cost(u, d);
    if (c.mana && u.mana - c.mana < K.maxMana(u) * 0.15) s -= 0.2;
    if (c.health) s -= 0.3;
    if (opts && opts.attackOnly && !p.dmg) return null;
    return { score: s + b.rng() * 0.25, target };
  };

  AI.choose = function (b, u, opts) {
    opts = opts || {};
    const enemies = K.enemies(b, u);
    if (!enemies.length) return { type: 'wait' };
    // familiar orders
    if (u.order && u.order.idx != null) {
      const slot = u.abilities[u.order.idx];
      const o = u.order; u.order = null;
      if (slot && !B().blocked(b, u, slot)) return { type: 'ability', slot: u.abilities.indexOf(slot), target: o.target, ordered: true };
    }
    if (u.kind === 'familiar' && u.stance === 'hold') return { type: 'defend' };
    let best = null;
    u.abilities.forEach((slot, i) => {
      if (B().blocked(b, u, slot)) return;
      const r = AI.score(b, u, slot, opts);
      if (!r) return;
      let sc = r.score;
      if (u.kind === 'familiar') {
        const p = AI.profile(slot.def);
        if (u.stance === 'support') sc += (p.heal + p.shield + p.buff + p.cleanse) * 1.5 - p.dmg * 0.5;
        if (u.stance === 'guard') sc += (p.shield + p.control) * 1.2;
        if (u.stance === 'aggressive') sc += p.dmg * 0.5;
      }
      if (!best || sc > best.score) best = { type: 'ability', slot: i, target: r.target && r.target.id, score: sc };
    });
    // weapon attack baseline
    const wdef = B().weaponDef(u);
    const wv = B().validTargets(b, u, wdef);
    const canWeapon = u.kind !== 'conjuration' && u.kind !== 'familiar' && wv.length;
    const wScore = 0.55 + b.rng() * 0.2;
    if (canWeapon && (!best || best.score < wScore)) {
      const t = AI.pickEnemyTarget(b, u, wv, opts);
      return { type: 'attack', target: t && t.id, score: wScore };
    }
    if (!best && !opts.attackOnly) {
      if (B().canMove(b, u) && u.row === 'rear' && u.kind !== 'familiar' && K.rowOf(b, u.side, 'vanguard').length === 0) return { type: 'swap' };
      return { type: 'defend' };
    }
    if (best && u.sta < K.maxSta(u) * 0.15 && best.score < 0.6 && !opts.attackOnly) return { type: 'defend' };
    return best;
  };

  AI.plan = function (b, u, opts) {
    if (!u.alive || u.isPlayer) return;
    const c = AI.choose(b, u, opts);
    if (!c) { u.intent = null; return; }
    const i = { type: c.type, target: c.target, slot: c.slot };
    if (c.type === 'ability') {
      const d = u.abilities[c.slot].def;
      i.name = d.name; i.tempo = d.tempo || 'standard';
      const p = AI.profile(d);
      i.kind = p.dmg ? (EB.B.hostileTarget(d.target) && d.target !== 'enemy_single' ? 'aoe' : 'attack') : p.heal || p.shield ? 'heal' : p.summon ? 'summon' : p.debuff || p.control ? 'debuff' : 'buff';
      i.desc = d.summary;
    } else if (c.type === 'attack') { i.name = 'Attack'; i.kind = 'attack'; i.tempo = 'standard'; }
    else if (c.type === 'defend') { i.name = 'Defend'; i.kind = 'defend'; i.tempo = 'quick'; }
    else { i.name = c.type === 'swap' ? 'Reposition' : 'Wait'; i.kind = 'move'; i.tempo = 'quick'; }
    u.intent = i;
  };

  // Execute this unit's turn (AI-controlled)
  AI.act = function (b, u) {
    let plan = u.intent;
    const valid = (p) => {
      if (!p) return false;
      if (p.type === 'ability') {
        const s = u.abilities[p.slot];
        if (!s || EB.B.blocked(b, u, s)) return false;
        if (EB.B.needsTarget(s.def)) { const t = K.byId(b, p.target); if (!t || !EB.B.validTargets(b, u, s.def).includes(t)) return false; }
        return true;
      }
      if (p.type === 'attack') { const t = K.byId(b, p.target); return t && EB.B.validTargets(b, u, EB.B.weaponDef(u)).includes(t); }
      return true;
    };
    // charmed units attack their own side
    if (u.flags.charmed) {
      const own = K.allies(b, u);
      if (own.length) {
        const t = b.rng.pick(own);
        K.say(b, `${u.name}, charmed, attacks ${t.name}!`, u.side === 'enemy' ? 'good' : 'bad');
        K.damage(b, u, t, K.hit(u, null, 'physical') * 0.8, 'physical', { melee: true });
        EB.B.finishAction(b, u, 'standard', { ab: { name: 'Charmed strike' } });
        return { charmed: true };
      }
    }
    if (u.order && u.order.idx != null) plan = AI.choose(b, u);
    if (!valid(plan)) plan = AI.choose(b, u);
    if (!plan) plan = { type: 'defend' };
    const r = EB.B.act(b, u, plan);
    if (r && r.error) return EB.B.act(b, u, { type: 'defend' });
    return r;
  };
})(globalThis.EB = globalThis.EB || {});
